Gen-Searcher: Reinforcing Agentic Search for Image Generation
Authors: Kaituo Feng, Manyuan Zhang, Shuang Chen, Yunlong Lin, Kaixuan Fan, Yilei Jiang, Hongyu Li, Dian Zheng, Chenyang Wang, Xiangyu Yue
First: 2026-03-30T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:59:56+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://gen-searcher.vercel.app Code: https://github.com/tulerfeng/Gen-Searcher
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent image generation models have shown strong capabilities in generating high-fidelity and photorealistic images. However, they are fundamentally constrained by frozen internal knowledge, thus often failing on real-world scenarios that are knowledge-intensive or require up-to-date information. In this paper, we present Gen-Searcher, as the first attempt to train a search-augmented image generation agent, which performs multi-hop reasoning and search to collect the textual knowledge and reference images needed for grounded generation. To achieve this, we construct a tailored data pipeline and curate two high-quality datasets, Gen-Searcher-SFT-10k and Gen-Searcher-RL-6k, containing diverse search-intensive prompts and corresponding ground-truth synthesis images. We further introduce KnowGen, a comprehensive benchmark that explicitly requires search-grounded external knowledge for image generation and evaluates models from multiple dimensions. Based on these resources, we train Gen-Searcher with SFT followed by agentic reinforcement learning with dual reward feedback, which combines text-based and image-based rewards to provide more stable and informative learning signals for GRPO training. Experiments show that Gen-Searcher brings substantial gains, improving Qwen-Image by around 16 points on KnowGen and 15 points on WISE. We hope this work can serve as an open foundation for search agents in image generation, and we fully open-source our data, models, and code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Gen-Searcher：强化代理搜索以实现图像生成
近期图像生成模型在生成高保真、逼真图像方面表现出强大能力，但受限于内部知识冻结，常在需要知识密集或最新信息的真实场景中失败。本文提出Gen-Searcher，首次尝试训练一个增强搜索的图像生成代理，通过多跳推理与搜索收集生成所需的文本知识和参考图像。为此，我们构建定制数据管道，整理两个高质量数据集Gen-Searcher-SFT-10k和Gen-Searcher-RL-6k，包含多样化的搜索密集提示及其对应真实合成图像。我们进一步提出KnowGen基准，明确要求图像生成需依赖搜索获取的外部知识，并从多维度评估模型。基于这些资源，我们采用SFT训练Gen-Searcher，随后结合双奖励反馈的代理强化学习（GRPO），融合文本与图像奖励以提供更稳定、信息丰富的学习信号。实验表明，Gen-Searcher显著提升性能，在KnowGen上提升Qwen-Image约16分，在WISE上提升15分。我们希望本工作能成为图像生成领域搜索代理的开放基础，并完全开源数据、模型与代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of image generation models in handling knowledge-intensive or up-to-date real-world tasks due to frozen internal knowledge, this paper introduces Gen-Searcher, the first search-augmented image generation agent that performs multi-hop reasoning and external search to gather textual knowledge and reference images. The method involves constructing two curated datasets (Gen-Searcher-SFT-10k and Gen-Searcher-RL-6k) and training the agent via supervised fine-tuning followed by agentic reinforcement learning with dual reward signals combining text and image feedback. Experimental results demonstrate that Gen-Searcher significantly improves upon Qwen-Image, achieving 16-point gains on the KnowGen benchmark and 15-point gains on WISE, showcasing its effectiveness in search-grounded image generation.
为解决当前图像生成模型因内部知识冻结而难以应对现实世界知识密集型任务的问题，本文提出Gen-Searcher，首个支持多跳推理与外部搜索的图像生成代理。该方法通过定制数据管道构建两个高质量数据集，并采用结合文本与图像奖励的强化学习策略进行训练。实验表明，Gen-Searcher在KnowGen和WISE上分别使Qwen-Image提升16分和15分，验证了其在搜索引导图像生成中的有效性。
HandX: Scaling Bimanual Motion and Interaction Generation
Authors: Zimu Zhang, Yucheng Zhang, Xiyan Xu, Ziyin Wang, Sirui Xu, Kai Zhou, Bing Zhou, Chuan Guo, Jian Wang, Yu-Xiong Wang, Liang-Yan Gui
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-30T17:59:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:59:49+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026. Project Page: https://handx-project.github.io. Code: https://github.com/handx-project/HandX
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Synthesizing human motion has advanced rapidly, yet realistic hand motion and bimanual interaction remain underexplored. Whole-body models often miss the fine-grained cues that drive dexterous behavior, finger articulation, contact timing, and inter-hand coordination, and existing resources lack high-fidelity bimanual sequences that capture nuanced finger dynamics and collaboration. To fill this gap, we present HandX, a unified foundation spanning data, annotation, and evaluation. We consolidate and filter existing datasets for quality, and collect a new motion-capture dataset targeting underrepresented bimanual interactions with detailed finger dynamics. For scalable annotation, we introduce a decoupled strategy that extracts representative motion features, e.g., contact events and finger flexion, and then leverages reasoning from large language models to produce fine-grained, semantically rich descriptions aligned with these features. Building on the resulting data and annotations, we benchmark diffusion and autoregressive models with versatile conditioning modes. Experiments demonstrate high-quality dexterous motion generation, supported by our newly proposed hand-focused metrics. We further observe clear scaling trends: larger models trained on larger, higher-quality datasets produce more semantically coherent bimanual motion. Our dataset is released to support future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HandX：扩展双手运动与交互生成
人体运动合成技术发展迅速，但真实手部运动和双手协同交互仍处于探索阶段。全身模型常忽略驱动灵巧行为的细粒度线索，如指关节运动、接触时机及双手协调，现有资源缺乏高保真双手交互序列，难以捕捉精细指部动态与协作细节。为填补这一空白，我们提出HandX，一个涵盖数据、标注与评估的统一基础框架。我们整合并筛选现有数据集以保证质量，并采集了针对代表性双手交互、包含详细指部动态的新运动捕捉数据集。为实现可扩展标注，我们引入解耦策略：提取代表性运动特征（如接触事件、指部屈伸），并借助大语言模型推理生成与这些特征对齐的细粒度、语义丰富的描述。基于所得数据与标注，我们对扩散模型和自回归模型在多种条件模式下进行基准测试。实验表明，我们的方法可生成高质量灵巧运动，且新提出的以手为中心的评估指标支持这一结论。我们还观察到明显的规模效应：在更大规模、更高保真度数据集上训练的更大模型，能生成语义更连贯的双手运动。本数据集已公开，以支持未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of high-fidelity bimanual hand motion data and fine-grained annotations, HandX introduces a unified framework combining curated datasets, scalable annotation via large language models, and evaluation metrics tailored to hand motion. The method decouples motion features like contact timing and finger flexion, then uses LLMs to generate rich semantic descriptions, enabling training of diffusion and autoregressive models. Experiments show that larger models trained on higher-quality, expanded datasets produce more semantically coherent and dexterous bimanual motion, validated by new hand-focused metrics.
为解决现有数据缺乏精细手部动作与双臂协同交互的问题，HandX构建了一个统一框架，整合高质量数据集、基于大语言模型的可扩展标注方法及针对性评估指标。该方法通过解耦接触事件与指关节弯曲等运动特征，利用LLM生成语义丰富的描述，从而训练扩散与自回归模型。实验表明，基于更大规模、更高保真度数据集训练的更大模型能生成更语义连贯、更灵巧的双臂运动，新提出的专注手部的评估指标验证了这一趋势。
ViPRA: Video Prediction for Robot Actions
Authors: Sandeep Routray, Hengkai Pan, Unnat Jain, Shikhar Bahl, Deepak Pathak
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-11-11T01:33:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:59:36+00:00
Comments: In ICLR 2026. Website: https://vipra-project.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Can we turn a video prediction model into a robot policy? Videos, including those of humans or teleoperated robots, capture rich physical interactions. However, most of them lack labeled actions, which limits their use in robot learning. We present Video Prediction for Robot Actions (ViPRA), a simple pretraining-finetuning framework that learns continuous robot control from these actionless videos. Instead of directly predicting actions, we train a video-language model to predict both future visual observations and motion-centric latent actions, which serve as intermediate representations of scene dynamics. We train these latent actions using perceptual losses and optical flow consistency to ensure they reflect physically grounded behavior. For downstream control, we introduce a chunked flow matching decoder that maps latent actions to robot-specific continuous action sequences, using only 100 to 200 teleoperated demonstrations. This approach avoids expensive action annotation, supports generalization across embodiments, and enables smooth, high-frequency continuous control upto 22 Hz via chunked action decoding. Unlike prior latent action works that treat pretraining as autoregressive policy learning, ViPRA explicitly models both what changes and how. Our method outperforms strong baselines, with a 16% gain on the SIMPLER benchmark and a 13% improvement across real world manipulation tasks. We have released models and code at https://vipra-project.github.io
中文标题/摘要
标题：ViPRA：用于机器人动作的视频预测
我们能否将视频预测模型转化为机器人策略？视频（包括人类或遥操作机器人拍摄的视频）捕捉了丰富的物理交互信息。然而，大多数视频缺乏标注的动作，这限制了它们在机器人学习中的应用。我们提出了视频预测用于机器人动作（ViPRA），这是一种简单的预训练-微调框架，可从这些无动作视频中学习连续的机器人控制。与直接预测动作不同，我们训练一个视频-语言模型，同时预测未来的视觉观测和以运动为中心的潜在动作，这些潜在动作作为场景动态的中间表示。我们通过感知损失和光流一致性训练这些潜在动作，以确保其反映物理上合理的运动。在下游控制中，我们引入了一种分块流匹配解码器，仅使用100至200个遥操作演示，即可将潜在动作映射为特定于机器人的连续动作序列。该方法避免了昂贵的动作标注，支持跨机器人形态的泛化，并通过分块动作解码实现高达22Hz的平滑高频连续控制。与以往将预训练视为自回归策略学习的潜在动作工作不同，ViPRA明确建模了“什么变化”和“如何变化”。我们的方法优于现有强基线，在SIMPLER基准上提升16%，在真实世界操作任务中整体提升13%。我们已在https://vipra-project.github.io发布了模型和代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of labeled robot actions in video data, ViPRA proposes a pretraining-finetuning framework that learns continuous robot control from unlabeled videos by predicting both future visual observations and motion-centric latent actions. These latent actions are trained using perceptual losses and optical flow consistency to ensure physical plausibility, then decoded into robot-specific action sequences via a chunked flow matching decoder using only 100–200 teleoperated demonstrations. Experiments show ViPRA outperforms strong baselines by 16% on SIMPLER and 13% across real-world manipulation tasks, enabling smooth, high-frequency control up to 22 Hz without requiring action annotations or embodiment-specific training.
为了解决视频数据中缺乏标注动作的问题，ViPRA提出一种预训练-微调框架，通过预测未来视觉观测和运动中心的潜在动作，从无标签视频中学习连续机器人控制。这些潜在动作通过感知损失和光流一致性训练以确保物理合理性，再通过分块流匹配解码器映射为机器人特定的动作序列，仅需100–200个遥操作演示。实验表明，ViPRA在SIMPLER基准上比强基线提升16%，在真实世界操作任务中平均提升13%，支持高达22Hz的平滑高频控制，无需动作标注或特定机器人训练。
Adaptive Block-Scaled Data Types
Authors: Jack Cook, Hyemin S. Lee, Kathryn Le, Junxian Guo, Giovanni Traverso, Anantha P. Chandrakasan, Song Han
First: 2026-03-30T17:59:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:59:33+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
NVFP4 has grown increasingly popular as a 4-bit format for quantizing large language models due to its hardware support and its ability to retain useful information with relatively few bits per parameter. However, the format is not without limitations: recent work has shown that NVFP4 suffers from its error distribution, resulting in large amounts of quantization error on near-maximal values in each group of 16 values. In this work, we leverage this insight to design new Adaptive Block-Scaled Data Types that can adapt to the distribution of their input values. For four-bit quantization, our proposed IF4 (Int/Float 4) data type selects between FP4 and INT4 representations for each group of 16 values, which are then scaled by an E4M3 scale factor as is done with NVFP4. The selected data type is denoted using the scale factor's sign bit, which is currently unused in NVFP4, and we apply the same insight to design formats for other bit-widths, including IF3 and IF6. When used to quantize language models, we find that IF4 outperforms existing 4-bit block-scaled formats, achieving lower loss during quantized training and achieving higher accuracy on many tasks in post-training quantization. We additionally design and evaluate an IF4 Multiply-Accumulate (MAC) unit to demonstrate that IF4 can be implemented efficiently in next-generation hardware accelerators. Our code is available at https://github.com/mit-han-lab/fouroversix.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应块缩放数据类型
由于其硬件支持和在每参数仅用少量比特即可保留有用信息的能力，NVFP4作为4位量化格式在大型语言模型中日益流行。然而，该格式存在局限性：近期研究表明，NVFP4因误差分布问题，在每组16个值中的近最大值处产生大量量化误差。本研究利用这一洞察，设计了新的自适应块缩放数据类型，可根据输入值分布进行自适应调整。对于4位量化，我们提出的IF4（Int/Float 4）数据类型为每组16个值选择FP4或INT4表示，再按NVFP4方式使用E4M3缩放因子进行缩放。所选数据类型通过缩放因子的符号位表示（该位在NVFP4中当前未使用），并应用相同思路设计了其他位宽格式，如IF3和IF6。在量化语言模型时，我们发现IF4优于现有4位块缩放格式，在量化训练中损失更低，在后训练量化中多项任务上精度更高。此外，我们设计并评估了IF4乘累加（MAC）单元，证明其可在下一代硬件加速器中高效实现。代码见https://github.com/mit-han-lab/fouroversix。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of NVFP4 in handling quantization errors on near-maximal values, this work introduces Adaptive Block-Scaled Data Types that dynamically select between FP4 and INT4 representations per 16-value block for four-bit quantization, using an unused sign bit in the scale factor to denote the chosen format. The proposed IF4 format, along with variants IF3 and IF6, adapts to input value distributions to reduce quantization error. Experimental results show IF4 achieves lower loss during quantized training and higher accuracy on multiple downstream tasks compared to existing 4-bit block-scaled formats, and a dedicated IF4 MAC unit demonstrates efficient hardware implementation.
鉴于NVFP4在近最大值处量化误差较大的问题，本文提出自适应块缩放数据类型，每16个值块动态选择FP4或INT4表示，再通过E4M3缩放因子统一处理。选择结果通过缩放因子的符号位（原未使用）编码，形成IF4、IF3、IF6等格式。实验表明，IF4在量化训练损失和多项任务后训练精度上均优于现有4位格式，且设计的IF4乘累加单元证明其可在下一代硬件中高效实现。
Geometry-aware similarity metrics for neural representations on Riemannian and statistical manifolds
Authors: N Alex Cayco Gajic, Arthur Pellegrino
First: 2026-03-30T17:59:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:59:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Similarity measures are widely used to interpret the representational geometries used by neural networks to solve tasks. Yet, because existing methods compare the extrinsic geometry of representations in state space, rather than their intrinsic geometry, they may fail to capture subtle yet crucial distinctions between fundamentally different neural network solutions. Here, we introduce metric similarity analysis (MSA), a novel method which leverages tools from Riemannian geometry to compare the intrinsic geometry of neural representations under the manifold hypothesis. We show that MSA can be used to i) disentangle features of neural computations in deep networks with different learning regimes, ii) compare nonlinear dynamics, and iii) investigate diffusion models. Hence, we introduce a mathematically grounded and broadly applicable framework to understand the mechanisms behind neural computations by comparing their intrinsic geometries.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向黎曼与统计流形的神经表示几何感知相似度度量
相似度度量常用于解释神经网络为解决任务所采用的表示几何结构。然而，现有方法比较的是状态空间中表示的外在几何，而非其内在几何，可能无法捕捉本质不同神经网络解决方案间微妙而关键的区别。本文引入度量相似性分析（MSA），一种利用黎曼几何工具在流形假设下比较神经表示内在几何的新方法。我们表明，MSA可用于：i）分离不同学习范式深度网络中神经计算的特征；ii）比较非线性动力学；iii）研究扩散模型。因此，我们提出了一种数学基础扎实且广泛适用的框架，通过比较神经计算的内在几何来理解其机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing similarity measures that rely on extrinsic geometry and overlook intrinsic manifold structures, the authors propose Metric Similarity Analysis (MSA), a method grounded in Riemannian geometry to compare the intrinsic geometry of neural representations. Applied to deep networks under different learning regimes, nonlinear dynamics, and diffusion models, MSA successfully disentangles distinct computational features, reveals differences in representation geometry, and provides a mathematically rigorous framework for understanding neural computation mechanisms through intrinsic geometric comparison.
鉴于现有相似性度量方法依赖于外在几何而忽略内在流形结构的局限性，作者提出了一种基于黎曼几何的度量相似性分析（MSA）方法，用于比较神经表示的内在几何。该方法应用于不同学习范式下的深度网络、非线性动力学及扩散模型，成功区分了不同的计算特征，揭示了表示几何差异，并提供了一个通过内在几何比较理解神经计算机制的严谨数学框架。
PoseDreamer: Scalable and Photorealistic Human Data Generation Pipeline with Diffusion Models
Authors: Lorenza Prospero, Orest Kupyn, Ostap Viniavskyi, João F. Henriques, Christian Rupprecht
First: 2026-03-30T17:59:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:59:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Acquiring labeled datasets for 3D human mesh estimation is challenging due to depth ambiguities and the inherent difficulty of annotating 3D geometry from monocular images. Existing datasets are either real, with manually annotated 3D geometry and limited scale, or synthetic, rendered from 3D engines that provide precise labels but suffer from limited photorealism, low diversity, and high production costs. In this work, we explore a third path: generated data. We introduce PoseDreamer, a novel pipeline that leverages diffusion models to generate large-scale synthetic datasets with 3D mesh annotations. Our approach combines controllable image generation with Direct Preference Optimization for control alignment, curriculum-based hard sample mining, and multi-stage quality filtering. Together, these components naturally maintain correspondence between 3D labels and generated images, while prioritizing challenging samples to maximize dataset utility. Using PoseDreamer, we generate more than 500,000 high-quality synthetic samples, achieving a 76% improvement in image-quality metrics compared to rendering-based datasets. Models trained on PoseDreamer achieve performance comparable to or superior to those trained on real-world and traditional synthetic datasets. In addition, combining PoseDreamer with synthetic datasets results in better performance than combining real-world and synthetic datasets, demonstrating the complementary nature of our dataset. We will release the full dataset and generation code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PoseDreamer：基于扩散模型的可扩展且逼真的人体数据生成管道
由于深度歧义和从单目图像标注3D几何的固有难度，获取用于3D人体网格估计的标注数据集具有挑战性。现有数据集要么是真实数据，具有手动标注的3D几何但规模有限；要么是合成数据，由3D引擎渲染，提供精确标签但存在逼真度低、多样性差和制作成本高的问题。本文探索第三条路径：生成数据。我们提出PoseDreamer，一种新颖的管道，利用扩散模型生成大规模带3D网格标注的合成数据集。我们的方法结合可控图像生成与直接偏好优化以实现控制对齐，基于课程学习的困难样本挖掘，以及多阶段质量过滤。这些组件共同自然地保持3D标签与生成图像之间的对应关系，同时优先处理困难样本以最大化数据集效用。使用PoseDreamer，我们生成超过50万个高质量合成样本，其图像质量指标相比渲染型数据集提升76%。在PoseDreamer上训练的模型性能与真实世界和传统合成数据集相当或更优。此外，将PoseDreamer与合成数据集结合可获得比真实世界与合成数据集结合更好的性能，证明了我们数据集的互补性。我们将发布完整数据集和生成代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of large-scale, photorealistic, and accurately labeled 3D human datasets, the authors propose PoseDreamer, a diffusion-based pipeline that generates synthetic 3D human mesh data. The method integrates controllable image generation, Direct Preference Optimization for alignment, curriculum-based hard sample mining, and multi-stage quality filtering to ensure 3D label-image correspondence and prioritize challenging samples. Experimental results show that PoseDreamer produces over 500,000 high-quality samples with 76% higher image-quality metrics than rendering-based datasets, and models trained on it match or exceed performance on real and traditional synthetic datasets. Combining PoseDreamer with synthetic data outperforms combining real and synthetic data, highlighting its complementary value.
为解决大规模、高保真且标注精确的3D人体数据稀缺问题，作者提出PoseDreamer，一种基于扩散模型的合成数据生成管道。该方法结合可控图像生成、直接偏好优化对齐、基于课程的困难样本挖掘及多阶段质量过滤，确保3D标签与生成图像的一致性并优先处理难例。实验表明，PoseDreamer生成超50万高质量样本，图像质量指标比渲染数据提升76%，训练模型性能与真实数据或传统合成数据相当甚至更优。将PoseDreamer与合成数据结合，性能优于真实数据与合成数据的组合，凸显其互补优势。
On-the-fly Repulsion in the Contextual Space for Rich Diversity in Diffusion Transformers
Authors: Omer Dahary, Benaya Koren, Daniel Garibi, Daniel Cohen-Or
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-03-30T17:59:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:59:13+00:00
Comments: Conditionally accepted to SIGGRAPH 2026. Project page: https://contextual-repulsion.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Modern Text-to-Image (T2I) diffusion models have achieved remarkable semantic alignment, yet they often suffer from a significant lack of variety, converging on a narrow set of visual solutions for any given prompt. This typicality bias presents a challenge for creative applications that require a wide range of generative outcomes. We identify a fundamental trade-off in current approaches to diversity: modifying model inputs requires costly optimization to incorporate feedback from the generative path. In contrast, acting on spatially-committed intermediate latents tends to disrupt the forming visual structure, leading to artifacts. In this work, we propose to apply repulsion in the Contextual Space as a novel framework for achieving rich diversity in Diffusion Transformers. By intervening in the multimodal attention channels, we apply on-the-fly repulsion during the transformer's forward pass, injecting the intervention between blocks where text conditioning is enriched with emergent image structure. This allows for redirecting the guidance trajectory after it is structurally informed but before the composition is fixed. Our results demonstrate that repulsion in the Contextual Space produces significantly richer diversity without sacrificing visual fidelity or semantic adherence. Furthermore, our method is uniquely efficient, imposing a small computational overhead while remaining effective even in modern "Turbo" and distilled models where traditional trajectory-based interventions typically fail.
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of visual diversity in modern Text-to-Image diffusion models, which often converge on similar outputs despite semantic alignment, this work introduces on-the-fly repulsion in the Contextual Space within Diffusion Transformers. The method intervenes in multimodal attention channels during the forward pass, applying repulsive forces between latent representations after text conditioning has been enriched with emerging image structure but before final composition is fixed. Experimental results show that this approach significantly enhances output diversity without compromising visual fidelity or semantic accuracy, and it remains computationally efficient even in lightweight or distilled models where traditional trajectory-based methods fail.
为解决现代文本到图像扩散模型因语义对齐而缺乏视觉多样性的问题，该研究在扩散Transformer中引入了上下文空间中的实时排斥机制。该方法在前向传播过程中干预多模态注意力通道，在文本条件增强图像结构但最终组合尚未固定时施加排斥力。实验表明，该方法显著提升了生成结果的多样性，同时不牺牲视觉保真度或语义准确性，并在轻量级或蒸馏模型中保持高效，传统轨迹干预方法在此类模型中通常失效。
SHOW3D: Capturing Scenes of 3D Hands and Objects in the Wild
Authors: Patrick Rim, Kevin Harris, Braden Copple, Shangchen Han, Xu Xie, Ivan Shugurov, Sizhe An, He Wen, Alex Wong, Tomas Hodan, Kun He
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-30T17:58:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:58:27+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate 3D understanding of human hands and objects during manipulation remains a significant challenge for egocentric computer vision. Existing hand-object interaction datasets are predominantly captured in controlled studio settings, which limits both environmental diversity and the ability of models trained on such data to generalize to real-world scenarios. To address this challenge, we introduce a novel marker-less multi-camera system that allows for nearly unconstrained mobility in genuinely in-the-wild conditions, while still having the ability to generate precise 3D annotations of hands and objects. The capture system consists of a lightweight, back-mounted, multi-camera rig that is synchronized and calibrated with a user-worn VR headset. For 3D ground-truth annotation of hands and objects, we develop an ego-exo tracking pipeline and rigorously evaluate its quality. Finally, we present SHOW3D, the first large-scale dataset with 3D annotations that show hands interacting with objects in diverse real-world environments, including outdoor settings. Our approach significantly reduces the fundamental trade-off between environmental realism and accuracy of 3D annotations, which we validate with experiments on several downstream tasks. show3d-dataset.github.io
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for realistic 3D hand-object interaction data for egocentric vision, the authors introduce SHOW3D, a large-scale dataset captured in real-world, unconstrained environments. They employ a lightweight, back-mounted multi-camera rig synchronized with a VR headset to enable markerless 3D tracking of hands and objects without studio constraints. Their ego-exo tracking pipeline ensures precise 3D annotations, overcoming the traditional trade-off between environmental realism and annotation accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate that SHOW3D significantly improves generalization across downstream tasks compared to studio-based datasets, validating its utility for training and evaluating robust manipulation models.
为解决第一人称视觉中手与物体交互的3D理解难题，该研究提出SHOW3D数据集，首次在真实野外环境中采集手与物体的3D标注数据。方法采用轻量级背挂式多相机系统，与VR头显同步校准，实现无标记、高精度3D标注与自由移动。实验表明，该方案有效弥合了环境真实性和标注精度之间的矛盾，显著提升下游任务性能。
FlowIt: Global Matching for Optical Flow with Confidence-Guided Refinement
Authors: Sadra Safadoust, Fabio Tosi, Matteo Poggi, Fatma Güney
First: 2026-03-30T17:58:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:58:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present FlowIt, a novel architecture for optical flow estimation designed to robustly handle large pixel displacements. At its core, FlowIt leverages a hierarchical transformer architecture that captures extensive global context, enabling the model to effectively model long-range correspondences. To overcome the limitations of localized matching, we formulate the flow initialization as an optimal transport problem. This formulation yields a highly robust initial flow field, alongside explicitly derived occlusion and confidence maps. These cues are then seamlessly integrated into a guided refinement stage, where the network actively propagates reliable motion estimates from high-confidence regions into ambiguous, low-confidence areas. Extensive experiments across the Sintel, KITTI, Spring, and LayeredFlow datasets validate the efficacy of our approach. FlowIt achieves state-of-the-art results on the competitive Sintel and KITTI benchmarks, while simultaneously establishing new state-of-the-art cross-dataset zero-shot generalization performance on Sintel, Spring, and LayeredFlow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlowIt：基于置信度引导优化的全局匹配光流估计
我们提出了FlowIt，一种专为稳健处理大像素位移而设计的新型光流估计架构。其核心采用分层Transformer架构，捕捉广泛全局上下文，有效建模长距离对应关系。为克服局部匹配的局限性，我们将光流初始化建模为最优传输问题，从而获得高度鲁棒的初始光流场，并显式推导出遮挡图与置信度图。这些线索随后无缝融入引导优化阶段，网络主动将高置信度区域的可靠运动估计传播至低置信度的模糊区域。在Sintel、KITTI、Spring和LayeredFlow数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性。FlowIt在竞争性Sintel和KITTI基准上达到最先进性能，同时在Sintel、Spring和LayeredFlow数据集上实现了跨数据集零样本泛化性能的新纪录。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately estimating optical flow under large displacements and occlusions, FlowIt introduces a hierarchical transformer architecture that captures global context to model long-range correspondences. It initializes flow via optimal transport to generate a robust field and derive occlusion and confidence maps, which guide a refinement stage that propagates reliable motion estimates from high-confidence to low-confidence regions. Experiments across Sintel, KITTI, Spring, and LayeredFlow show FlowIt achieves state-of-the-art performance on Sintel and KITTI benchmarks and sets new zero-shot generalization records across multiple datasets.
为应对大像素位移下的光流估计难题，FlowIt提出一种分层Transformer架构，通过捕捉全局上下文建模长距离对应关系。其通过最优传输初始化光流，生成鲁棒初始场并推导遮挡与置信度图，再引导网络将高置信区域的可靠运动估计传播至低置信区域。在Sintel、KITTI、Spring和LayeredFlow数据集上的实验表明，FlowIt在Sintel和KITTI基准上达到最先进性能，并在Sintel、Spring和LayeredFlow上创下新的零样本迁移性能纪录。
SonoWorld: From One Image to a 3D Audio-Visual Scene
Authors: Derong Jin, Xiyi Chen, Ming C. Lin, Ruohan Gao
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-30T17:57:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:57:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026, project page: https://humathe.github.io/sonoworld/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Tremendous progress in visual scene generation now turns a single image into an explorable 3D world, yet immersion remains incomplete without sound. We introduce Image2AVScene, the task of generating a 3D audio-visual scene from a single image, and present SonoWorld, the first framework to tackle this challenge. From one image, our pipeline outpaints a 360° panorama, lifts it into a navigable 3D scene, places language-guided sound anchors, and renders ambisonics for point, areal, and ambient sources, yielding spatial audio aligned with scene geometry and semantics. Quantitative evaluations on a newly curated real-world dataset and a controlled user study confirm the effectiveness of our approach. Beyond free-viewpoint audio-visual rendering, we also demonstrate applications to one-shot acoustic learning and audio-visual spatial source separation. Project website: https://humathe.github.io/sonoworld/

Motivated by the need to enhance immersion in 3D visual scenes with spatial audio, the authors introduce SonoWorld, the first framework to generate a 3D audio-visual scene from a single image. Their method outpaints a 360° panorama, lifts it into a navigable 3D scene, places language-guided sound anchors, and renders ambisonics for point, areal, and ambient sources, ensuring spatial audio aligns with scene geometry and semantics. Experimental results on a real-world dataset and user study validate the framework’s effectiveness, while additional applications in one-shot acoustic learning and audio-visual source separation demonstrate its versatility.
为提升3D视觉场景的沉浸感，作者提出了SonoWorld框架，首次实现从单张图像生成3D音视频场景。该方法通过扩展全景图、构建可导航3D场景、放置语言引导的声音锚点，并渲染点声源、面声源和环境声的球形声场，确保音频空间分布与场景几何和语义一致。在真实世界数据集和用户研究中验证了其有效性，同时展示了其在单次声学学习和音视频空间声源分离中的应用潜力。
APPLE: Attribute-Preserving Pseudo-Labeling for Diffusion-Based Face Swapping
Authors: Jiwon Kang, Yeji Choi, JoungBin Lee, Wooseok Jang, Jinhyeok Choi, Taekeun Kang, Yongjae Park, Myungin Kim, Seungryong Kim
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-01-21T18:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:57:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CVPR 2026. Project Page: https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/APPLE/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Face swapping aims to transfer the identity of a source face onto a target face while preserving target-specific attributes such as pose, expression, lighting, skin tone, and makeup. However, since real ground truth for face swapping is unavailable, achieving both accurate identity transfer and high-quality attribute preservation remains challenging. Recent diffusion-based approaches attempt to improve visual fidelity through conditional inpainting on masked target images, but the masked condition removes crucial appearance cues, resulting in plausible yet misaligned attributes. To address this limitation, we propose APPLE (Attribute-Preserving Pseudo-Labeling), a fully diffusion-based teacher-student framework for attribute-preserving face swapping. Our approach introduces a teacher design to produce pseudo-labels aligned with the target attributes through (1) a conditional deblurring formulation that improves the preservation of global attributes such as skin tone and illumination, and (2) an attribute-aware inversion scheme that further enhances fine-grained attribute preservation such as makeup. APPLE conditions the student on clean pseudo-labels rather than degraded masked inputs, enabling more faithful attribute preservation. As a result, APPLE achieves state-of-the-art performance in attribute preservation while maintaining competitive identity transferability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：APPLE：基于扩散模型的属性保持人脸交换伪标签方法
人脸交换旨在将源人脸的身份转移到目标人脸，同时保留目标人脸的特定属性（如姿态、表情、光照、肤色和妆容）。然而，由于人脸交换的真实标注不可得，实现准确的身份转移与高质量的属性保持仍具挑战。近期基于扩散的方案通过在遮罩目标图像上进行条件修复以提升视觉保真度，但遮罩条件会移除关键外观线索，导致生成结果虽合理却错位。为解决此问题，我们提出APPLE（属性保持伪标签），一种完全基于扩散的教师-学生框架，用于属性保持的人脸交换。我们的方法引入教师设计，通过（1）条件去模糊公式提升肤色和光照等全局属性的保持，（2）属性感知的反演方案进一步增强妆容等细粒度属性的保持。APPLE对学生使用干净伪标签而非退化遮罩输入进行条件引导，从而实现更忠实的属性保留。结果表明，APPLE在属性保持方面达到最先进水平，同时保持了竞争力的身份转移能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of preserving target-specific attributes like pose, lighting, and makeup during face swapping without ground truth, the authors propose APPLE, a diffusion-based teacher-student framework. The teacher generates pseudo-labels aligned with target attributes via conditional deblurring for global features and attribute-aware inversion for fine-grained details, which the student then uses for synthesis. Experiments show APPLE outperforms prior diffusion methods in attribute preservation while maintaining competitive identity transfer accuracy, demonstrating its effectiveness in balancing fidelity and realism.
为了解决在缺乏真实标注的情况下进行人脸交换时难以同时保持目标属性（如姿态、光照、妆容）与身份转移的问题，作者提出了APPLE框架，采用扩散模型的教师-学生架构。教师通过条件去模糊和属性感知反演生成与目标属性对齐的伪标签，学生则基于这些干净伪标签进行合成。实验表明，APPLE在属性保持方面优于现有扩散方法，同时保持了良好的身份转移能力，有效平衡了真实感与属性一致性。
Temporal Credit Is Free
Authors: Aur Shalev Merin
First: 2026-03-30T17:54:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:54:55+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recurrent networks do not need Jacobian propagation to adapt online. The hidden state already carries temporal credit through the forward pass; immediate derivatives suffice if you stop corrupting them with stale trace memory and normalize gradient scales across parameter groups. An architectural rule predicts when normalization is needed: \b{eta}2 is required when gradients must pass through a nonlinear state update with no output bypass, and unnecessary otherwise. Across ten architectures, real primate neural data, and streaming ML benchmarks, immediate derivatives with RMSprop match or exceed full RTRL, scaling to n = 1024 at 1000x less memory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：时间信用是免费的
循环网络无需雅可比传播即可在线适应。隐藏状态已在前向传播中携带时间信用；只要停止用过时的迹记忆污染它们，并在参数组间归一化梯度尺度，即时导数就足够。一个架构规则可预测何时需要归一化：当梯度必须通过无输出旁路的非线性状态更新时，需使用\b{eta}2；否则无需。在十种架构、真实灵长类神经数据及流式机器学习基准测试中，使用RMSprop的即时导数表现与完整RTRL相当，且在n=1024时内存消耗仅为后者的1/1000。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of full RTRL for online learning in recurrent networks, this work argues that temporal credit can be captured via immediate derivatives without Jacobian propagation, provided gradients are normalized across parameter groups and stale trace memory is avoided. The method introduces an architectural rule: RMSprop (\b{eta}2) is necessary only when gradients must pass through a nonlinear state update without output bypass. Experiments across ten architectures, primate neural data, and streaming ML benchmarks show that immediate derivatives with RMSprop achieve performance matching or exceeding full RTRL, while scaling to n = 1024 with 1000x less memory.
为解决递归网络在线学习中全RTRL方法效率低下的问题，本文提出通过即时梯度而非雅可比传播即可捕捉时序信用，前提是避免梯度污染并跨参数组归一化梯度尺度。方法提出一个简单架构规则：仅当梯度需经过无输出旁路的非线性状态更新时才需\b{eta}2归一化，否则无需归一化。实验在十种架构、灵长类神经数据及流式ML基准上表明，即时梯度配合RMSprop可达到或超越全RTRL性能，同时在n=1024规模下内存消耗仅为1000分之一。
To Augment or Not to Augment? Diagnosing Distributional Symmetry Breaking
Authors: Hannah Lawrence, Elyssa Hofgard, Vasco Portilheiro, Yuxuan Chen, Tess Smidt, Robin Walters
Venue: ICLR 2026 short
First: 2025-10-01T18:26:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:52:45+00:00
Comments: Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026. A short version of this paper appeared at the ICLR AI4Mat workshop in April 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Symmetry-aware methods for machine learning, such as data augmentation and equivariant architectures, encourage correct model behavior on all transformations (e.g. rotations or permutations) of the original dataset. These methods can improve generalization and sample efficiency, under the assumption that the transformed datapoints are highly probable, or "important", under the test distribution. In this work, we develop a method for critically evaluating this assumption. In particular, we propose a metric to quantify the amount of symmetry breaking in a dataset, via a two-sample classifier test that distinguishes between the original dataset and its randomly augmented equivalent. We validate our metric on synthetic datasets, and then use it to uncover surprisingly high degrees of symmetry-breaking in several benchmark point cloud datasets, constituting a severe form of dataset bias. We show theoretically that distributional symmetry-breaking can prevent invariant methods from performing optimally even when the underlying labels are truly invariant, for invariant ridge regression in the infinite feature limit. Empirically, the implication for symmetry-aware methods is dataset-dependent: equivariant methods still impart benefits on some symmetry-biased datasets, but not others, particularly when the symmetry bias is predictive of the labels. Overall, these findings suggest that understanding equivariance -- both when it works, and why -- may require rethinking symmetry biases in the data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：是否需要增强？诊断分布对称性破坏
机器学习中的对称感知方法（如数据增强和等变架构）鼓励模型在原始数据集的所有变换（如旋转或排列）上表现正确。这些方法在假设变换后的数据点在测试分布中高度可能或“重要”时，可提升泛化能力和样本效率。本文提出一种方法，用于批判性评估该假设。具体而言，我们提出一种度量指标，通过两样本分类器测试（区分原始数据集与其随机增强版本）量化数据集中对称性破坏的程度。我们在合成数据集上验证该指标，并发现多个基准点云数据集中存在显著对称性破坏，构成严重形式的数据偏差。理论上，我们证明分布对称性破坏即使在标签真正不变的情况下，也可能阻碍不变方法在无限特征极限下的等变岭回归中达到最优性能。实证上，对称感知方法的效果依赖于数据集：等变方法在部分对称性偏倚数据集上仍有益，但在其他数据集（特别是对称性偏倚可预测标签时）则无益。总体而言，这些发现表明，理解等变性——包括其何时有效及为何有效——可能需要重新审视数据中的对称性偏倚。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether data augmentation and symmetry-aware methods are beneficial by diagnosing whether datasets exhibit distributional symmetry-breaking, where augmented data points are not representative of the true test distribution. The authors propose a two-sample classifier metric to quantify how much a dataset deviates from symmetry, validated on synthetic data and applied to benchmark point cloud datasets, revealing significant asymmetry. Theoretically, they show that even with truly invariant labels, symmetry-breaking can hinder invariant methods like invariant ridge regression. Empirically, equivariant methods still improve performance on some biased datasets but not others, especially when symmetry correlates with label prediction, suggesting that the effectiveness of symmetry-aware methods depends critically on the dataset’s inherent bias.
本文探讨数据增强与对称性感知方法是否有效，通过提出一种两样本分类器指标来量化数据集是否存在分布对称性破坏——即增强数据点不代表真实测试分布。该指标在合成数据上验证后，应用于多个基准点云数据集，发现存在显著的不对称性。理论上证明，即使标签本身具有对称性，分布不对称也会阻碍如不变岭回归等不变方法的最优表现。实证表明，对称性方法在部分偏倚数据集上仍有效，但在对称性与标签预测强相关的数据集上无效，说明对称性方法的效果高度依赖于数据本身的偏倚特性。
Object-Reconstruction-Aware Whole-body Control of Mobile Manipulators
Authors: Fatih Dursun, Bruno Vilhena Adorno, Simon Watson, Wei Pan
First: 2025-09-04T10:52:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:52:20+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 17 figures, 5 tables. Under Review for the IEEE Transactions on Robotics (T-RO)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Object reconstruction and inspection tasks play a crucial role in various robotics applications. Identifying paths that reveal the most unknown areas of the object is paramount in this context, as it directly affects reconstruction efficiency. Current methods often use sampling based path planning techniques, evaluating views along the path to enhance reconstruction performance. However, these methods are computationally expensive as they require evaluating several candidate views on the path. To this end, we propose a computationally efficient solution that relies on calculating a focus point in the most informative region and having the robot maintain this point in the camera field of view along the path. In this way, object reconstruction related information is incorporated into the whole body control of a mobile manipulator employing a visibility constraint without the need for an additional path planner. We conducted comprehensive and realistic simulations using a large dataset of 114 diverse objects of varying sizes from 57 categories to compare our method with a sampling based planning strategy and a strategy that does not employ informative paths using Bayesian data analysis. Furthermore, to demonstrate the applicability and generality of the proposed approach, we conducted real world experiments with an 8 DoF omnidirectional mobile manipulator and a legged manipulator. Our results suggest that, compared to a sampling based strategy, there is no statistically significant difference in object reconstruction entropy, and there is a 52.3% probability that they are practically equivalent in terms of coverage. In contrast, our method is 6.2 to 19.36 times faster in terms of computation time and reduces the total time the robot spends between views by 13.76% to 27.9%, depending on the camera FoV and model resolution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向物体重建的移动操作臂全身控制
物体重建与检测任务在各类机器人应用中至关重要。在此背景下，识别能揭示物体最大未知区域的路径极为关键，因为它直接影响重建效率。当前方法常采用基于采样的路径规划技术，沿路径评估不同视角以提升重建性能，但此类方法计算成本高昂，需评估路径上多个候选视角。为此，我们提出一种计算高效的解决方案：通过计算最具信息量区域的焦点，并使机器人沿路径保持该点在相机视野内。该方法将物体重建相关信息融入移动操作臂的全身控制中，且无需额外路径规划器。我们使用包含57类、114个不同尺寸物体的大规模数据集，通过贝叶斯数据分析，对本方法与基于采样的规划策略及不采用信息路径策略进行了全面且真实的仿真比较。此外，为验证所提方法的适用性与通用性，我们在8自由度全向移动操作臂和腿式操作臂上进行了真实世界实验。结果表明，与基于采样的策略相比，本方法在物体重建熵上无统计显著差异，且在覆盖率上实际等效的概率达52.3%。相比之下，本方法在计算时间上快6.2至19.36倍，且根据相机视场角和模型分辨率不同，机器人在视角间停留的总时间减少13.76%至27.9%。
Summary / 总结
To improve object reconstruction efficiency in mobile manipulator tasks, this work proposes a computationally efficient whole-body control method that dynamically maintains a focus point in the most informative region of the object within the camera’s field of view, eliminating the need for expensive sampling-based path planning. The method integrates visibility constraints directly into motion control, enabling real-time adaptation without additional planners. Experimental results across 114 diverse objects and real-world tests with two robotic platforms show that the proposed approach achieves comparable reconstruction entropy to sampling-based methods while being 6.2 to 19.36 times faster in computation and reducing view-transition time by 13.76% to 27.9%, demonstrating both practical equivalence and significant performance gains.
为提升移动操作臂在物体重建任务中的效率，本文提出一种无需额外路径规划器的计算高效方法，通过动态保持相机视野内最信息丰富的区域焦点，将重建相关视觉信息直接融入全身运动控制。实验在114个多样化物体和两种真实机器人平台（8自由度全向移动操作臂与腿式操作臂）上进行，结果显示该方法在重建熵上与采样路径规划方法无显著差异，计算速度提升6.2至19.36倍，且视点切换时间减少13.76%至27.9%，验证了其在实际应用中的等效性与高效性。
Stop Probing, Start Coding: Why Linear Probes and Sparse Autoencoders Fail at Compositional Generalisation
Authors: Vitória Barin Pacela, Shruti Joshi, Isabela Camacho, Simon Lacoste-Julien, David Klindt
First: 2026-03-30T17:52:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:52:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The linear representation hypothesis states that neural network activations encode high-level concepts as linear mixtures. However, under superposition, this encoding is a projection from a higher-dimensional concept space into a lower-dimensional activation space, and a linear decision boundary in the concept space need not remain linear after projection. In this setting, classical sparse coding methods with per-sample iterative inference leverage compressed sensing guarantees to recover latent factors. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs), on the other hand, amortise sparse inference into a fixed encoder, introducing a systematic gap. We show this amortisation gap persists across training set sizes, latent dimensions, and sparsity levels, causing SAEs to fail under out-of-distribution (OOD) compositional shifts. Through controlled experiments that decompose the failure, we identify dictionary learning -- not the inference procedure -- as the binding constraint: SAE-learned dictionaries point in substantially wrong directions, and replacing the encoder with per-sample FISTA on the same dictionary does not close the gap. An oracle baseline proves the problem is solvable with a good dictionary at all scales tested. Our results reframe the SAE failure as a dictionary learning challenge, not an amortisation problem, and point to scalable dictionary learning as the key open problem for sparse inference under superposition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：停止探针，开始编码：为何线性探针与稀疏自编码器在组合泛化中失效
线性表示假设认为神经网络激活值以线性混合方式编码高级概念。然而，在叠加效应下，该编码是从高维概念空间投影到低维激活空间，概念空间中的线性决策边界在投影后未必保持线性。在此背景下，经典稀疏编码方法通过逐样本迭代推断，利用压缩感知保证恢复潜在因子；而稀疏自编码器（SAEs）则将稀疏推断的计算摊销至固定编码器，引入系统性差距。我们证明该摊销差距在训练集规模、潜在维度和稀疏度上均持续存在，导致SAEs在分布外（OOD）组合偏移下失效。通过分解失败原因的受控实验，我们识别出字典学习——而非推断过程——是关键约束：SAE学习到的字典指向明显错误方向，且用相同字典替换编码器为逐样本FISTA推断也无法弥合差距。一个Oracle基线证明，在所有测试尺度下，只要字典足够好，问题即可解决。我们的结果将SAE失效重新定位为字典学习挑战，而非摊销问题，并指出可扩展字典学习是稀疏推断在叠加效应下亟待解决的核心问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to generalize compositionally under out-of-distribution shifts, this work investigates whether their amortized inference mechanism is the root cause. The authors demonstrate that the core issue lies not in the inference procedure but in the learned dictionary, which points in incorrect directions under superposition. Through controlled experiments across varying training sizes, latent dimensions, and sparsity levels, they show that replacing the encoder with per-sample FISTA on the same dictionary does not improve performance, while an oracle baseline with a correct dictionary achieves success. This reframes SAE failure as a dictionary learning problem, highlighting scalable dictionary learning as the key challenge for sparse inference under superposition.
该研究旨在解释为何在分布外组合迁移任务中，稀疏自编码器（SAE）表现不佳。作者指出，在叠加性假设下，SAE失败的根本原因并非其近似推理机制，而是其学习到的字典方向错误。通过控制实验，他们在不同训练规模、潜在维度和稀疏度下发现，SAE的字典指向错误方向，即使替换为逐样本FISTA推理也无法弥补差距。一个理想基线表明，只要字典正确，性能可恢复，从而将问题重新定位为可扩展的字典学习挑战，而非推理近似问题。
Equivariant symmetry-aware head pose estimation for fetal MRI
Authors: Ramya Muthukrishnan, Borjan Gagoski, Aryn Lee, P. Ellen Grant, Elfar Adalsteinsson, Benjamin Billot, Polina Golland
First: 2025-12-04T15:15:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:52:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present E(3)-Pose, a novel fast pose estimation method that jointly and explicitly models rotation equivariance and object symmetry. Our work is motivated by the challenging problem of accounting for fetal head motion during a diagnostic MRI scan. We aim to enable automatic adaptive prescription of diagnostic 2D MRI slices with 6-DoF head pose estimation, supported by rapid low-resolution 3D MRI volumes acquired before each 2D slice. Existing pose estimation methods struggle to generalize to clinical volumes due to pose ambiguities induced by inherent anatomical symmetries, as well as low resolution, noise, and artifacts. In contrast, E(3)-Pose captures anatomical symmetries and rigid pose equivariance by construction, and yields robust estimates of the fetal head pose. Our experiments on publicly available and representative clinical fetal MRI datasets demonstrate the superior robustness and generalization of our method across domains. Crucially, E(3)-Pose achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on clinical MRI volumes, supporting future clinical translation. Our implementation is publicly available at github.com/MedicalVisionGroup/E3-Pose.

Motivated by the need to accurately estimate fetal head pose during MRI scans to guide adaptive 2D slice acquisition, E(3)-Pose introduces a novel method that explicitly models rotation equivariance and anatomical symmetry. It leverages a symmetry-aware architecture designed to handle pose ambiguities caused by fetal anatomy, low resolution, and imaging artifacts. 为了解决胎儿MRI扫描中因解剖对称性、低分辨率和噪声导致的头部姿态估计难题，E(3)-Pose提出了一种联合建模旋转等变性和物体对称性的新方法，支持6自由度的精准姿态估计。该方法通过架构设计显式融入解剖对称性和刚体变换，从而提升跨临床数据集的泛化能力。在公开的胎儿MRI数据集上的实验表明，E(3)-Pose在准确性和鲁棒性上优于现有方法，达到临床数据上的最先进性能，并展现出良好的跨域泛化能力。
Rethinking Language Model Scaling under Transferable Hypersphere Optimization
Authors: Liliang Ren, Yang Liu, Yelong Shen, Weizhu Chen
First: 2026-03-30T17:51:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:51:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Scaling laws for large language models depend critically on the optimizer and parameterization. Existing hyperparameter transfer laws are mainly developed for first-order optimizers, and they do not structurally prevent training instability at scale. Recent hypersphere optimization methods constrain weight matrices to a fixed-norm hypersphere, offering a promising alternative for more stable scaling. We introduce HyperP (Hypersphere Parameterization), the first framework for transferring optimal learning rates across model width, depth, training tokens, and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) granularity under the Frobenius-sphere constraint with the Muon optimizer. We prove that weight decay is a first-order no-op on the Frobenius sphere, show that Depth-$μ$P remains necessary, and find that the optimal learning rate follows the same data-scaling power law with the "magic exponent" 0.32 previously observed for AdamW. A single base learning rate tuned at the smallest scale transfers across all compute budgets under HyperP, yielding $1.58\times$ compute efficiency over a strong Muon baseline at $6\times10^{21}$ FLOPs. Moreover, HyperP delivers transferable stability: all monitored instability indicators, including $Z$-values, output RMS, and activation outliers, remain bounded and non-increasing under training FLOPs scaling. We also propose SqrtGate, an MoE gating mechanism derived from the hypersphere constraint that preserves output RMS across MoE granularities for improved granularity scaling, and show that hypersphere optimization enables substantially larger auxiliary load-balancing weights, yielding both strong performance and good expert balance. We release our training codebase at https://github.com/microsoft/ArchScale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在可迁移超球优化下重新思考语言模型的扩展规律
大语言模型的扩展规律高度依赖优化器和参数化方式。现有超参数迁移规律主要针对一阶优化器，无法在规模扩展时结构性地防止训练不稳定。近期的超球优化方法将权重矩阵约束在固定范数的超球面上，为更稳定的扩展提供了有前景的替代方案。我们提出了HyperP（超球参数化），这是首个在Frobenius球面约束下，使用Muon优化器实现跨模型宽度、深度、训练token数及MoE粒度最优学习率迁移的框架。我们证明了权重衰减在Frobenius球面上是一阶无操作，表明Depth-μP仍为必要，且发现最优学习率遵循与AdamW观察到的“魔法指数”0.32相同的幂律数据扩展规律。在HyperP下，仅需在最小规模调优一个基础学习率，即可在所有计算预算下迁移，相比强Muon基线在6×10²¹ FLOPs下实现1.58倍的计算效率。此外，HyperP提供可迁移的稳定性：所有监控的不稳定性指标（包括Z值、输出RMS和激活异常值）在训练FLOPs扩展下保持有界且非递增。我们还提出了SqrtGate，一种源于超球约束的MoE门控机制，可保持不同MoE粒度下的输出RMS，从而提升粒度扩展能力，并表明超球优化可显著增大辅助负载均衡权重，实现强性能与良好专家平衡。我们将在https://github.com/microsoft/ArchScale发布训练代码库。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of traditional optimizers at scale, this work proposes HyperP, a hypersphere parameterization framework that constrains weight matrices to a fixed-norm Frobenius sphere under the Muon optimizer. It enables transferable learning rates across model width, depth, training tokens, and MoE configurations, leveraging a "magic exponent" of 0.32 for learning rate scaling. Experiments show a single base learning rate transfers across compute budgets, achieving 1.58x compute efficiency over a Muon baseline at 6e21 FLOPs, while maintaining bounded and non-increasing instability metrics like Z-values and output RMS. Additionally, the SqrtGate MoE gating mechanism preserves output RMS and supports larger load-balancing weights, enhancing both performance and expert balance.
为解决传统优化器在大规模训练中的不稳定性，本文提出HyperP框架，将权重矩阵约束在固定范数的Frobenius球面上并配合Muon优化器，实现学习率在模型宽度、深度、训练token数及MoE粒度间的可迁移。实验表明，单一基础学习率即可跨算力预算迁移，于6e21 FLOPs下比Muon基线提升1.58倍计算效率，同时保持Z值、输出RMS等不稳定性指标有界且随训练FLOPs非递增。此外，提出的SqrtGate MoE门控机制维持输出RMS稳定，并支持更大负载均衡权重，兼顾性能与专家均衡。
FocusVLA: Focused Visual Utilization for Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Yichi Zhang, Weihao Yuan, Yizhuo Zhang, Xidong Zhang, Jia Wan
First: 2026-03-30T17:50:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:50:54+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 18 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models improve action generation by conditioning policies on rich vision-language information. However, current auto-regressive policies are constrained by three bottlenecks: (1) architectural bias drives models to overlook visual details, (2) an excessive number of visual tokens makes attention difficult to focus on the correct regions, and (3) task-irrelevant visual information introduces substantial noise - together severely impairing the quality of action. In this paper, we investigate how to effectively utilize different visual representations for action generation. To this end, we first empirically validate the above issues and show that VLA performance is primarily limited by how visual information is utilized, rather than by the quality of visual representations. Based on these insights, we introduce FocusVLA, a novel paradigm that directs the model's attention to task-relevant visual regions to effectively bridge vision to action. Specifically, we first propose Modality Cascaded Attention to eliminate shortcut pathways, thereby compelling VLA models to rely on task-relevant visual details for action generation. Furthermore, we propose Focus Attention, which dynamically selects task-relevant visual patches to control information quantity while explicitly modulating their influence to suppress task-irrelevant noise. Extensive experiments on both simulated and real-world robotic benchmarks demonstrate that FocusVLA not only effectively leverages visual details to perform dexterous manipulations, but also substantially improves performance and accelerates convergence across a variety of tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FocusVLA：面向视觉-语言-动作模型的聚焦视觉利用
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通过将策略条件化于丰富的视觉-语言信息来提升动作生成能力。然而，当前自回归策略受三大瓶颈制约：（1）架构偏置导致模型忽略视觉细节；（2）过多视觉标记使注意力难以聚焦于正确区域；（3）任务无关视觉信息引入大量噪声——三者共同严重损害动作质量。本文研究如何有效利用不同视觉表征进行动作生成。为此，我们首先实证验证上述问题，表明VLA性能主要受限于视觉信息的利用方式，而非视觉表征质量。基于此，我们提出FocusVLA，一种新范式，引导模型关注任务相关视觉区域，有效连接视觉与动作。具体而言，我们提出模态级联注意力，消除捷径路径，迫使VLA模型依赖任务相关视觉细节生成动作；并提出聚焦注意力，动态选择任务相关视觉块，控制信息量并显式调节其影响以抑制无关噪声。在模拟与真实机器人基准上的大量实验表明，FocusVLA不仅有效利用视觉细节实现灵巧操作，还在多种任务中显著提升性能并加速收敛。

The method employs Modality Cascaded Attention to eliminate shortcut pathways and Force Attention to dynamically select and modulate relevant visual patches. 为解决当前视觉-语言-动作模型在利用视觉信息生成动作时存在的瓶颈，本文提出FocusVLA新范式，通过引导模型关注任务相关视觉区域以实现视觉到动作的有效衔接。该方法结合模态级联注意力机制消除捷径路径，强制模型依赖相关视觉细节，并引入聚焦注意力机制动态选择和调节任务相关视觉块的影响以抑制噪声。在模拟与真实机器人基准测试中，实验表明FocusVLA显著提升动作质量、加速收敛，并支持精细操作。
Expectation Error Bounds for Transfer Learning in Linear Regression and Linear Neural Networks
Authors: Meitong Liu, Christopher Jung, Rui Li, Xue Feng, Han Zhao
First: 2026-03-30T17:50:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:50:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In transfer learning, the learner leverages auxiliary data to improve generalization on a main task. However, the precise theoretical understanding of when and how auxiliary data help remains incomplete. We provide new insights on this issue in two canonical linear settings: ordinary least squares regression and under-parameterized linear neural networks. For linear regression, we derive exact closed-form expressions for the expected generalization error with bias-variance decomposition, yielding necessary and sufficient conditions for auxiliary tasks to improve generalization on the main task. We also derive globally optimal task weights as outputs of solvable optimization programs, with consistency guarantees for empirical estimates. For linear neural networks with shared representations of width $q \leq K$, where $K$ is the number of auxiliary tasks, we derive a non-asymptotic expectation bound on the generalization error, yielding the first non-vacuous sufficient condition for beneficial auxiliary learning in this setting, as well as principled directions for task weight curation. We achieve this by proving a new column-wise low-rank perturbation bound for random matrices, which improves upon existing bounds by preserving fine-grained column structures. Our results are verified on synthetic data simulated with controlled parameters.
Summary / 总结
In transfer learning, the learner leverages auxiliary data to improve generalization on a main task.
ParaSpeechCLAP: A Dual-Encoder Speech-Text Model for Rich Stylistic Language-Audio Pretraining
Authors: Anuj Diwan, Eunsol Choi, David Harwath
First: 2026-03-30T17:50:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:50:07+00:00
Comments: Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce ParaSpeechCLAP, a dual-encoder contrastive model that maps speech and text style captions into a common embedding space, supporting a wide range of intrinsic (speaker-level) and situational (utterance-level) descriptors (such as pitch, texture and emotion) far beyond the narrow set handled by existing models. We train specialized ParaSpeechCLAP-Intrinsic and ParaSpeechCLAP-Situational models alongside a unified ParaSpeechCLAP-Combined model, finding that specialization yields stronger performance on individual style dimensions while the unified model excels on compositional evaluation. We further show that ParaSpeechCLAP-Intrinsic benefits from an additional classification loss and class-balanced training. We demonstrate our models' performance on style caption retrieval, speech attribute classification and as an inference-time reward model that improves style-prompted TTS without additional training. ParaSpeechCLAP outperforms baselines on most metrics across all three applications. Our models and code are released at https://github.com/ajd12342/paraspeechclap .
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for richer, more nuanced modeling of speech style beyond speaker identity, ParaSpeechCLAP introduces a dual-encoder contrastive model that aligns speech and text captions in a shared embedding space, capturing intrinsic (speaker-level) and situational (utterance-level) attributes like pitch, texture, and emotion. The model is trained in three variants—Intrinsic, Situational, and Combined—with specialization improving performance on individual style dimensions while the unified model excels in compositional tasks. Experimental results show that ParaSpeechCLAP-Intrinsic further benefits from classification loss and class-balanced training, and the overall models outperform baselines across style caption retrieval, speech attribute classification, and as a reward model for style-guided TTS without retraining.
为突破现有模型对语音风格建模的局限，ParaSpeechCLAP提出一种双编码器对比模型，将语音与文本风格描述映射到统一嵌入空间，支持如音高、音质、情绪等更丰富的内在（说话人级）与情境（语句级）属性。模型分内在、情境及联合三种变体训练，专精变体在单维度风格任务上表现更优，联合模型在组合式评估中更胜一筹。实验表明，内在模型结合分类损失与类别平衡训练可进一步提升性能，且整体模型在风格检索、语音属性分类及无额外训练的风格引导TTS奖励建模中均优于基线方法。
RAD-AI: Rethinking Architecture Documentation for AI-Augmented Ecosystems
Authors: Oliver Aleksander Larsen, Mahyar T. Moghaddam
First: 2026-03-30T17:48:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:48:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ANGE 2026, co-located with IEEE ICSA 2026. 8 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI-augmented ecosystems (interconnected systems where multiple AI components interact through shared data and infrastructure) are becoming the architectural norm for smart cities, autonomous fleets, and intelligent platforms. Yet the architecture documentation frameworks practitioners rely on, arc42 and the C4 model, were designed for deterministic software and cannot capture probabilistic behavior, data-dependent evolution, or dual ML/software lifecycles. This gap carries regulatory consequence: the EU AI Act (Regulation 2024/1689) mandates technical documentation through Annex IV that no existing framework provides structured support for, with enforcement for high-risk systems beginning August 2, 2026. We present RAD-AI, a backward-compatible extension framework that augments arc42 with eight AI-specific sections and C4 with three diagram extensions, complemented by a systematic EU AI Act Annex IV compliance mapping. A regulatory coverage assessment with six experienced software-architecture practitioners provides preliminary evidence that RAD-AI increases Annex IV addressability from approximately 36% to 93% (mean rating) and demonstrates substantial improvement over existing frameworks. Comparative analysis on two production AI platforms (Uber Michelangelo, Netflix Metaflow) captures eight additional AI-specific concerns missed by standard frameworks and demonstrates that documentation deficiencies are structural rather than domain-specific. An illustrative smart mobility ecosystem case study reveals ecosystem-level concerns, including cascading drift and differentiated compliance obligations, that are invisible under standard notation.

EgoDemoGen: Egocentric Demonstration Generation for Viewpoint Generalization in Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Yuan Xu, Jiabing Yang, Xiaofeng Wang, Yixiang Chen, Zheng Zhu, Bowen Fang, Guan Huang, Xinze Chen, Yun Ye, Qiang Zhang, Peiyan Li, Xiangnan Wu, Kai Wang, Bing Zhan, Shuo Lu, Jing Liu, Nianfeng Liu, Yan Huang, Liang Wang
First: 2025-09-26T16:54:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:48:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Imitation learning based visuomotor policies have achieved strong performance in robotic manipulation, yet they often remain sensitive to egocentric viewpoint shifts. Unlike third-person viewpoint changes that only move the camera, egocentric shifts simultaneously alter both the camera pose and the robot action coordinate frame, making it necessary to jointly transfer action trajectories and synthesize corresponding observations under novel egocentric viewpoints. To address this challenge, we present EgoDemoGen, a framework that generates paired observation--action demonstrations under novel egocentric viewpoints through two key components: 1{)} EgoTrajTransfer, which transfers robot trajectories to the novel egocentric coordinate frame through motion-skill segmentation, geometry-aware transformation, and inverse kinematics filtering; and 2{)} EgoViewTransfer, a conditional video generation model that fuses a novel-viewpoint reprojected scene video and a robot motion video rendered from the transferred trajectory to synthesize photorealistic observations, trained with a self-supervised double reprojection strategy without requiring multi-viewpoint data. Experiments in simulation and real-world settings show that EgoDemoGen consistently improves policy success rates under both standard and novel egocentric viewpoints, with absolute gains of +24.6\% and +16.9\% in simulation and +16.0\% and +23.0\% on the real robot. Moreover, EgoViewTransfer achieves superior video generation quality for novel egocentric observations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EgoDemoGen：面向机器人操作视角泛化的第一人称演示生成
基于模仿学习的视觉运动策略在机器人操作中表现优异，但常对第一人称视角变化敏感。与仅移动相机的第三人称视角变化不同，第一人称视角变化同时改变相机位姿和机器人动作坐标系，因此必须联合转移动作轨迹并合成对应的新视角观测。为解决此问题，我们提出EgoDemoGen框架，通过两个关键组件生成新第一人称视角下的观测-动作演示对：1) EgoTrajTransfer，通过运动技能分割、几何感知变换和逆运动学过滤，将机器人轨迹转移至新第一人称坐标系；2) EgoViewTransfer，一种条件视频生成模型，融合新视角重投影场景视频与从转移轨迹渲染的机器人运动视频，合成逼真观测，采用自监督双重重投影策略训练，无需多视角数据。仿真与真实实验表明，EgoDemoGen在标准及新第一人称视角下均显著提升策略成功率，仿真中提升24.6%和16.9%，真实机器人上提升16.0%和23.0%。此外，EgoViewTransfer在新第一人称观测视频生成质量上表现更优。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the sensitivity of visuomotor policies to egocentric viewpoint changes—where both camera pose and action coordinate frame shift—EgoDemoGen generates paired demonstrations under novel viewpoints using two components: EgoTrajTransfer, which reorients robot trajectories via motion segmentation, geometry-aware transformation, and inverse kinematics filtering; and EgoViewTransfer, a conditional video generator that combines reprojected scene videos with rendered motion videos to produce photorealistic observations, trained via self-supervised double reprojection. Experiments show EgoDemoGen boosts policy success rates by +24.6% and +16.9% in simulation, and +16.0% and +23.0% on real robots, with EgoViewTransfer demonstrating superior video quality for novel egocentric views.
为解决机器人操作策略在视角变化时性能下降的问题，EgoDemoGen通过两个模块生成新视角下的观察-动作演示：EgoTrajTransfer将机器人轨迹通过运动分割、几何感知变换和逆运动学过滤转换至新坐标系；EgoViewTransfer结合重投影场景视频与轨迹渲染视频生成逼真观察，采用自监督双重重投影训练无需多视角数据。实验表明，该方法在仿真中提升成功率24.6%和16.9%，在真实机器人上提升16.0%和23.0%，且EgoViewTransfer生成的视频质量优异。
See it to Place it: Evolving Macro Placements with Vision-Language Models
Authors: Ikechukwu Uchendu, Swati Goel, Karly Hou, Ebrahim Songhori, Kuang-Huei Lee, Joe Wenjie Jiang, Vijay Janapa Reddi, Vincent Zhuang
First: 2026-03-30T17:47:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:47:34+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, 11 figures, 14 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose using Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for macro placement in chip floorplanning, a complex optimization task that has recently shown promising advancements through machine learning methods. Because human designers rely heavily on spatial reasoning to arrange components on the chip canvas, we hypothesize that VLMs with strong visual reasoning abilities can effectively complement existing learning-based approaches. We introduce VeoPlace (Visual Evolutionary Optimization Placement), a novel framework that uses a VLM, without any fine-tuning, to guide the actions of a base placer by constraining them to subregions of the chip canvas. The VLM proposals are iteratively optimized through an evolutionary search strategy with respect to resulting placement quality. On open-source benchmarks, VeoPlace outperforms the best prior learning-based approach on 9 of 10 benchmarks with peak wirelength reductions exceeding 32%. We further demonstrate that VeoPlace generalizes to analytical placers, improving DREAMPlace performance on all 8 evaluated benchmarks with gains up to 4.3%. Our approach opens new possibilities for electronic design automation tools that leverage foundation models to solve complex physical design problems.
