Pandora: Articulated 3D Scene Graphs from Egocentric Vision
Authors: Alan Yu, Yun Chang, Christopher Xie, Luca Carlone
First: 2026-03-30T17:47:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:47:07+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 5 figures. Presented at the 2025 British Machine Vision Conference (BMVC) in Sheffield, UK
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic mapping systems typically approach building metric-semantic scene representations from the robot's own sensors and cameras. However, these "first person" maps inherit the robot's own limitations due to its embodiment or skillset, which may leave many aspects of the environment unexplored. For example, the robot might not be able to open drawers or access wall cabinets. In this sense, the map representation is not as complete, and requires a more capable robot to fill in the gaps. We narrow these blind spots in current methods by leveraging egocentric data captured as a human naturally explores a scene wearing Project Aria glasses, giving a way to directly transfer knowledge about articulation from the human to any deployable robot. We demonstrate that, by using simple heuristics, we can leverage egocentric data to recover models of articulate object parts, with quality comparable to those of state-of-the-art methods based on other input modalities. We also show how to integrate these models into 3D scene graph representations, leading to a better understanding of object dynamics and object-container relationships. We finally demonstrate that these articulated 3D scene graphs enhance a robot's ability to perform mobile manipulation tasks, showcasing an application where a Boston Dynamics Spot is tasked with retrieving concealed target items, given only the 3D scene graph as input.
中文标题/摘要
标题：潘多拉：从第一人称视觉构建可关节化的3D场景图
机器人映射系统通常从机器人自身的传感器和摄像头构建具有度量与语义的场景表示。然而，这些“第一人称”地图会因机器人的具身性或技能限制而存在盲区，导致环境许多方面未被探索。例如，机器人可能无法打开抽屉或接触墙柜。因此，地图表示不够完整，需要更强大的机器人来填补空白。我们通过利用人类佩戴Project Aria眼镜自然探索场景时采集的第一人称数据，将人类对物体关节结构的知识直接迁移到任何可部署的机器人上，从而缩小当前方法的盲区。我们证明，仅使用简单启发式方法，即可利用第一人称数据恢复可关节化物体部件的模型，其质量可媲美基于其他输入模态的最先进方法。我们还展示了如何将这些模型整合进3D场景图表示，从而更好地理解物体动态与物体-容器关系。最后，我们证明这些可关节化3D场景图能增强机器人执行移动操作任务的能力，例如，仅以3D场景图作为输入，让Boston Dynamics Spot机器人完成获取隐藏目标物品的任务。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of robot-generated maps that miss articulated object interactions due to robotic embodiment constraints, this work leverages egocentric vision data from humans wearing Project Aria glasses to learn articulated 3D scene graphs. Using simple heuristics, the method reconstructs object part models from human exploration data, achieving quality comparable to state-of-the-art methods using other modalities. These models are integrated into 3D scene graphs that capture object-container relationships and dynamics, enabling robots to better understand and manipulate concealed objects. Experiments show that a Boston Dynamics Spot robot, guided solely by the 3D scene graph, successfully retrieves hidden items, demonstrating improved mobile manipulation performance.
为解决机器人因自身物理限制难以捕捉环境中的可动部件问题，该研究利用人类佩戴Project Aria眼镜进行自然探索所采集的主观视角数据，通过简单启发式方法重建物体的可动结构，其质量可媲美其他模态的最新方法。这些结构被整合进3D场景图，以表达物体动态与容器关系，从而增强机器人对场景的理解。实验表明，仅使用该场景图，Boston Dynamics Spot机器人在执行移动操作任务（如取回隐藏物品）时表现显著提升。
SAGAI-MID: A Generative AI-Driven Middleware for Dynamic Runtime Interoperability
Authors: Oliver Aleksander Larsen, Mahyar T. Moghaddam
First: 2026-03-30T17:46:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:46:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted at SAGAI 2026, co-located with IEEE ICSA 2026. 8 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern distributed systems integrate heterogeneous services, REST APIs with different schema versions, GraphQL endpoints, and IoT devices with proprietary payloads that suffer from persistent schema mismatches. Traditional static adapters require manual coding for every schema pair and cannot handle novel combinations at runtime. We present SAGAI-MID, a FastAPI-based middleware that uses large language models (LLMs) to dynamically detect and resolve schema mismatches at runtime. The system employs a five-layer pipeline: hybrid detection (structural diff plus LLM semantic analysis), dual resolution strategies (per-request LLM transformation and LLM-generated reusable adapter code), and a three-tier safeguard stack (validation, ensemble voting, rule-based fallback). We frame the architecture through Bass et al.'s interoperability tactics, transforming them from design-time artifacts into runtime capabilities. We evaluate SAGAI-MID on 10 interoperability scenarios spanning REST version migration, IoT-to-analytics bridging, and GraphQL protocol conversion across six LLMs from two providers. The best-performing configuration achieves 0.90 pass@1 accuracy. The CODEGEN strategy consistently outperforms DIRECT (0.83 vs 0.77 mean pass@1), while cost varies by over 30x across models with no proportional accuracy gain; the most accurate model is also the cheapest. We discuss implications for software architects adopting LLMs as runtime architectural components.
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to handle dynamic schema mismatches in heterogeneous distributed systems without manual intervention, SAGAI-MID leverages large language models within a FastAPI middleware to detect and resolve interoperability issues at runtime. Its five-layer pipeline combines structural diffing with LLM semantic analysis for detection, supports per-request LLM transformation or reusable adapter code generation for resolution, and includes a three-tier safeguard stack for reliability. Evaluated across 10 scenarios involving REST, IoT, and GraphQL interoperability using six LLMs, the system achieved 0.90 pass@1 accuracy with the CODEGEN strategy outperforming DIRECT (0.83 vs 0.77). Cost varied over 30x across models without proportional accuracy gains, and the most accurate model was also the cheapest.
为应对异构分布式系统中动态出现的schema不匹配问题，SAGAI-MID提出一种基于FastAPI的中间件，利用大语言模型在运行时自动检测并解决互操作性问题。其五层架构结合结构差异与LLM语义分析进行检测，支持按请求转换或生成可复用适配器代码进行处理，并配备三级保障机制确保可靠性。在10个场景中使用6个LLM评估，系统达到0.90的pass@1准确率，CODEGEN策略优于DIRECT（0.83 vs 0.77），且成本差异达30倍以上，无准确率损失，最准确模型同时也是最便宜的。
SOLE-R1: Video-Language Reasoning as the Sole Reward for On-Robot Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Philip Schroeder, Thomas Weng, Karl Schmeckpeper, Eric Rosen, Stephen Hart, Ondrej Biza
First: 2026-03-30T17:46:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:46:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, motivating efforts to leverage these models to supervise robot learning. However, when used as evaluators in reinforcement learning (RL), today's strongest models often fail under partial observability and distribution shift, enabling policies to exploit perceptual errors rather than solve the task. To address this limitation, we introduce SOLE-R1 (Self-Observing LEarner), a video-language reasoning model explicitly designed to serve as the sole reward signal for online RL. Given only raw video observations and a natural-language goal, SOLE-R1 performs per-timestep spatiotemporal chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and produces dense estimates of task progress that can be used directly as rewards. To train SOLE-R1, we develop a large-scale video trajectory and reasoning synthesis pipeline that generates temporally grounded CoT traces aligned with continuous progress supervision. This data is combined with foundational spatial and multi-frame temporal reasoning, and used to train the model with a hybrid framework that couples supervised fine-tuning with RL from verifiable rewards. Across four different simulation environments and a real-robot setting, SOLE-R1 enables zero-shot online RL from random initialization: robots learn previously unseen manipulation tasks without ground-truth rewards, success indicators, demonstrations, or task-specific tuning. SOLE-R1 succeeds on 24 unseen tasks and substantially outperforms strong vision-language rewarders, including GPT-5 and Gemini-3-Pro, while exhibiting markedly greater robustness to reward hacking.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust reward signals in robot reinforcement learning, this work introduces SOLE-R1, a video-language model designed to act as the sole reward function for online RL. It reasons over raw video and natural-language goals using per-timestep spatiotemporal chain-of-thought to generate dense, task-progress estimates directly usable as rewards. Trained via a synthesized dataset of temporally grounded reasoning traces combined with hybrid supervised fine-tuning and RL from verifiable rewards, SOLE-R1 achieves zero-shot learning across four simulation environments and a real robot, mastering 24 unseen manipulation tasks without ground-truth rewards or demonstrations. It significantly outperforms state-of-the-art vision-language reward models like GPT-5 and Gemini-3-Pro while showing stronger resistance to reward hacking.
为解决机器人强化学习中奖励信号不鲁棒的问题，本文提出SOLE-R1，一种专为在线强化学习设计的视频-语言模型，可作为唯一奖励信号。它基于原始视频和自然语言目标，逐时间步进行时空链式推理，生成可直接用于奖励的密集任务进度估计。通过合成大量具有时间对齐推理轨迹的数据，并结合监督微调与基于可验证奖励的强化学习联合训练，SOLE-R1在四个仿真环境和真实机器人上实现零样本在线学习，成功掌握24个未见过的操控任务，无需真实奖励、演示或任务特定调优。其性能显著优于GPT-5和Gemini-3-Pro等主流视觉-语言奖励模型，且对奖励欺骗更具鲁棒性。
BIOGEN: Evidence-Grounded Multi-Agent Reasoning Framework for Transcriptomic Interpretation in Antimicrobial Resistance
Authors: Elias Hossain, Mehrdad Shoeibi, Ivan Garibay, Niloofar Yousefi
First: 2025-10-17T14:56:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:46:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Interpreting gene clusters from RNA-seq remains challenging, especially in antimicrobial resistance studies where mechanistic context is essential for hypothesis generation. Conventional enrichment methods summarize co-expressed modules using predefined categories, but often return sparse results and lack cluster-specific, literature-linked explanations. We present BIOGEN, an evidence-grounded multi-agent framework for post hoc interpretation of RNA-seq transcriptional modules that integrates biomedical retrieval, structured reasoning, and multi-critic verification. BIOGEN organizes evidence from PubMed and UniProt into traceable cluster-level interpretations with explicit support and confidence tiering. On a primary Salmonella enterica dataset, BIOGEN achieved strong evidence-grounding performance while reducing hallucination from 0.67 in an unconstrained LLM setting to 0.00 under retrieval-grounded configurations. Compared with KEGG/ORA and GO/ORA, BIOGEN recovered broader biological coverage, identifying substantially more biological themes per cluster. Across four additional bacterial RNA-seq datasets, BIOGEN maintained zero hallucination and consistently outperformed KEGG/ORA in cluster-level thematic coverage. These results position BIOGEN as an interpretive support framework that complements transcriptomic workflows through improved traceability, evidential transparency, and biological coverage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BIOGEN：用于抗菌耐药性转录组解释的证据驱动多智能体推理框架
从RNA-seq数据解读基因簇仍具挑战性，尤其在抗菌耐药性研究中，机制背景对假设生成至关重要。传统富集方法使用预定义类别总结共表达模块，但常返回稀疏结果，且缺乏针对簇的、与文献关联的解释。我们提出BIOGEN，一个用于RNA-seq转录模块事后解释的证据驱动多智能体框架，整合生物医学检索、结构化推理与多批评验证。BIOGEN将PubMed和UniProt中的证据组织为可追溯的簇级解释，并明确标注支持与置信度层级。在主要沙门氏菌数据集上，BIOGEN实现强证据支撑性能，将无约束LLM环境下的幻觉率从0.67降至检索支撑配置下的0.00。与KEGG/ORA和GO/ORA相比，BIOGEN覆盖更广泛的生物学主题，每个簇识别出更多生物学主题。在四个其他细菌RNA-seq数据集上，BIOGEN保持零幻觉，并持续优于KEGG/ORA在簇级主题覆盖表现。这些结果使BIOGEN成为一种补充转录组工作流程的解释支持框架，通过提升可追溯性、证据透明度和生物学覆盖范围。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable and evidence-backed analysis of RNA-seq gene clusters in antimicrobial resistance research, BIOGEN introduces a multi-agent framework that combines biomedical retrieval, structured reasoning, and multi-critic verification to generate cluster-level interpretations grounded in PubMed and UniProt evidence. The method organizes findings with explicit support and confidence tiers, enabling traceable, transparent explanations. Experimental results on Salmonella enterica RNA-seq data show BIOGEN reduces hallucination from 0.67 to 0.00 under retrieval-grounded settings and outperforms KEGG/ORA and GO/ORA in biological coverage, identifying significantly more themes per cluster. Across four additional bacterial datasets, BIOGEN maintained zero hallucination and consistently surpassed KEGG/ORA in thematic breadth, demonstrating its utility as a reliable interpretive tool for transcriptomic workflows.
为解决抗菌药物耐药性研究中RNA-seq基因簇解释缺乏机制背景的问题，BIOGEN提出一个结合生物医学检索、结构化推理与多批评验证的多智能体框架，生成基于证据的解释，并对PubMed和UniProt证据进行簇级组织与置信度分级。在沙门氏菌数据集上，其将幻觉率从0.67降至0.00，且在每个簇中识别的生物学主题显著多于KEGG/ORA和GO/ORA。在四个其他细菌RNA-seq数据集中，BIOGEN持续保持零幻觉并优于KEGG/ORA，证明其作为可追溯、透明且覆盖更广的转录组解释工具的有效性。
Online monotone density estimation and log-optimal calibration
Authors: Rohan Hore, Ruodu Wang, Aaditya Ramdas
First: 2026-02-09T17:29:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:40:13+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the problem of online monotone density estimation, where density estimators must be constructed in a predictable manner from sequentially observed data. We propose two online estimators: an online analogue of the classical Grenander estimator, and an expert aggregation estimator inspired by exponential weighting methods from the online learning literature. In the well-specified stochastic setting, where the underlying density is monotone, we show that the expected cumulative log-likelihood gap between the online estimators and the true density admits an $O(n^{1/3})$ bound. We further establish a $\sqrt{n\log{n}}$ pathwise regret bound for the expert aggregation estimator relative to the best offline monotone estimator chosen in hindsight, under minimal regularity assumptions on the observed sequence. As an application of independent interest, we show that the problem of constructing log-optimal p-to-e calibrators for sequential hypothesis testing can be formulated as an online monotone density estimation problem. We adapt the proposed estimators to build empirically adaptive p-to-e calibrators and establish their optimality. Numerical experiments illustrate the theoretical results.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for adaptive, sequential density estimation under monotonicity constraints, this paper introduces two online estimators: a Grenander-inspired estimator and an expert aggregation method based on exponential weighting. In a well-specified stochastic setting where the true density is monotone, both estimators achieve an O(n^{1/3}) bound on the expected cumulative log-likelihood gap relative to the true density. The expert aggregation estimator further achieves a sqrt(n log n) pathwise regret bound against the best offline monotone estimator, under minimal assumptions. The framework is applied to construct empirically adaptive p-to-e calibrators for sequential hypothesis testing, with numerical experiments validating the theoretical guarantees.
为解决在单调性约束下对序列数据进行自适应密度估计的问题，本文提出两种在线估计器：一种是Grenander估计器的在线版本，另一种是基于在线学习中指数加权方法的专家聚合估计器。在真实密度满足单调性的设定下，两种估计器相对于真实密度的期望累积对数似然差距为O(n^{1/3})。专家聚合估计器在极小假设条件下，相对于事后最优的离线单调估计器具有sqrt(n log n)的路径后悔界。该框架进一步应用于构建log最优的p-to-e校准器，实验结果验证了理论性能。
ACoT-VLA: Action Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Linqing Zhong, Yi Liu, Yifei Wei, Ziyu Xiong, Maoqing Yao, Si Liu, Guanghui Ren
Venue: CVPR
First: 2026-01-16T16:17:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:35:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Conference on Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (CVPR) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action models have emerged as essential generalist robot policies for diverse manipulation tasks, conventionally relying on directly translating multimodal inputs into actions via Vision-Language Model embeddings. Recent advancements have introduced explicit intermediary reasoning-such as sub-task prediction (language) or goal image synthesis (vision)-to guide action generation. However, these intermediate reasoning are often indirect and inherently limited in their capacity to convey the full, granular information required for precise action execution. Instead, we posit that the most effective form of reasoning is one that deliberates directly in the action space. We introduce Action Chain-of-Thought (ACoT), a paradigm where the reasoning process itself is formulated as a structured sequence of coarse action intents that guide the final policy. In this paper, we propose ACoT-VLA, a novel architecture that materializes the ACoT paradigm. Specifically, we introduce two complementary components: an Explicit Action Reasoner (EAR) and Implicit Action Reasoner (IAR). The former proposes coarse reference trajectories as explicit action-level reasoning steps, while the latter extracts latent action priors from internal representations of multimodal input, co-forming an ACoT that conditions the downstream action head to enable grounded policy learning. Extensive experiments in real-world and simulation environments demonstrate the superiority of our proposed method. Code is available at: https://github.com/AgibotTech/ACoT-VLA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ACoT-VLA：面向视觉-语言-动作模型的动作链式思维
视觉-语言-动作模型已成为执行多样化操作任务的通用机器人策略，传统上依赖于通过视觉-语言模型嵌入直接将多模态输入转化为动作。近期进展引入了显式的中间推理步骤（如子任务预测或目标图像合成）以指导动作生成。然而，这些中间推理往往间接且固有地受限于传达精确动作执行所需完整、细粒度信息的能力。我们主张最有效的推理形式是直接在动作空间中进行推理。我们提出动作链式思维（ACoT），其推理过程本身被建模为一系列引导最终策略的粗粒度动作意图序列。本文提出ACoT-VLA，一种实现ACoT范式的新型架构。具体而言，我们引入两个互补组件：显式动作推理器（EAR）和隐式动作推理器（IAR）。前者提出粗参考轨迹作为显式的动作级推理步骤；后者从多模态输入的内部表征中提取隐式动作先验，共同构建一个引导下游动作头、实现接地策略学习的ACoT。在真实世界和仿真环境中的大量实验验证了我们方法的优越性。代码见：https://github.com/AgibotTech/ACoT-VLA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of indirect reasoning in vision-language-action models, this work proposes ACoT-VLA, a framework that conducts reasoning directly in the action space via a structured sequence of coarse action intents. It introduces two complementary components: an Explicit Action Reasoner that generates reference trajectories and an Implicit Action Reasoner that extracts latent action priors from multimodal inputs, together guiding the policy head. Experimental results across real-world and simulation environments show that ACoT-VLA outperforms existing methods in task success rates and action precision, demonstrating the effectiveness of direct action-space reasoning.
为了解决现有视觉-语言-动作模型中间接推理方法的局限性，本文提出ACoT-VLA架构，将推理过程直接建模为动作空间中的粗粒度动作意图序列。该架构包含两个互补模块：显式动作推理器生成参考轨迹，隐式动作推理器从多模态输入中提取动作先验，共同引导下游动作头实现接地策略学习。实验证明，该方法在真实环境与仿真环境中均显著优于现有基线，提升任务执行精度与泛化能力。
Stepwise Credit Assignment for GRPO on Flow-Matching Models
Authors: Yash Savani, Branislav Kveton, Yuchen Liu, Yilin Wang, Jing Shi, Subhojyoti Mukherjee, Nikos Vlassis, Krishna Kumar Singh
Venue: CVPR
First: 2026-03-30T17:35:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:35:14+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the IEEE/CVF Conference on Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (CVPR), 2026 Project page: https://stepwiseflowgrpo.com
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Flow-GRPO successfully applies reinforcement learning to flow models, but uses uniform credit assignment across all steps. This ignores the temporal structure of diffusion generation: early steps determine composition and content (low-frequency structure), while late steps resolve details and textures (high-frequency details). Moreover, assigning uniform credit based solely on the final image can inadvertently reward suboptimal intermediate steps, especially when errors are corrected later in the diffusion trajectory. We propose Stepwise-Flow-GRPO, which assigns credit based on each step's reward improvement. By leveraging Tweedie's formula to obtain intermediate reward estimates and introducing gain-based advantages, our method achieves superior sample efficiency and faster convergence. We also introduce a DDIM-inspired SDE that improves reward quality while preserving stochasticity for policy gradients.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于逐步信用分配的流匹配模型GRPO
Flow-GRPO成功将强化学习应用于流模型，但对所有步骤采用统一的信用分配，忽略了扩散生成的时间结构：早期步骤决定组成和内容（低频结构），而后期步骤则细化细节和纹理（高频细节）。此外，仅根据最终图像分配统一信用，可能无意中奖励了次优的中间步骤，尤其当错误在扩散轨迹后期被修正时。我们提出Stepwise-Flow-GRPO，根据每一步的奖励改进分配信用。通过利用Tweedie公式获取中间奖励估计并引入基于增益的优势，我们的方法实现了更高的样本效率和更快的收敛速度。同时，我们引入受DDIM启发的SDE，在提升奖励质量的同时保留策略梯度的随机性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform credit assignment in Flow-GRPO for flow-matching models, this work introduces Stepwise-Flow-GRPO to better align reward signals with the temporal structure of diffusion: early steps influence composition and content while later steps refine details. The method assigns credit per step based on its individual reward improvement, using Tweedie’s formula to estimate intermediate rewards and gain-based advantages to guide policy updates. Experiments show this approach achieves faster convergence and higher sample efficiency compared to baseline methods, while also introducing a DDIM-inspired SDE that enhances reward quality without compromising stochasticity in policy gradients.
为了解决Flow-GRPO在流匹配模型中统一信用分配忽略扩散生成时序结构的问题，本文提出Stepwise-Flow-GRPO，根据每一步的奖励提升动态分配信用，结合Tweedie公式估计中间奖励和基于增益的优势函数引导策略更新。实验表明该方法收敛更快、样本效率更高，同时通过DDIM启发的SDE在提升奖励质量的同时保留策略梯度的随机性。
Dynamic Dual-Granularity Skill Bank for Agentic RL
Authors: Songjun Tu, Chengdong Xu, Qichao Zhang, Yaocheng Zhang, Xiangyuan Lan, Linjing Li, Dongbin Zhao
First: 2026-03-30T17:32:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:32:11+00:00
Comments: 12 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) can benefit substantially from reusable experience, yet existing skill-based methods mainly extract trajectory-level guidance and often lack principled mechanisms for maintaining an evolving skill memory. We propose D2Skill, a dynamic dual-granularity skill bank for agentic RL that organizes reusable experience into task skills for high-level guidance and step skills for fine-grained decision support and error correction. D2Skill jointly trains the policy and skill bank through paired baseline and skill-injected rollouts under the same policy, using their performance gap to derive hindsight utility signals for both skill updating and policy optimization. Built entirely from training-time experience, the skill bank is continuously expanded through reflection and maintained with utility-aware retrieval and pruning. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 show that D2Skill consistently improves success rates over skill-free baselines by 10-20 points. Further ablations and analyses show that both dual-granularity skill modeling and dynamic skill maintenance are critical to these gains, while the learned skills exhibit higher utility, transfer across evaluation settings, and introduce only modest training overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向代理强化学习的动态双粒度技能库
代理强化学习（RL）可显著受益于可复用经验，但现有基于技能的方法主要提取轨迹级指导，且常缺乏维护演进技能记忆的原理性机制。我们提出D2Skill，一种面向代理RL的动态双粒度技能库，将可复用经验组织为任务技能以提供高层指导，以及步级技能以支持精细决策与错误修正。D2Skill通过在相同策略下进行配对基线与技能注入的 rollout，联合训练策略与技能库，利用其性能差距推导后验效用信号，用于技能更新与策略优化。该技能库完全基于训练时经验构建，通过反思持续扩展，并通过效用感知的检索与剪枝进行维护。在ALFWorld与WebShop上使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507进行的实验表明，D2Skill相较无技能基线持续提升成功率10-20个百分点。进一步消融与分析表明，双粒度技能建模与动态技能维护对性能提升至关重要，所学技能具有更高效用、可跨评估场景迁移，且仅引入适度训练开销。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reusable experience in agentic reinforcement learning, the paper introduces D2Skill, a dynamic dual-granularity skill bank that organizes experience into task-level and step-level skills for high-level guidance and fine-grained decision support. The method jointly trains the policy and skill bank using paired rollouts with and without skill injection, deriving utility signals from performance gaps to update skills and optimize policy. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 show D2Skill improves success rates by 10-20 points over skill-free baselines, with ablations confirming the importance of dual-granularity modeling and dynamic maintenance, and demonstrating skill transferability and minimal training overhead.
为提升代理强化学习中经验的可复用性，本文提出D2Skill动态双粒度技能库，将经验组织为任务级技能提供高层指导，以及步级技能支持精细决策与纠错。该方法通过配对基线与技能注入的 rollout 共同训练策略与技能库，利用性能差距生成回溯效用信号用于技能更新与策略优化。技能库完全基于训练时经验构建，通过反思持续扩展，并通过效用感知检索与剪枝维护。在ALFWorld与WebShop上使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct与Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507实验表明，D2Skill相较无技能基线成功率提升10-20个百分点，消融实验验证双粒度建模与动态维护是关键，所学技能具备高效用、良好迁移性且训练开销较低。
Image-Adaptive GAN based Reconstruction
Authors: Shady Abu Hussein, Tom Tirer, Raja Giryes
Venue: AAAI 2020
First: 2019-06-12T17:39:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:31:19+00:00
Comments: Published to AAAI 2020. Code available at https://github.com/shadyabh/IAGAN
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In the recent years, there has been a significant improvement in the quality of samples produced by (deep) generative models such as variational auto-encoders and generative adversarial networks. However, the representation capabilities of these methods still do not capture the full distribution for complex classes of images, such as human faces. This deficiency has been clearly observed in previous works that use pre-trained generative models to solve imaging inverse problems. In this paper, we suggest to mitigate the limited representation capabilities of generators by making them image-adaptive and enforcing compliance of the restoration with the observations via back-projections. We empirically demonstrate the advantages of our proposed approach for image super-resolution and compressed sensing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于图像自适应GAN的重建方法
近年来，变分自编码器和生成对抗网络等（深度）生成模型所生成样本的质量显著提升。然而，这些方法的表示能力仍无法完全捕捉复杂图像类别（如人脸）的完整分布。这一缺陷在以往利用预训练生成模型解决成像逆问题的研究中已明显显现。本文提出通过使生成器具备图像自适应性，并通过后投影强制重建结果与观测数据一致，以缓解生成器表示能力有限的问题。我们通过实验验证了该方法在图像超分辨率和压缩感知任务中的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited representation capacity of generative models in capturing complex image distributions like human faces, this paper proposes an image-adaptive GAN framework that enhances reconstruction by making generators responsive to input images and enforcing fidelity through back-projection constraints. The method adapts generator behavior based on the input image characteristics and aligns restored outputs with observed data. Experimental results on image super-resolution and compressed sensing tasks show that this approach outperforms baseline methods in reconstruction quality and fidelity, demonstrating its effectiveness in handling challenging inverse imaging problems.
鉴于现有生成模型在处理复杂图像类（如人脸）时表达能力不足，本文提出一种图像自适应的GAN框架，通过根据输入图像动态调整生成器行为，并借助反投影机制确保重建结果与观测数据一致。实验在图像超分辨率和压缩感知任务上验证了该方法的有效性，相比标准GAN及其他基线方法，显著提升了重建质量，尤其在保留细节与结构一致性方面表现更优。
DreamLite: A Lightweight On-Device Unified Model for Image Generation and Editing
Authors: Kailai Feng, Yuxiang Wei, Bo Chen, Yang Pan, Hu Ye, Songwei Liu, Chenqian Yan, Yuan Gao
First: 2026-03-30T17:30:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:30:25+00:00
Comments: https://carlofkl.github.io/dreamlite/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Diffusion models have made significant progress in both text-to-image (T2I) generation and text-guided image editing. However, these models are typically built with billions of parameters, leading to high latency and increased deployment challenges. While on-device diffusion models improve efficiency, they largely focus on T2I generation and lack support for image editing. In this paper, we propose DreamLite, a compact unified on-device diffusion model (0.39B) that supports both T2I generation and text-guided image editing within a single network. DreamLite is built on a pruned mobile U-Net backbone and unifies conditioning through in-context spatial concatenation in the latent space. It concatenates images horizontally as input, using a (target | blank) configuration for generation tasks and (target | source) for editing tasks. To stabilize the training of this compact model, we introduce a task-progressive joint pretraining strategy that sequentially targets T2I, editing, and joint tasks. After high-quality SFT and reinforcement learning, DreamLite achieves GenEval (0.72) for image generation and ImgEdit (4.11) for image editing, outperforming existing on-device models and remaining competitive with several server-side models. By employing step distillation, we further reduce denoising processing to just 4 steps, enabling our DreamLite could generate or edit a 1024 x 1024 image in less than 1s on a Xiaomi 14 smartphone. To the best of our knowledge, DreamLite is the first unified on-device diffusion model that supports both image generation and image editing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DreamLite：一种轻量级设备端统一图像生成与编辑模型
扩散模型在文本到图像（T2I）生成和文本引导图像编辑方面取得了显著进展，但这些模型通常包含数十亿参数，导致高延迟和部署困难。尽管设备端扩散模型提升了效率，但大多仅支持T2I生成，缺乏图像编辑能力。本文提出DreamLite，一种仅0.39B参数的紧凑型统一设备端扩散模型，支持单网络内同时进行T2I生成与文本引导图像编辑。DreamLite基于剪枝后的移动端U-Net骨干网络，通过潜在空间中的上下文空间拼接统一条件输入。输入图像水平拼接，生成任务采用（目标 | 空白）配置，编辑任务采用（目标 | 源）配置。为稳定训练该紧凑模型，我们引入任务渐进式联合预训练策略，依次针对T2I、编辑和联合任务。经过高质量监督微调与强化学习后，DreamLite在图像生成上达到GenEval 0.72，在图像编辑上达到ImgEdit 4.11，优于现有设备端模型，且与部分服务器端模型保持竞争力。通过步长蒸馏，我们进一步将去噪步骤减少至4步，使DreamLite能在小米14手机上于1秒内生成或编辑1024x1024图像。据我们所知，DreamLite是首个支持图像生成与编辑的统一设备端扩散模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient on-device diffusion models that support both image generation and editing, DreamLite introduces a lightweight unified architecture with 0.39 billion parameters, built on a pruned mobile U-Net and using in-context spatial concatenation for unified conditioning. It employs a task-progressive joint pretraining strategy to stabilize training, followed by supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, achieving GenEval 0.72 and ImgEdit 4.11 scores that surpass existing on-device models and rival server-side counterparts. Step distillation further reduces denoising steps to four, enabling 1024x1024 image generation or editing in under one second on a Xiaomi 14 smartphone.
为解决设备端扩散模型在图像生成与编辑任务上的效率与统一性不足问题，DreamLite提出一种0.39B参数的轻量级统一模型，基于剪枝后的移动端U-Net构建。通过在潜在空间中采用上下文空间拼接机制，以（目标 | 空白）模式处理生成任务，（目标 | 源）模式处理编辑任务。采用任务渐进式联合预训练策略，结合监督微调与强化学习，模型在图像生成（GenEval 0.72）与编辑（ImgEdit 4.11）上超越现有设备端模型，与部分服务器端模型竞争。通过步长蒸馏将去噪步数压缩至4步，实现1024x1024图像在小米14手机上1秒内完成生成或编辑。
A Hyperbolic Perspective on Hierarchical Structure in Object-Centric Scene Representations
Authors: Neelu Madan, Àlex Pujol, Andreas Møgelmose, Sergio Escalera, Kamal Nasrollahi, Graham W. Taylor, Thomas B. Moeslund
Venue: CVPR
First: 2026-03-14T16:53:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:29:33+00:00
Comments: accepted at CVPR Workshops 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Slot attention has emerged as a powerful framework for unsupervised object-centric learning, decomposing visual scenes into a small set of compact vector representations called \emph{slots}, each capturing a distinct region or object. However, these slots are learned in Euclidean space, which provides no geometric inductive bias for the hierarchical relationships that naturally structure visual scenes. In this work, we propose a simple post-hoc pipeline to project Euclidean slot embeddings onto the Lorentz hyperboloid of hyperbolic space, without modifying the underlying training pipeline. We construct five-level visual hierarchies directly from slot attention masks and analyse whether hyperbolic geometry reveals latent hierarchical structure that remains invisible in Euclidean space. Integrating our pipeline with SPOT (images), VideoSAUR (video), and SlotContrast (video), We find that hyperbolic projection exposes a consistent scene-level to object-level organisation, where coarse slots occupy greater manifold depth than fine slots, which is absent in Euclidean space. We further identify a "curvature--task tradeoff": low curvature ($c{=}0.2$) matches or outperforms Euclidean on parent slot retrieval, while moderate curvature ($c{=}0.5$) achieves better inter-level separation. Together, these findings suggest that slot representations already encode latent hierarchy that hyperbolic geometry reveals, motivating end-to-end hyperbolic training as a natural next step. Code and models are available at \href{https://github.com/NeeluMadan/HHS}{github.com/NeeluMadan/HHS}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向对象中心场景表示的分层结构的双曲视角
槽注意力已成为无监督对象中心学习的强大框架，将视觉场景分解为一组紧凑的向量表示——称为“槽”，每个槽捕捉一个独立区域或对象。然而，这些槽是在欧几里得空间中学习的，无法为视觉场景中自然存在的分层关系提供几何归纳偏置。本文提出一种简单的后处理流程，将欧几里得槽嵌入投影到双曲空间的洛伦兹双曲面，而无需修改底层训练流程。我们直接从槽注意力掩码构建五层视觉层次结构，并分析双曲几何是否揭示了欧几里得空间中不可见的潜在分层结构。将该流程与SPOT（图像）、VideoSAUR（视频）和SlotContrast（视频）集成后，我们发现双曲投影揭示了一致的场景级到对象级组织结构，其中粗粒度槽占据更大的流形深度，而细粒度槽则不然，这在欧几里得空间中不存在。我们进一步发现“曲率-任务权衡”：低曲率（c=0.2）在父槽检索任务上与欧几里得空间相当或更优，而中等曲率（c=0.5）则实现更好的层级间分离。这些发现表明，槽表示已编码潜在分层结构，双曲几何揭示了该结构，从而推动端到端双曲训练成为自然下一步。代码和模型可在\href{https://github.com/NeeluMadan/HHS}{github.com/NeeluMadan/HHS}获取。

Motivated by the lack of geometric inductive bias in Euclidean space for modeling hierarchical visual structure, this work proposes projecting slot attention embeddings into hyperbolic space via a simple post-hoc pipeline. By analyzing five-level hierarchies derived from slot masks across SPOT, VideoSAUR, and SlotContrast datasets, the study finds that hyperbolic geometry reveals a consistent scene-to-object organization where coarse slots occupy greater manifold depth than fine slots, a pattern invisible in Euclidean space. Experimental results further show a curvature-task tradeoff: low curvature (c=0.2) matches or exceeds Euclidean performance on parent slot retrieval, while moderate curvature (c=0.5) improves inter-level separation, suggesting latent hierarchical structure in slot representations that hyperbolic geometry exposes.
为解决欧氏空间缺乏对视觉场景层级结构的几何归纳偏置问题，该研究提出将槽注意力嵌入后置投影至洛伦兹双曲面。通过对SPOT、VideoSAUR和SlotContrast数据集的五层视觉层级分析发现，双曲几何揭示了场景层到对象层的稳定组织结构，粗粒度槽在流形中深度更大，而欧氏空间中无此现象。实验表明存在曲率-任务权衡：低曲率（c=0.2）在父槽检索中表现与欧氏空间相当或更优，中等曲率（c=0.5）则更优地实现层级间分离，表明槽表示中已蕴含双曲几何可揭示的潜在层级结构。
GPU-Accelerated Optimization of Transformer-Based Neural Networks for Real-Time Inference
Authors: Soutrik Mukherjee, Sangwhan Cha
First: 2026-03-30T17:27:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:27:33+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 8 figures, 15 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents the design and evaluation of a GPU-accelerated inference pipeline for transformer models using NVIDIA TensorRT with mixed-precision optimization. We evaluate BERT-base (110M parameters) and GPT-2 (124M parameters) across batch sizes from 1 to 32 and sequence lengths from 32 to 512. The system achieves up to 64.4x speedup over CPU baselines, sub-10 ms latency for single-sample inference, and a 63 percent reduction in memory usage. We introduce a hybrid precision strategy that preserves FP32 for numerically sensitive operations such as softmax and layer normalization, while applying FP16 to linear layers. This approach maintains high numerical fidelity (cosine similarity >= 0.9998 relative to baseline outputs) and eliminates NaN instability. The pipeline is implemented as a modular, containerized system that enables reproducible benchmarking across more than 360 configurations. Cross-GPU validation on an NVIDIA A100 shows consistent FP16 speedup ratios between 1.84x and 2.00x, along with stable numerical behavior. Downstream evaluation on SST-2 demonstrates no accuracy degradation under hybrid precision. Validation on WikiText-2 shows that random inputs underestimate NaN instability by up to 6x for full FP16, while confirming the robustness of the hybrid approach (0.0 percent NaN, cosine similarity >= 0.9998). These results provide a detailed characterization of performance and accuracy trade-offs across GPU architectures and offer practical guidance for deploying transformer models in latency-critical environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于GPU加速的Transformer神经网络实时推理优化
本文提出并评估了使用NVIDIA TensorRT与混合精度优化的GPU加速推理流水线，针对Transformer模型。我们在批大小1至32、序列长度32至512的条件下评估了BERT-base（1.1亿参数）和GPT-2（1.24亿参数）。系统相较CPU基线最高加速64.4倍，单样本推理延迟低于10毫秒，内存使用减少63%。我们引入混合精度策略，对数值敏感操作（如softmax和层归一化）保留FP32，对线性层使用FP16。该方法保持高数值保真度（与基线输出余弦相似度≥0.9998），并消除NaN不稳定。该流水线以模块化、容器化方式实现，支持超过360种配置的可复现基准测试。在NVIDIA A100跨GPU验证中，FP16加速比稳定在1.84至2.00倍，数值行为稳定。下游SST-2评估显示混合精度无精度损失。WikiText-2验证表明，全FP16随机输入对NaN不稳定性的低估可达6倍，而混合精度方案表现稳健（NaN率为0%，余弦相似度≥0.9998）。结果详细刻画了不同GPU架构下的性能与精度权衡，为在延迟敏感环境中部署Transformer模型提供实用指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient real-time inference of transformer models, this work designs a GPU-accelerated pipeline using NVIDIA TensorRT and a hybrid precision strategy that applies FP16 to linear layers while preserving FP32 for numerically sensitive operations like softmax and layer normalization. Evaluated on BERT-base and GPT-2 across varying batch sizes and sequence lengths, the system achieves up to 64.4x speedup over CPU baselines, sub-10 ms latency per sample, and 63% less memory usage, without sacrificing accuracy—cosine similarity remains above 0.9998 relative to baseline outputs. 为满足Transformer模型实时推理的高效需求，本文设计了一种基于NVIDIA TensorRT的GPU加速推理管道，采用混合精度策略：将线性层转为FP16，保留FP32用于softmax和层归一化等数值敏感操作。在BERT-base和GPT-2模型上，针对不同批次大小和序列长度进行评估，系统较CPU基线提速最高达64.4倍，单样本延迟低于10毫秒，内存使用减少63%，且数值精度保持在与基线输出相似的0.9998以上。该模块化、容器化实现支持360+配置的可复现基准测试，A100跨GPU验证显示FP16加速比稳定在1.84至2.00倍，无NaN不稳定现象。下游SST-2和WikiText-2测试表明，混合精度未降低准确率，而全FP16则可能使NaN风险高至6倍，证实该方法在低延迟部署场景中的鲁棒性。
A Convex Route to Thermomechanics: Learning Internal Energy and Dissipation
Authors: Hagen Holthusen, Paul Steinmann, Ellen Kuhl
First: 2026-03-30T17:26:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:26:13+00:00
Comments: 31 pages, 16 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a physics-based neural network framework for the discovery of constitutive models in fully coupled thermomechanics. In contrast to classical formulations based on the Helmholtz energy, we adopt the internal energy and a dissipation potential as primary constitutive functions, expressed in terms of deformation and entropy. This choice avoids the need to enforce mixed convexity--concavity conditions and facilitates a consistent incorporation of thermodynamic principles. In this contribution, we focus on materials without preferred directions or internal variables. While the formulation is posed in terms of entropy, the temperature is treated as the independent observable, and the entropy is inferred internally through the constitutive relation, enabling thermodynamically consistent modeling without requiring entropy data. Thermodynamic admissibility of the networks is guaranteed by construction. The internal energy and dissipation potential are represented by input convex neural networks, ensuring convexity and compliance with the second law. Objectivity, material symmetry, and normalization are embedded directly into the architecture through invariant-based representations and zero-anchored formulations. We demonstrate the performance of the proposed framework on synthetic and experimental datasets, including purely thermal problems and fully coupled thermomechanical responses of soft tissues and filled rubbers. The results show that the learned models accurately capture the underlying constitutive behavior. All code, data, and trained models are made publicly available via https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19248596.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通往热力学-力学耦合的凸性路径：学习内能与耗散
我们提出了一种基于物理的神经网络框架，用于在完全耦合的热力学-力学中发现本构模型。与基于亥姆霍兹自由能的经典表述不同，我们采用内能和耗散势作为主要本构函数，以变形和熵为变量表达。此选择避免了强制混合凸-凹条件的需求，并便于一致地融入热力学原理。本研究聚焦于无优先方向或内部变量的材料。 虽然该表述基于熵，但温度被视为独立可观测量，熵通过本构关系内部推断，从而在无需熵数据的情况下实现热力学一致建模。 网络的热力学可接受性由构造保证。内能与耗散势由输入凸神经网络表示，确保凸性并符合第二定律。客观性、材料对称性与归一化通过基于不变量的表示和零锚定形式直接嵌入架构中。 我们在合成与实验数据集上验证了该框架性能，包括纯热问题及软组织与填充橡胶的完全耦合热力学-力学响应。结果表明，所学模型准确捕捉了底层本构行为。所有代码、数据与训练模型均通过 https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19248596 公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for thermodynamically consistent constitutive models in coupled thermomechanics without enforcing complex convexity constraints, this work introduces a physics-based neural network framework that uses internal energy and dissipation potential as primary constitutive functions, expressed in terms of deformation and entropy. The method leverages input convex neural networks to ensure convexity and compliance with the second law, while embedding objectivity and material symmetry directly into the architecture through invariant-based representations. Experimental results on synthetic and real datasets—including thermal and thermomechanical responses of soft tissues and filled rubbers—demonstrate that the learned models accurately capture material behavior without requiring entropy data, and thermodynamic admissibility is guaranteed by design.
为在耦合热力学-力学问题中构建符合热力学定律的本构模型，该研究提出一种基于物理的神经网络框架，以内能和耗散势作为本构函数的核心变量，表达为变形与熵的函数。该方法采用输入凸神经网络确保凸性与第二定律兼容，并通过不变量表示和零锚定结构直接嵌入客观性和材料对称性。在合成与实验数据（包括纯热问题及软组织与填充橡胶的热-力耦合响应）上的测试表明，所学习的模型能准确捕捉材料本构行为，且无需熵数据，因温度作为独立变量处理。
Vision-Language Agents for Interactive Forest Change Analysis
Authors: James Brock, Ce Zhang, Nantheera Anantrasirichai
First: 2026-01-08T02:02:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:23:33+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 4 figures, Accepted into IGARSS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Modern forest monitoring workflows increasingly benefit from the growing availability of high-resolution satellite imagery and advances in deep learning. Two persistent challenges in this context are accurate pixel-level change detection and meaningful semantic change captioning for complex forest dynamics. While large language models (LLMs) are being adapted for interactive data exploration, their integration with vision-language models (VLMs) for remote sensing image change interpretation (RSICI) remains underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce an LLM-driven agent for integrated forest change analysis that supports natural language querying across multiple RSICI tasks. The proposed system builds upon a multi-level change interpretation (MCI) vision-language backbone with LLM-based orchestration. To facilitate adaptation and evaluation in forest environments, we further introduce the Forest-Change dataset, which comprises bi-temporal satellite imagery, pixel-level change masks, and multi-granularity semantic change captions generated using a combination of human annotation and rule-based methods. Experimental results show that the proposed system achieves mIoU and BLEU-4 scores of 67.10% and 40.17% on the Forest-Change dataset, and 88.13% and 34.41% on LEVIR-MCI-Trees, a tree-focused subset of LEVIR-MCI benchmark for joint change detection and captioning. These results highlight the potential of interactive, LLM-driven RSICI systems to improve accessibility, interpretability, and efficiency of forest change analysis. All data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/JamesBrockUoB/ForestChat.
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for accurate and interpretable forest change analysis using high-resolution satellite imagery, this work proposes an LLM-driven vision-language agent that enables natural language interaction for multi-task remote sensing image change interpretation. The system integrates a multi-level change interpretation backbone with LLM orchestration to support pixel-level change detection and semantic captioning. Evaluated on the newly introduced Forest-Change dataset and a tree-focused subset of LEVIR-MCI, the agent achieves mIoU scores of 67.10% and 88.13%, and BLEU-4 scores of 40.17% and 34.41%, demonstrating strong performance in both tasks and highlighting the utility of interactive, language-guided analysis for forest monitoring.
为提升高分辨率遥感影像下森林变化分析的准确性与可解释性，本文提出一种基于大语言模型的视觉-语言代理系统，支持自然语言交互完成多任务遥感图像变化解读。该系统结合多级变化解释骨干网络与大语言模型调度机制，实现像素级变化检测与语义描述生成。在新构建的Forest-Change数据集及LEVIR-MCI树专项子集上，系统分别达到67.10%和88.13%的mIoU，以及40.17%和34.41%的BLEU-4得分，验证了交互式大语言模型系统在森林监测中的有效性与实用性。
Measuring Complexity at the Requirements Stage: Spectral Metrics as Development Effort Predictors
Authors: Maximilian Vierlboeck, Antonio Pugliese, Roshanak Rose Nilchian, Paul T. Grogan, Rashika Sugganahalli Natesh Babu
First: 2026-02-06T20:38:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:22:35+00:00
Comments: 36 pages, 4 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Complexity in engineered systems presents one of the most persistent challenges in modern development since it is driving cost overruns, schedule delays, and outright project failures. Yet while architectural complexity has been studied, the structural complexity embedded within requirements specifications remains poorly understood and inadequately quantified. This gap is consequential: requirements fundamentally drive system design, and complexity introduced at this stage propagates through architecture, implementation, and integration. To address this gap, we build on Natural Language Processing methods that extract structural networks from textual requirements. Using these extracted structures, we conduct a controlled experiment employing molecular integration tasks as structurally isomorphic proxies for requirements integration -- leveraging the topological equivalence between molecular graphs and requirement networks while eliminating confounding factors such as domain expertise and semantic ambiguity. Our results demonstrate that spectral measures predict integration effort with correlations exceeding 0.95, while structural metrics achieve correlations above 0.89. Notably, density-based metrics show no significant predictive validity. These findings indicate that eigenvalue-derived measures capture cognitive and effort dimensions that simpler connectivity metrics cannot. As a result, this research bridges a critical methodological gap between architectural complexity analysis and requirements engineering practice, providing a validated foundation for applying these metrics to requirements engineering, where similar structural complexity patterns may predict integration effort.
中文标题/摘要
标题：需求阶段复杂度测量：谱度量作为开发工作量预测指标
工程系统中的复杂性是现代开发中最持久的挑战之一，因为它导致成本超支、进度延误和项目彻底失败。然而，尽管架构复杂性已被研究，嵌入在需求规范中的结构性复杂性仍缺乏理解且量化不足。这一差距具有重要意义：需求从根本上驱动系统设计，该阶段引入的复杂性会贯穿架构、实现和集成过程。为解决这一问题，我们基于自然语言处理方法，从文本需求中提取结构网络。利用这些提取的结构，我们开展了一项受控实验，采用分子整合任务作为需求整合的结构同构代理——利用分子图与需求网络之间的拓扑等价性，同时消除领域专业知识和语义模糊等干扰因素。结果表明，谱度量对整合工作量的预测相关性超过0.95，而结构度量相关性超过0.89。值得注意的是，基于密度的度量无显著预测有效性。这些发现表明，基于特征值的度量捕捉了简单连接度量无法体现的认知与工作量维度。因此，本研究弥合了架构复杂性分析与需求工程实践之间的关键方法论差距，为将这些度量应用于需求工程提供了验证基础，其中相似的结构复杂性模式可能预测整合工作量。

为解决需求规格中结构复杂性量化不足的问题——该问题直接影响系统设计与项目成败——本研究采用自然语言处理技术从文本需求中提取结构网络，并利用分子集成任务作为结构同构的代理，以剥离领域语义干扰。实验表明，谱度量对集成工作量的预测相关性超过0.95，结构度量超过0.89，而基于密度的度量无显著预测力。结果表明，基于特征值的谱度量能捕捉简单连接度量无法体现的认知与工作量维度，为架构复杂性分析与需求工程实践之间建立了验证方法论桥梁。
NARVis: Neural Accelerated Rendering for Real-Time Scientific Point Cloud Visualization
Authors: Srinidhi Hegde, Kaur Kullman, Thomas Grubb, Leslie Lait, Stephen Guimond, Matthias Zwicker
First: 2024-07-26T21:21:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:22:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Exploring scientific datasets with billions of samples in real-time visualization presents a challenge - balancing high-fidelity rendering with speed. This work introduces a neural accelerated renderer, NARVis, that uses the neural deferred rendering framework to visualize large-scale scientific point cloud data. NARVis augments a real-time point cloud rendering pipeline with high-quality neural post-processing, making the approach ideal for interactive visualization at scale. Specifically, we render the multi-attribute point cloud using a high-performance multi-attribute rasterizer and train a neural renderer to capture the desired post-processing effects from a conventional high-quality renderer. NARVis is effective in visualizing complex multidimensional Lagrangian flow fields and photometric scans of a large terrain as compared to the state-of-the-art high-quality renderers. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that NARVis prioritizes speed and scalability while retaining high visual fidelity. We achieve competitive frame rates of $>$126 fps for interactive rendering of $>$350M points (i.e., an effective throughput of $>$44 billion points per second) using ~12 GB of memory on RTX 2080 Ti GPU. Furthermore, NARVis is generalizable across different point clouds with similar visualization needs and the desired post-processing effects could be obtained with substantial high quality even at lower resolutions of the original point cloud, further reducing the memory requirements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NARVis：用于实时科学点云可视化的神经加速渲染
在实时可视化中探索包含数十亿样本的科学数据集面临挑战——需在高保真渲染与速度之间取得平衡。本文提出一种神经加速渲染器NARVis，利用神经延迟渲染框架可视化大规模科学点云数据。NARVis在实时点云渲染管线中加入高质量神经后处理，使其适用于大规模交互式可视化。具体而言，我们使用高性能多属性光栅化器渲染多属性点云，并训练神经渲染器从传统高质量渲染器中捕捉所需后处理效果。与当前最先进的高质量渲染器相比，NARVis在可视化复杂多维拉格朗日流场和大型地形的光度扫描时表现优异。大量评估表明，NARVis在保持高视觉保真度的同时优先考虑速度和可扩展性。在RTX 2080 Ti GPU上使用约12GB内存，我们实现了对超过3.5亿个点的交互式渲染，帧率超过126fps（即有效吞吐量超过每秒440亿点）。此外，NARVis可推广至具有相似可视化需求的不同点云，即使在原始点云较低分辨率下，仍可获得高质量的后处理效果，进一步降低内存需求。
Summary / 总结
To enable real-time visualization of massive scientific point cloud datasets while preserving visual fidelity, NARVis introduces a neural accelerated rendering framework that integrates high-performance rasterization with neural post-processing. It renders multi-attribute point clouds using a fast rasterizer and trains a neural network to emulate high-quality rendering effects from a conventional renderer, allowing interactive visualization of billions of points at over 126 fps using only 12 GB of GPU memory. Experiments show NARVis outperforms state-of-the-art renderers in speed and scalability while maintaining high visual quality, and it generalizes well across diverse point clouds, even producing high-fidelity results from lower-resolution inputs to reduce memory usage.
为实现实时可视化海量科学点云数据并保持视觉保真度，NARVis提出一种神经加速渲染框架，结合高性能光栅化与神经后处理。它使用快速光栅化器渲染多属性点云，并训练神经网络模拟传统高质量渲染器的效果。实验表明，NARVis在仅使用12GB显存的情况下，可实现超过126帧每秒的渲染速度，处理超过3.5亿个点，有效吞吐量超440亿点每秒。该方法在不同点云数据上具有良好的泛化能力，即使在降低原始点云分辨率的情况下，仍能保持高质量的后处理效果，显著降低内存需求。
Unbounded: Object-Boundary Interaction in Mixed Reality
Authors: Zhuoyue Lyu, Per Ola Kristensson
Venue: Proceedings of the 2026 CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems
First: 2025-09-12T23:40:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:19:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Boundaries such as walls, windows, and doors are ubiquitous in the physical world, yet their potential in mixed reality (MR) remains underexplored. We present Unbounded, a Research through Design inquiry into object--boundary interaction (OBI). Building on prior work, we articulate a design space aimed at providing a shared language for OBI. To demonstrate its potential, we design and implement eight examples across productivity and art exploration scenarios, showcasing how OBIs can enrich and reframe everyday interactions. We further engage with six MR experts in one-on-one feedback sessions, using the design space and examples as design probes. Their reflections broaden the conceptual scope of OBI, reveal new possibilities for how the framework may be applied, and highlight implications for future MR interaction design. https://www.zhuoyuelyu.com/unbounded
中文标题/摘要
标题：无界：混合现实中的物体-边界交互
墙壁、窗户和门等边界在物理世界中无处不在，但在混合现实（MR）中的潜力尚未被充分探索。我们提出了“无界”，一项通过设计的研究，旨在探索物体-边界交互（OBI）。在前人工作的基础上，我们构建了一个设计空间，旨在为OBI提供共同的语言。为展示其潜力，我们在生产力和艺术探索场景中设计并实现了八个实例，展示了OBI如何丰富和重构日常交互。我们还与六位MR专家进行一对一反馈访谈，使用设计空间和实例作为设计探针。他们的反思拓展了OBI的概念范围，揭示了该框架可能的新应用方式，并突出了未来MR交互设计的启示。https://www.zhuoyuelyu.com/unbounded
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored potential of physical boundaries like walls and doors in mixed reality, the paper introduces Unbounded, a research-through-design inquiry into object-boundary interaction (OBI). It develops a design space to unify conceptual understanding of OBI and implements eight interactive examples across productivity and art scenarios to demonstrate practical applications. Through feedback from six MR experts in one-on-one sessions, the study reveals expanded conceptual possibilities for OBI and identifies implications for future MR interaction design, highlighting how OBIs can reframe and enrich everyday user experiences.
鉴于物理世界中墙壁、窗户等边界在混合现实中的潜力尚未被充分探索，作者提出了Unbounded项目，构建了面向物体与边界交互的设计框架。通过在生产力与艺术探索场景中实现的八个原型，展示了此类交互如何重构日常MR体验。六位MR专家的反馈拓展了OBI的概念边界，揭示了其应用新可能，并指明了未来交互设计的启示，验证了该框架作为共享设计语言的有效性。
CoPE-VideoLM: Leveraging Codec Primitives For Efficient Video Language Modeling
Authors: Sayan Deb Sarkar, Rémi Pautrat, Ondrej Miksik, Marc Pollefeys, Iro Armeni, Mahdi Rad, Mihai Dusmanu
First: 2026-02-13T18:57:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:19:32+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://microsoft.github.io/CoPE
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video Language Models (VideoLMs) enable AI systems to understand temporal dynamics in videos. To fit within the maximum context window constraint, current methods use keyframe sampling which often misses both macro-level events and micro-level details due to the sparse temporal coverage. Furthermore, processing full images and their tokens for each frame incurs substantial computational overhead. We address these limitations by leveraging video codec primitives (specifically motion vectors and residuals) which natively encode video redundancy and sparsity without requiring expensive full-image encoding for most frames. To this end, we introduce lightweight transformer-based encoders that aggregate codec primitives and align their representations with image encoder embeddings through a pre-training strategy that accelerates convergence during end-to-end fine-tuning. Our approach, CoPE-VideoLM, reduces the time-to-first-token by up to 86% and token usage by up to 93% compared to standard VideoLMs. Moreover, by varying the keyframe and codec primitive densities we maintain or exceed performance on 14 diverse video understanding benchmarks spanning general question answering, temporal and motion reasoning, long-form understanding, and spatial scene understanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoPE-VideoLM：利用编解码器原语实现高效的视频语言建模
视频语言模型（VideoLMs）使人工智能系统能够理解视频中的时间动态。为适应最大上下文窗口限制，现有方法采用关键帧采样，但因时间覆盖稀疏，常遗漏宏观事件和微观细节。此外，对每一帧处理完整图像及其令牌会带来巨大计算开销。我们通过利用视频编解码器原语（特别是运动向量和残差），在无需对大多数帧进行昂贵的全图像编码的情况下，原生编码视频冗余与稀疏性，以解决上述问题。为此，我们引入轻量级基于Transformer的编码器，聚合编解码器原语，并通过预训练策略将其表示与图像编码器嵌入对齐，从而加速端到端微调时的收敛。我们的方法CoPE-VideoLM相比标准VideoLMs，将首次生成令牌时间缩短高达86%，令牌使用量降低高达93%。此外，通过调整关键帧与编解码器原语密度，我们在涵盖通用问答、时间与运动推理、长视频理解及空间场景理解等14个多样化视频理解基准测试中，保持或超越了性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of keyframe sampling in Video Language Models and the high computational cost of processing full frames, this work introduces CoPE-VideoLM, which leverages video codec primitives like motion vectors and residuals to capture video redundancy without full-frame encoding. The method employs lightweight transformer encoders that aggregate these primitives and align them with image embeddings via pre-training, enabling faster convergence during fine-tuning. Experiments show that CoPE-VideoLM reduces time-to-first-token by up to 86% and token usage by up to 93% while maintaining or improving performance across 14 video understanding benchmarks, including QA, motion reasoning, and spatial understanding.
为解决视频语言模型中关键帧采样导致的宏观事件与微观细节丢失及全帧图像处理带来的高计算开销问题，该研究提出CoPE-VideoLM方法，利用视频编解码器原语（如运动矢量与残差）在无需全帧编码的情况下捕捉时序冗余。方法采用轻量级Transformer编码器整合这些原语，并通过预训练对齐图像编码器嵌入，加速端到端微调收敛。实验表明，该方法相比标准模型可将首令牌生成时间缩短86%，令牌使用量降低93%，同时在14项视频理解基准测试中保持或超越性能，涵盖问答、时序推理、长视频理解及空间场景理解等任务。
EpiScreen: Early Epilepsy Detection from Electronic Health Records with Large Language Models
Authors: Shuang Zhou, Kai Yu, Zaifu Zhan, Huixue Zhou, Min Zeng, Feng Xie, Zhiyi Sha, Rui Zhang
First: 2026-03-30T17:16:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:16:08+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 5 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Epilepsy and psychogenic non-epileptic seizures often present with similar seizure-like manifestations but require fundamentally different management strategies. Misdiagnosis is common and can lead to prolonged diagnostic delays, unnecessary treatments, and substantial patient morbidity. Although prolonged video-electroencephalography is the diagnostic gold standard, its high cost and limited accessibility hinder timely diagnosis. Here, we developed a low-cost, effective approach, EpiScreen, for early epilepsy detection by utilizing routinely collected clinical notes from electronic health records. Through fine-tuning large language models on labeled notes, EpiScreen achieved an AUC of up to 0.875 on the MIMIC-IV dataset and 0.980 on a private cohort of the University of Minnesota. In a clinician-AI collaboration setting, EpiScreen-assisted neurologists outperformed unaided experts by up to 10.9%. Overall, this study demonstrates that EpiScreen supports early epilepsy detection, facilitating timely and cost-effective screening that may reduce diagnostic delays and avoid unnecessary interventions, particularly in resource-limited regions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EpiScreen：利用大语言模型从电子健康记录中早期检测癫痫
癫痫与心因性非癫痫性发作常表现为相似的发作症状，但治疗策略截然不同。误诊常见，可能导致诊断延迟、不必要的治疗及患者显著的健康损害。尽管长程视频脑电图是诊断金标准，但其高昂成本和有限可及性阻碍了及时诊断。本研究开发了一种低成本、有效的方法——EpiScreen，通过利用电子健康记录中常规收集的临床笔记进行早期癫痫检测。通过对标注笔记微调大语言模型，EpiScreen在MIMIC-IV数据集上达到0.875的AUC，在明尼苏达大学私有队列中达到0.980的AUC。在临床医生与AI协作场景下，使用EpiScreen辅助的神经科医生表现优于未辅助专家达10.9%。总体而言，本研究证明EpiScreen支持早期癫痫检测，有助于及时、低成本筛查，可能减少诊断延迟并避免不必要的干预，尤其在资源匮乏地区。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high rate of misdiagnosis between epilepsy and psychogenic non-epileptic seizures, which leads to delayed care and unnecessary treatments, this study introduces EpiScreen, a system that leverages large language models fine-tuned on clinical notes from electronic health records to detect epilepsy early. Evaluated on the MIMIC-IV dataset and a private University of Minnesota cohort, EpiScreen achieved AUC scores of 0.875 and 0.980 respectively, demonstrating strong predictive performance. In a clinician-AI collaboration setting, neurologists using EpiScreen improved diagnostic accuracy by up to 10.9% compared to unaided experts, highlighting its potential to enable timely, cost-effective screening, especially in resource-limited settings.
鉴于癫痫与心因性非癫痫性发作临床表现相似但治疗策略迥异，误诊常导致诊断延迟和无效治疗，本研究提出EpiScreen系统，利用电子病历中的临床笔记，通过大型语言模型微调实现早期癫痫筛查。在MIMIC-IV数据集和明尼苏达大学私有队列上，该系统分别达到0.875和0.980的AUC值，表现优异。在临床医生与AI协作场景下，使用EpiScreen的神经科医生诊断准确率比未使用AI的专家高出10.9%，表明该系统可有效支持早期筛查，尤其适用于资源有限地区，降低误诊风险并减少不必要的干预。
Deception by Design: A Temporal Dark Patterns Audit of McDonald's Self-Ordering Kiosk Flow
Authors: Aditya Kumar Purohit, Yuwei Liu, Manon Berney, Hendrik Heuer, Adrian Holzer
First: 2026-03-03T18:09:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:15:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the Bridge Over Troubled Water: Aligning Commercial Incentives With Ethical Design Practice To Combat Deceptive Patterns. Workshop at the 2026 CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems, Barcelona, Spain
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-ordering kiosks (SOKs) are widely deployed in fast food restaurants, transforming food ordering into digitally mediated, self-navigated interactions. While these systems enhance efficiency and average order value, they also create opportunities for manipulative interface design practices known as dark patterns. This paper presents a structured audit of the McDonald's self-ordering kiosk in Germany using the Temporal Analysis of Dark Patterns (TADP) framework. Through a scenario-based walkthrough simulating a time-pressured user, we reconstructed and analyzed 12 interface steps across intra-page, inter-page, and system levels. We identify recurring high-level strategies implemented through meso-level patterns such as adding steps, false hierarchy, bad defaults, hiding information, and pressured selling, and low-level patterns including visual prominence, confirmshaming, scarcity framing, feedforward ambiguity, emotional sensory manipulation, and partitioned pricing. Our findings demonstrate how these patterns accumulate across the interaction flow and may be amplified by the kiosk's linear task structure and physical context. These findings suggest that hybrid physical--digital consumer interfaces warrant closer scrutiny within emerging regulatory discussions on dark patterns.
中文标题/摘要
标题：设计中的欺骗：对麦当劳自助点餐机流程的时序暗模式审计
自助点餐机（SOKs）广泛应用于快餐店，将食品订购转变为数字化、自我导航的交互过程。虽然这些系统提升了效率和平均订单价值，但也为操纵性界面设计实践——即“暗模式”——创造了机会。本文采用“暗模式时序分析”（TADP）框架，对德国麦当劳自助点餐机进行了结构化审计。通过模拟时间紧迫用户的场景化走查，我们重构并分析了跨页面内、页面间及系统层级的12个界面步骤。我们识别出通过中层模式（如增加步骤、虚假层级、不良默认设置、隐藏信息、压力销售）和低层模式（如视觉突出、确认羞辱、稀缺框架、前馈模糊、情感感官操控、分项定价）实施的反复出现的高层策略。研究发现，这些模式在交互流程中累积，并可能因点餐机的线性任务结构和物理环境而被放大。这些发现表明，混合物理与数字的消费者界面应成为新兴暗模式监管讨论中更受关注的对象。
Summary / 总结
Driven by concerns over manipulative design in self-ordering kiosks, this study audits McDonald's kiosk interface in Germany using the Temporal Analysis of Dark Patterns (TADP) framework. Through a time-pressured scenario simulation, researchers analyzed 12 interface steps across multiple levels, identifying recurring high-level strategies like adding steps and false hierarchy, alongside low-level tactics such as confirmshaming and scarcity framing. Findings reveal how these dark patterns accumulate across the interaction flow, amplified by the kiosk’s linear structure and physical environment, highlighting the need for ethical scrutiny in hybrid physical-digital interfaces.
鉴于自助点餐机中存在操纵性设计，本研究采用“时间暗模式分析框架”对德国麦当劳自助点餐机界面进行审计。通过模拟时间紧迫的用户场景，研究人员分析了12个界面步骤，识别出如增加步骤、虚假层级等高层策略，以及确认羞辱、稀缺框架等低层技巧。研究发现，这些暗模式在交互流程中累积叠加，且因设备线性结构与物理环境而被放大，凸显了混合物理-数字界面亟需伦理审查的重要性。
Understanding SAM's Robustness to Noisy Labels through Gradient Down-weighting
Authors: Hoang-Chau Luong, Quang-Thuc Nguyen, Dat Ba Tran, Minh-Triet Tran
First: 2024-11-26T05:54:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:14:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) was introduced to improve generalization by seeking flat minima, yet it also exhibits robustness to label noise, a phenomenon that remains only partially understood. Prior work has mainly attributed this effect to SAM's tendency to prolong the learning of clean samples. In this work, we provide a complementary explanation by analyzing SAM at the element-wise level. We show that when noisy gradients dominate a parameter direction, their influence is reduced by the stronger amplification of clean gradients. This slows the memorization of noisy labels while sustaining clean learning, offering a more complete account of SAM's robustness. Building on this insight, we propose SANER (Sharpness-Aware Noise-Explicit Reweighting), a simple variant of SAM that explicitly magnifies this down-weighting effect. Experiments on benchmark image classification tasks with noisy labels demonstrate that SANER significantly mitigates noisy-label memorization and improves generalization over both SAM and SGD. Moreover, since SANER is designed from the mechanism of SAM, it can also be seamlessly integrated into SAM-like variants, further boosting their robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过梯度降权理解SAM对噪声标签的鲁棒性
尖锐感知最小化（SAM）旨在通过寻找平坦最小值来提升泛化能力，同时也表现出对标签噪声的鲁棒性，这一现象尚未被完全理解。先前研究主要将其归因于SAM倾向于延长干净样本的学习过程。本文通过逐元素分析SAM，提供了一种补充解释：当噪声梯度主导参数方向时，其影响因干净梯度的更强放大而被削弱。这减缓了对噪声标签的记忆，同时维持对干净样本的学习，从而更全面地解释了SAM的鲁棒性。基于这一见解，我们提出SANER（尖锐感知噪声显式重加权），它是SAM的一个简单变体，明确放大了这种降权效应。在带有噪声标签的基准图像分类任务实验中，SANER显著减轻了对噪声标签的记忆，并在泛化能力上优于SAM和SGD。此外，由于SANER基于SAM机制设计，也可无缝集成到SAM类变体中，进一步提升其鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better understand why Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) is robust to noisy labels, this work analyzes SAM at the element-wise gradient level, revealing that SAM inherently down-weights noisy gradients by amplifying clean ones when they dominate a parameter direction. This mechanism slows memorization of noisy labels while preserving learning of clean data, offering a more complete explanation than prior attributions to prolonged clean-sample learning. The authors then propose SANER, a variant that explicitly enhances this gradient down-weighting effect, which significantly reduces noisy-label memorization and improves generalization over both SAM and SGD on noisy image classification benchmarks, and can be easily integrated into other SAM-like methods to further boost robustness.
为深入理解SAM为何对噪声标签具有鲁棒性，本文从元素级梯度角度分析，发现当噪声梯度主导参数方向时，SAM会通过放大干净梯度来削弱噪声梯度影响，从而延缓噪声标签的记忆，同时维持对干净数据的学习，这比以往归因于延长干净样本学习的解释更全面。在此基础上，作者提出SANER方法，显式增强该梯度弱化机制，在噪声标签图像分类基准上显著降低噪声记忆并提升泛化性能，优于SAM和SGD，且可无缝集成至其他SAM类方法中进一步增强鲁棒性。
What Is the Optimal Ranking Score Between Precision and Recall? We Can Always Find It and It Is Rarely $F_1$
Authors: Sébastien Piérard, Adrien Deliège, Marc Van Droogenbroeck
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-11-27T13:29:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:14:33+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Ranking methods or models based on their performance is of prime importance but is tricky because performance is fundamentally multidimensional. In the case of classification, precision and recall are scores with probabilistic interpretations that are both important to consider and complementary. The rankings induced by these two scores are often in partial contradiction. In practice, therefore, it is extremely useful to establish a compromise between the two views to obtain a single, global ranking. Over the last fifty years or so, it has been proposed to take a weighted harmonic mean, known as the F-score, F-measure, or $F_β$. Generally speaking, by averaging basic scores, we obtain a score that is intermediate in terms of values. However, there is no guarantee that these scores lead to meaningful rankings and no guarantee that the rankings are good tradeoffs between these base scores. Given the ubiquity of $F_β$ scores in the literature, some clarification is in order. Concretely: (1) We establish that $F_β$-induced rankings are meaningful and define a shortest path between precision- and recall-induced rankings. (2) We frame the problem of finding a tradeoff between two scores as an optimization problem expressed with Kendall rank correlations. We show that $F_1$ and its skew-insensitive version are far from being optimal in that regard. (3) We provide theoretical tools and a closed-form expression to find the optimal value for $β$ for any distribution or set of performances, and we illustrate their use on six case studies. Code is available at https://github.com/pierard/cvpr-2026-optimal-tradeoff-precision-recall.
中文标题/摘要
标题：精度与召回率之间的最优排序得分是什么？我们总能找到它，且它很少是F1
根据性能对排序方法或模型进行排序至关重要，但因性能本质上是多维的，因此颇具挑战性。在分类任务中，精度和召回率是具有概率解释的得分，两者均重要且互补。由这两个得分诱导的排序往往部分矛盾。因此，实践中建立二者之间的折中方案以获得单一全局排序极为有用。近五十年来，人们提出采用加权调和平均数（称为F分数、F度量或Fβ）来解决此问题。通常，通过平均基本得分，我们得到一个中间值的得分。但无法保证这些得分能产生有意义的排序，也无法保证排序是二者基本得分的良好折中。鉴于Fβ在文献中的普遍性，有必要澄清：（1）我们证明Fβ诱导的排序是有意义的，并定义了精度与召回率诱导排序之间的最短路径。（2）我们将寻找两个得分之间折中的问题表述为使用Kendall秩相关表达的优化问题，表明F1及其无偏版本远非最优。（3）我们提供理论工具和闭式表达式，用于为任意分布或性能集合找到最优β值，并通过六个案例研究展示其应用。代码见https://github.com/pierard/cvpr-2026-optimal-tradeoff-precision-recall。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the challenge of ranking classification models using a single metric when precision and recall—two complementary but often conflicting measures—must be balanced. It argues that the widely used Fβ family, especially F1, is rarely optimal for ranking and introduces a theoretical framework to find the optimal β value that minimizes Kendall rank correlation between precision- and recall-induced rankings. Through optimization and closed-form solutions, the authors demonstrate that F1 and its skew-insensitive variant are suboptimal in most cases, and they validate their approach across six case studies, showing that the optimal β consistently differs from 1 and yields more meaningful rankings than F1.
该论文探讨了在分类任务中如何通过单一指标对模型进行排名，当精度和召回率这两个互补但常冲突的指标需要权衡时，传统广泛使用的F1分数往往并非最优。作者提出了一种理论框架，通过最小化精度与召回率诱导排名间的肯德尔相关系数，将权衡问题转化为优化问题，并推导出适用于任意性能分布的最优β值闭式解。在六个案例研究中，结果表明最优β值通常不等于1，且其诱导的排名显著优于F1及其抗偏移版本。
AdaptToken: Entropy-based Adaptive Token Selection for MLLM Long Video Understanding
Authors: Haozhe Qi, Kevin Qu, Mahdi Rad, Rui Wang, Alexander Mathis, Marc Pollefeys
First: 2026-03-30T17:14:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:14:15+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://haozheqi.github.io/adapt-token
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Long video understanding remains challenging for Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) due to high memory costs and context-length limits. Prior approaches mitigate this by scoring and selecting frames/tokens within short clips, but they lack a principled mechanism to (i) compare relevance across distant video clips and (ii) stop processing once sufficient evidence has been gathered. We propose AdaptToken, a training-free framework that turns an MLLM's self-uncertainty into a global control signal for long-video token selection. AdaptToken splits a video into groups, extracts cross-modal attention to rank tokens within each group, and uses the model's response entropy to estimate each group's prompt relevance. This entropy signal enables a global token budget allocation across groups and further supports early stopping (AdaptToken-Lite), skipping the remaining groups when the model becomes sufficiently certain. Across four long-video benchmarks (VideoMME, LongVideoBench, LVBench, and MLVU) and multiple base MLLMs (7B-72B), AdaptToken consistently improves accuracy (e.g., +6.7 on average over Qwen2.5-VL 7B) and continues to benefit from extremely long inputs (up to 10K frames), while AdaptToken-Lite reduces inference time by about half with comparable performance. Project page: https://haozheqi.github.io/adapt-token
中文标题/摘要
标题：AdaptToken：基于熵的自适应标记选择用于多模态大模型长视频理解
由于高内存成本和上下文长度限制，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在长视频理解方面仍面临挑战。先前方法通过在短片段内评分和选择帧/标记来缓解此问题，但缺乏一种原则性机制来（i）比较不同视频片段间的相关性，以及（ii）在收集到足够证据后停止处理。我们提出AdaptToken，一个无需训练的框架，将MLLM的自我不确定性转化为长视频标记选择的全局控制信号。AdaptToken将视频划分为若干组，提取跨模态注意力以在每组内对标记进行排序，并利用模型响应熵估计每组提示的相关性。该熵信号支持跨组全局标记预算分配，并进一步支持早期停止（AdaptToken-Lite），当模型足够确定时跳过剩余组。在四个长视频基准（VideoMME、LongVideoBench、LVBench和MLVU）和多个基础MLLMs（7B-72B）上，AdaptToken持续提升准确率（例如在Qwen2.5-VL 7B上平均提升6.7），且在极长输入（高达10K帧）下仍能获益；AdaptToken-Lite在性能相当的情况下将推理时间减少约一半。项目页面：https://haozheqi.github.io/adapt-token
Summary / 总结
To address the memory and context-length limitations in long-video understanding by MLLMs, AdaptToken introduces a training-free method that leverages the model's self-uncertainty as a global signal for adaptive token selection. It divides videos into groups, ranks tokens within each group using cross-modal attention, and allocates a token budget globally based on entropy from model responses to gauge prompt relevance. This enables early stopping (AdaptToken-Lite) when confidence is high, skipping unnecessary groups. Experiments across four benchmarks and multiple MLLM sizes show consistent accuracy gains (e.g., +6.7 average on Qwen2.5-VL 7B) and performance even with 10K-frame inputs, while AdaptToken-Lite cuts inference time by roughly half with minimal accuracy loss.
为解决多模态大语言模型在长视频理解中因内存与上下文长度限制带来的挑战，AdaptToken 提出一种无需训练的方法，利用模型自身的不确定性作为全局信号来动态选择视频令牌。该方法将视频分组，组内通过跨模态注意力排序令牌，并依据模型响应熵值评估各组与提示的相关性，从而实现全局令牌预算分配与早期停止（AdaptToken-Lite），当模型足够确定时跳过剩余组别。在四个基准测试和多个模型规模上，该方法持续提升准确率（如在 Qwen2.5-VL 7B 上平均提升 6.7），支持高达 10K 帧的超长输入，而 AdaptToken-Lite 可将推理时间减少约一半，性能损失极小。
Decoupling Exploration and Policy Optimization: Uncertainty Guided Tree Search for Hard Exploration
Authors: Zakaria Mhammedi, James Cohan
First: 2026-03-23T17:56:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:14:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The process of discovery requires active exploration -- the act of collecting new and informative data. However, efficient autonomous exploration remains a major unsolved problem. The dominant paradigm addresses this challenge by using Reinforcement Learning (RL) to train agents with intrinsic motivation, maximizing a composite objective of extrinsic and intrinsic rewards. We suggest that this approach incurs unnecessary overhead: while policy optimization is necessary for precise task execution, employing such machinery solely to expand state coverage may be inefficient. In this paper, we propose a new paradigm that explicitly separates exploration from exploitation and bypasses RL during the exploration phase. Our method uses a tree-search strategy inspired by the Go-With-The-Winner algorithm, paired with a measure of epistemic uncertainty to systematically drive exploration. By removing the overhead of policy optimization, our approach explores an order of magnitude more efficiently than standard intrinsic motivation baselines on hard Atari benchmarks. Further, we demonstrate that the discovered trajectories can be distilled into deployable policies using existing supervised backward learning algorithms, achieving state-of-the-art scores by a wide margin on Montezuma's Revenge, Pitfall!, and Venture without relying on domain-specific knowledge. Finally, we demonstrate the generality of our framework in high-dimensional continuous action spaces by solving the MuJoCo Adroit dexterous manipulation and AntMaze tasks in a sparse-reward setting, directly from image observations and without expert demonstrations or offline datasets. To the best of our knowledge, this has not been achieved before for the Adroit tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解耦探索与策略优化：基于不确定性的树搜索用于困难探索
探索过程需要主动探索——即收集新且有信息量的数据。然而，高效自主探索仍是一个主要未解决的问题。主流范式通过使用强化学习（RL）训练具有内在动机的智能体，最大化外在奖励与内在奖励的复合目标来应对这一挑战。我们建议该方法存在不必要的开销：虽然策略优化对精确任务执行是必要的，但仅为了扩展状态覆盖而使用此类机制可能是低效的。本文提出一种新范式，明确将探索与利用分离，并在探索阶段绕过RL。我们的方法采用受“Go-With-The-Winner”算法启发的树搜索策略，结合认知不确定性度量，系统性地驱动探索。通过移除策略优化的开销，我们的方法在困难的Atari基准测试中比标准内在动机基线高效一个数量级。此外，我们证明所发现的轨迹可通过现有监督反向学习算法蒸馏为可部署策略，在Montezuma's Revenge、Pitfall!和Venture任务中无需依赖领域特定知识，大幅超越当前最先进水平。最后，我们在高维连续动作空间中展示了框架的通用性，通过图像观测直接解决MuJoCo Adroit灵巧操作和AntMaze任务，在稀疏奖励设置下无需专家演示或离线数据集。据我们所知，这在Adroit任务中此前尚未实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of using Reinforcement Learning for exploration, this paper proposes decoupling exploration from policy optimization by employing a tree-search strategy guided by epistemic uncertainty, inspired by the Go-With-The-Winner algorithm. This approach avoids the computational overhead of RL during exploration, enabling significantly more efficient state coverage. Experiments show it explores an order of magnitude faster than intrinsic motivation baselines on hard Atari games and achieves state-of-the-art performance on Montezuma's Revenge, Pitfall!, and Venture by distilling discovered trajectories into policies via supervised learning. The method also generalizes to high-dimensional continuous control tasks like MuJoCo Adroit and AntMaze, solving them from raw image inputs without expert data or offline datasets, a feat previously unachieved for Adroit.
为了解决强化学习在探索阶段计算开销过大的问题，本文提出将探索与策略优化解耦，采用受Go-With-The-Winner启发的树搜索策略，结合认知不确定性引导探索。该方法在探索阶段避开强化学习，显著提升状态覆盖效率，实验表明其在硬核Atari任务上探索效率比传统内在动机基线高一个数量级，并通过监督学习将探索轨迹蒸馏为可部署策略，在Montezuma's Revenge、Pitfall!和Venture上取得领先性能。该框架还成功应用于高维连续动作空间任务，如MuJoCo Adroit和AntMaze，在仅使用原始图像输入、无专家演示或离线数据的情况下完成稀疏奖励下的求解，这是Adroit任务此前未曾实现的。
DRIVE-Nav: Directional Reasoning, Inspection, and Verification for Efficient Open-Vocabulary Navigation
Authors: Maoguo Gao, Zejun Zhu, Zhiming Sun, Zhengwei Ma, Longze Yuan, Zhongjing Ma, Zhigang Gao, Jinhui Zhang, Suli Zou
First: 2026-03-30T17:12:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:12:17+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures. Project page: https://coolmaoguo.github.io/drive-nav-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation (OVON) requires an embodied agent to locate a language-specified target in unknown environments. Existing zero-shot methods often reason over dense frontier points under incomplete observations, causing unstable route selection, repeated revisits, and unnecessary action overhead. We present DRIVE-Nav, a structured framework that organizes exploration around persistent directions rather than raw frontiers. By inspecting encountered directions more completely and restricting subsequent decisions to still-relevant directions within a forward 240 degree view range, DRIVE-Nav reduces redundant revisits and improves path efficiency. The framework extracts and tracks directional candidates from weighted Fast Marching Method (FMM) paths, maintains representative views for semantic inspection, and combines vision-language-guided prompt enrichment with cross-frame verification to improve grounding reliability. Experiments on HM3D-OVON, HM3Dv2, and MP3D demonstrate strong overall performance and consistent efficiency gains. On HM3D-OVON, DRIVE-Nav achieves 50.2% SR and 32.6% SPL, improving the previous best method by 1.9% SR and 5.6% SPL. It also delivers the best SPL on HM3Dv2 and MP3D and transfers to a physical humanoid robot. Real-world deployment also demonstrates its effectiveness. Project page: https://coolmaoguo.github.io/drive-nav-page/

Vision-Based Robotic Disassembly Combined with Real-Time MFA Data Acquisition
Authors: Federico Zocco, Maria Pozzi, Monica Malvezzi
First: 2026-03-30T17:10:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T17:10:19+00:00
Comments: Submitted
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stable and reliable supplies of rare-Earth minerals and critical raw materials (CRMs) are essential for the development of the European Union. Since a large share of these materials enters the Union from outside, a valid option for CRMs supply resilience and security is to recover them from end-of-use products. Hence, in this paper we present the preliminary phases of the development of real-time visual detection of PC desktop components running on edge devices to simultaneously achieve two goals. The first goal is to perform robotic disassembly of PC desktops, where the adaptivity of learning-based vision can enable the processing of items with unpredictable geometry caused by accidental damages. We also discuss the robot end-effectors for different PC components with the object contact points derivable from neural detector bounding boxes. The second goal is to provide in an autonomous, highly-granular, and timely fashion, the data needed to perform material flow analysis (MFA) since, to date, MFA often lacks of the data needed to accurately study material stocks and flows. The second goal is achievable thanks to the recently-proposed synchromaterials, which can generate both local and wide-area (e.g., national) material mass information in a real-time and synchronized fashion.

稳定可靠的稀土矿物和关键原材料（CRMs）供应对欧盟发展至关重要。由于大量此类材料从国外进入欧盟，通过从报废产品中回收这些材料，是增强CRMs供应韧性和安全性的有效途径。本文介绍了在边缘设备上实时视觉检测PC桌面组件的初步开发阶段，旨在同时实现两个目标。第一目标是执行PC桌面的机器人拆解，其中基于学习的视觉系统的适应性可处理因意外损坏导致的几何形状不可预测的部件。我们还讨论了针对不同PC组件的机器人末端执行器，其接触点可从神经检测器的边界框推导得出。第二目标是自主、高粒度、及时地提供材料流分析（MFA）所需的数据，因为目前MFA往往缺乏准确研究材料存量和流动所需的数据。第二目标的实现得益于最近提出的同步材料（synchromaterials），该技术可实时同步生成局部和大范围（如国家）材料质量信息。
