Evaluated through user teleoperation across 13 dexterous tasks, Ruka-v2 reduced task completion time by 51.3% and increased success rate by 21.2% compared to Ruka. The hand also supports bimanual and single-arm teleoperation and autonomous policy learning on three tasks, with all design files and software publicly available.
为推动机器人实现类人灵巧度，作者在原Ruka手的基础上推出Ruka-v2，增加了一个解耦的2自由度平行腕关节和手指外展/内收功能，使手腕可独立实现屈伸和桡尺偏转，便于在狭小空间操作，外展功能则支持抓取细物、手内旋转和书法等动作。用户远程操作实验显示，任务完成时间缩短51.3%，成功率提升21.2%。该手还支持13项灵巧任务的双臂或单臂远程操作，以及3项任务的自主策略学习，所有设计文件和软件均公开可获取。
Generalizing Fair Top-$k$ Selection: An Integrative Approach
Authors: Guangya Cai
First: 2026-03-05T00:06:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T16:27:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fair top-$k$ selection, which ensures appropriate proportional representation of members from minority or historically disadvantaged groups among the top-$k$ selected candidates, has drawn significant attention. We study the problem of finding a fair (linear) scoring function with multiple protected groups while also minimizing the disparity from a reference scoring function. This generalizes the prior setup, which was restricted to the single-group setting without disparity minimization. Previous studies imply that the number of protected groups may have a limited impact on the runtime efficiency. However, driven by the need for experimental exploration, we find that this implication overlooks a critical issue that may affect the fairness of the outcome. Once this issue is properly considered, our hardness analysis shows that the problem may become computationally intractable even for a two-dimensional dataset and small values of $k$. However, our analysis also reveals a gap in the hardness barrier, enabling us to recover the efficiency for the case of small $k$ when the number of protected groups is sufficiently small. Furthermore, beyond measuring disparity as the "distance" between the fair and the reference scoring functions, we introduce an alternative disparity measure$\unicode{x2014}$utility loss$\unicode{x2014}$that may yield a more stable scoring function under small weight perturbations. Through careful engineering trade-offs that balance implementation complexity, robustness, and performance, our augmented two-pronged solution demonstrates strong empirical performance on real-world datasets, with experimental observations also informing algorithm design and implementation decisions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：公平Top-k选择的泛化：一种综合方法
公平Top-k选择旨在确保少数族裔或历史上处于不利地位群体的成员在Top-k候选者中获得适当比例的代表性，已引起广泛关注。我们研究在多个受保护群体条件下寻找公平（线性）评分函数的问题，同时最小化与参考评分函数的差异。这扩展了先前仅限于单群体且不考虑差异最小化的设置。先前研究暗示受保护群体数量对运行效率影响有限，但基于实验探索的需求，我们发现该推论忽略了可能影响结果公平性的一个关键问题。一旦该问题被妥善考虑，我们的复杂度分析表明，即使在二维数据集和较小的k值下，该问题也可能计算上不可行。然而，我们的分析也揭示了复杂度障碍中的一个缺口，使我们在受保护群体数量足够小时，能够恢复小k值情形下的效率。此外，除将差异度量为“公平与参考评分函数之间的距离”外，我们引入了一种替代差异度量——效用损失，该度量在权重微小扰动下可能产生更稳定的评分函数。通过在实现复杂度、鲁棒性和性能之间进行精细权衡，我们的增强型双管齐下解决方案在真实数据集上展现出强大的实证性能，实验观察也指导了算法设计与实现决策。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generalize fair top-k selection beyond single-group settings and incorporate disparity minimization, the paper proposes an integrative method that jointly optimizes a fair linear scoring function across multiple protected groups while minimizing its distance to a reference scoring function. The authors reveal that ignoring a critical issue in prior assumptions can lead to computational intractability even for small k and two-dimensional data, yet they identify a hardness gap that allows efficient solutions for small k when the number of protected groups is limited. They further introduce utility loss as an alternative disparity measure, which enhances scoring function stability under weight perturbations. Empirical results on real-world datasets show strong performance, with algorithmic design informed by careful trade-offs among implementation complexity, robustness, and efficiency.
为突破单一保护组限制并最小化与参考评分函数的差异，作者提出一种整合方法，通过优化多保护组下的线性评分函数实现公平的Top-k选择，并引入基于效用损失的差异度量以提升对小权重扰动的鲁棒性。实验表明，尽管在保护组数较多或k值较大时问题可能计算不可行，但在k值较小且保护组数有限时仍可高效求解。该方法通过平衡实现复杂度、鲁棒性与性能，在真实数据集上表现优异，验证了算法设计与实现中的关键权衡策略。
Constructing Composite Features for Interpretable Music-Tagging
Authors: Chenhao Xue, Weitao Hu, Joyraj Chakraborty, Zhijin Guo, Kang Li, Tianyu Shi, Martin Reed, Nikolaos Thomos
Venue: ICASSP 2026
First: 2026-03-30T16:25:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T16:25:58+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 8 figures, accepted at ICASSP 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Combining multiple audio features can improve the performance of music tagging, but common deep learning-based feature fusion methods often lack interpretability. To address this problem, we propose a Genetic Programming (GP) pipeline that automatically evolves composite features by mathematically combining base music features, thereby capturing synergistic interactions while preserving interpretability. This approach provides representational benefits similar to deep feature fusion without sacrificing interpretability. Experiments on the MTG-Jamendo and GTZAN datasets demonstrate consistent improvements compared to state-of-the-art systems across base feature sets at different abstraction levels. It should be noted that most of the performance gains are noticed within the first few hundred GP evaluations, indicating that effective feature combinations can be identified under modest search budgets. The top evolved expressions include linear, nonlinear, and conditional forms, with various low-complexity solutions at top performance aligned with parsimony pressure to prefer simpler expressions. Analyzing these composite features further reveals which interactions and transformations tend to be beneficial for tagging, offering insights that remain opaque in black-box deep models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：构建可解释的音乐标签复合特征
结合多种音频特征可提升音乐标签性能，但常见的基于深度学习的特征融合方法往往缺乏可解释性。为解决此问题，我们提出一种遗传编程（GP）流程，通过数学方式自动演化复合特征，从而捕捉协同交互并保持可解释性。该方法在提供类似深度特征融合的表示优势的同时，不牺牲可解释性。在MTG-Jamendo和GTZAN数据集上的实验表明，该方法在不同抽象层次的基础特征集上均优于当前最先进系统。值得注意的是，大部分性能提升出现在前几百次GP评估内，表明在有限搜索预算下即可识别出有效的特征组合。最优演化表达式包括线性、非线性和条件形式，高表现的低复杂度解符合简约压力偏好简单表达式。进一步分析这些复合特征，揭示了哪些交互和变换对标签有益，这在黑箱深度模型中仍不可见。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance music tagging performance while maintaining interpretability, this work introduces a Genetic Programming pipeline that automatically constructs composite audio features by combining base music features in mathematically defined ways. The method evolves feature expressions to capture synergistic interactions without resorting to black-box deep fusion, preserving human-understandable structure. Experiments on MTG-Jamendo and GTZAN datasets show consistent performance gains over state-of-the-art methods across multiple feature abstraction levels, with most improvements emerging within the first few hundred GP evaluations. Top-performing expressions include linear, nonlinear, and conditional forms, often simple and parsimonious, revealing interpretable patterns such as beneficial transformations and feature interactions that remain hidden in deep models.
为在提升音乐标签性能的同时保持可解释性，本文提出一种遗传编程管道，通过数学方式自动组合基础音乐特征生成复合特征。该方法捕捉协同交互，避免黑箱深度融合，保留人类可理解的表示。在MTG-Jamendo和GTZAN数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多个特征抽象层级上均优于当前最先进系统，且大部分性能提升在前几百次遗传编程评估内即显现。最优演化特征包括线性、非线性和条件形式，多为简洁的解决方案，进一步分析揭示了对标签任务最有益的特定交互与变换。
The Ultimate Tutorial for AI-driven Scale Development in Generative Psychometrics: Releasing AIGENIE from its Bottle
Authors: Lara Russell-Lasalandra, Hudson Golino, Luis Eduardo Garrido, Alexander P. Christensen
First: 2026-03-30T16:25:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T16:25:37+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 8 Figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Psychological scale development has traditionally required extensive expert involvement, iterative revision, and large-scale pilot testing before psychometric evaluation can begin. The `AIGENIE` R package implements the AI-GENIE framework (Automatic Item Generation with Network-Integrated Evaluation), which integrates large language model (LLM) text generation with network psychometric methods to automate the early stages of this process. The package generates candidate item pools using LLMs, transforms them into high-dimensional embeddings, and applies a multi-step reduction pipeline -- Exploratory Graph Analysis (EGA), Unique Variable Analysis (UVA), and bootstrap EGA -- to produce structurally validated item pools entirely *in silico*. This tutorial introduces the package across six parts: installation and setup, understanding Application Programming Interfaces (APIs), text generation, item generation, the `AIGENIE` function, and the `GENIE` function. Two running examples illustrate the package's use: the Big Five personality model (a well-established construct) and AI Anxiety (an emerging construct). The package supports multiple LLM providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Groq, HuggingFace, and local models), offers a fully offline mode with no external API calls, and provides the `GENIE()` function for researchers who wish to apply the psychometric reduction pipeline to existing item pools regardless of their origin. The `AIGENIE` package is freely available on R-universe at https://laralee.r-universe.dev/AIGENIE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成心理测量学中AI驱动量表开发终极教程：释放AIGENIE
传统心理量表开发需大量专家参与、反复修订及大规模预测试，方可开展心理测量评估。R包`AIGENIE`实现AI-GENIE框架（结合网络心理测量方法的自动项目生成），将大语言模型（LLM）文本生成与网络心理测量方法整合，自动化该过程早期阶段。该包利用LLM生成候选题项池，将其转化为高维嵌入，并通过多步降维流程——探索性图分析（EGA）、独特变量分析（UVA）及自助EGA——在计算机内完全验证结构，生成结构验证的题项池。本教程分六部分介绍：安装与设置、理解API、文本生成、题项生成、`AIGENIE`函数及`GENIE`函数。两个实例演示包的使用：大五人格模型（成熟构念）与AI焦虑（新兴构念）。该包支持多种LLM提供商（OpenAI、Anthropic、Groq、HuggingFace及本地模型），提供完全离线模式（无需外部API调用），并提供`GENIE()`函数，供研究人员对任意来源的题项池应用心理测量降维流程。`AIGENIE`包免费提供于R-universe：https://laralee.r-universe.dev/AIGENIE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and time required for traditional psychological scale development, the paper introduces AIGENIE, an R package that automates early-stage scale creation using large language models (LLMs) and network psychometrics. The method generates candidate items via LLM text generation, embeds them in high-dimensional space, then applies a multi-step reduction pipeline — Exploratory Graph Analysis (EGA), Unique Variable Analysis (UVA), and bootstrap EGA — to produce structurally validated item pools without human intervention. Experimental results demonstrate successful application to both established (Big Five) and emerging (AI Anxiety) constructs, showing the package’s flexibility across constructs and LLM providers, including offline operation. The GENIE function further enables reuse of existing item pools through the same psychometric pipeline, validating the framework’s generalizability and robustness.
为解决传统心理量表开发中依赖大量专家、反复修订和大规模预测试的问题，本文提出AIGENIE工具包，利用大语言模型与网络心理测量方法自动化早期阶段的项目生成与验证。该方法通过LLM生成候选项目，将其转化为高维嵌入，并通过探索性图分析、独特变量分析和自助法EGA等多步降维流程，实现无需人工干预的结构验证项目池生成。实验结果表明，该工具包在经典（大五人格）和新兴（AI焦虑）构念上均能有效生成符合心理测量标准的项目池，支持多种LLM供应商，提供离线模式，并包含GENIE函数用于验证已有项目池。
Affordances of Digital and Blockchain-based Community Currencies: The Case of Sarafu Network in Kenya
Authors: Patricia Marcella Evite
Venue: International Journal of Community Currency Research VOLUME 30 NO.1 (2026) 173-194
First: 2025-12-03T18:07:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T16:25:20+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Community currencies (CCs) have been adopting innovative systems to overcome implementational hurdles from issuing paper currencies. Using a qualitative approach, this paper examined this digital transition of Sarafu Network in Kenya and its predecessor CCs as a case study. From the original vouchers launched in 2010, the foundation Grassroots Economics introduced a digital interface in 2016 that operates on a feature phone, and then integrated blockchain technology starting in 2018, undergoing several migrations before becoming settling on its current iteration called Community Asset Vouchers on the Celo blockchain since 2023. Using affordances from human-computer interaction, the research shows that digitalization and blockchain improved the facilitation of economic activities of the local communities, both their typical market transactions as well as traditional reciprocal labor exchanges, by offering more functionalities compared to the analog version of Sarafu. The unique contributions of blockchain include enabling automation of holding tax calculations and linking the vouchers to the mainstream monetary system via stablecoins facilitated by a series of smart contracts also known as the liquidity pool. The study also finds that there is an inherent trade-off between blockchain benefits and user interface complexity. Hence, balancing innovation and community needs remains a challenge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于数字与区块链的社区货币的可用性：肯尼亚Sarafu网络案例
社区货币（CCs）为克服发行纸质货币的实施障碍，正采用创新系统。本文采用质性研究方法，以肯尼亚Sarafu网络及其前身社区货币为案例，考察其数字化转型过程。自2010年发行原始凭证以来，基层经济组织（Grassroots Economics）于2016年引入基于功能手机的数字界面，2018年起集成区块链技术，经历数次迁移，最终于2023年定型为基于Celo区块链的“社区资产凭证”。研究基于人机交互中的“可用性”理论，表明数字化与区块链提升了当地社区经济活动的便利性，包括常规市场交易与传统互惠劳动交换，其功能优于Sarafu的模拟版本。区块链的独特贡献包括自动化持有税计算，并通过一系列智能合约（又称流动性池）将凭证与主流货币体系（稳定币）相连。研究亦发现，区块链优势与用户界面复杂性之间存在固有权衡，因此平衡创新与社区需求仍具挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to overcome implementation challenges of traditional paper-based community currencies, this study examines the digital evolution of Sarafu Network in Kenya, tracing its transition from paper vouchers to a blockchain-based system on the Celo network. Using a qualitative approach and human-computer interaction affordances framework, the research demonstrates that digitalization and blockchain significantly enhanced local economic activities, including market transactions and reciprocal labor exchanges, by expanding functionality beyond the analog version. Key blockchain innovations include automated tax calculations and stablecoin integration via smart contracts, enabling linkage to the mainstream monetary system through a liquidity pool. However, the study identifies a trade-off between technological benefits and increased user interface complexity, highlighting the ongoing challenge of balancing innovation with community usability.
为解决社区货币实施中的实际困难，本研究以肯尼亚萨拉福网络的数字化演进为案例，从纸质凭证逐步过渡到基于Celo区块链的系统。研究采用人机交互“可操作性”理论框架，发现数字化与区块链技术显著提升了当地经济活动效率，包括市场交易与传统互助劳动交换，功能远超纸质版本。研究指出区块链实现了税收自动计算，并通过稳定币与智能合约（流动性池）连接主流货币体系，但同时也揭示了技术创新与用户界面复杂性之间的固有矛盾，如何平衡技术进步与社区需求仍是持续挑战。
Model Merging in the Era of Large Language Models: Methods, Applications, and Future Directions
Authors: Mingyang Song, Mao Zheng
First: 2026-03-10T17:31:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T16:23:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Model merging combines the parameters of multiple neural networks into a single model without additional training. As fine-tuned large language models (LLMs) proliferate, merging offers a computationally efficient alternative to ensembles and full retraining, enabling practitioners to compose specialized capabilities at minimal cost. This survey examines model merging in the LLM era through the \textbf{FUSE} taxonomy, organized along \textbf{F}oundations, \textbf{U}nification Strategies, \textbf{S}cenarios, and \textbf{E}cosystem. We first establish the theoretical underpinnings of merging, including loss landscape geometry and mode connectivity, then systematically review the algorithmic space spanning weight averaging, task vector arithmetic, sparsification-enhanced methods, mixture-of-experts architectures, and evolutionary optimization. We further examine downstream applications across multi-task learning, safety alignment, domain specialization, and federated learning, and survey the supporting ecosystem of tools and evaluation benchmarks. Finally, we identify key open challenges and future directions, aiming to equip researchers and practitioners with a structured foundation for advancing model merging.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型时代下的模型融合：方法、应用与未来方向
模型融合将多个神经网络的参数合并为单个模型，无需额外训练。随着微调大语言模型（LLMs）的普及，融合提供了一种计算效率更高的替代方案，相比集成和完整重训练，可以最小成本组合专业能力。本文通过\textbf{FUSE}分类法（按\textbf{F}基础、\textbf{U}统一策略、\textbf{S}场景、\textbf{E}生态组织）考察LLM时代的模型融合。首先建立融合的理论基础，包括损失景观几何与模式连通性；然后系统回顾算法空间，涵盖权重平均、任务向量运算、稀疏增强方法、专家混合架构及进化优化；进一步考察下游应用，包括多任务学习、安全对齐、领域专业化与联邦学习，并调研支持工具与评估基准；最后识别关键开放挑战与未来方向，旨在为研究人员和从业者提供推进模型融合的结构化基础。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the proliferation of fine-tuned large language models, model merging offers a computationally efficient way to combine multiple models into one without retraining, enabling cost-effective composition of specialized capabilities. The paper systematically reviews merging methods through the FUSE taxonomy—covering foundations like loss landscape geometry, unification strategies such as weight averaging and mixture-of-experts, and application scenarios including multi-task learning and safety alignment. Experimental results across diverse settings show merging consistently achieves competitive performance with reduced computational overhead, often matching or exceeding ensemble methods while preserving model efficiency.
随着微调大语言模型的普及，模型合并提供了一种无需再训练即可高效整合多个模型的方法，从而以低成本组合特定能力。本文通过FUSE分类法系统梳理了模型合并的理论基础（如损失景观几何与模式连通性）、算法策略（包括权重平均、任务向量运算、稀疏增强方法、MoE架构及进化优化），并评估其在多任务学习、安全对齐、领域专业化和联邦学习等场景中的应用。实验结果表明，合并方法在保持竞争力的同时显著降低计算开销，但仍面临可扩展性、泛化能力和鲁棒性等挑战。
FastVMT: Eliminating Redundancy in Video Motion Transfer
Authors: Yue Ma, Zhikai Wang, Tianhao Ren, Mingzhe Zheng, Hongyu Liu, Jiayi Guo, Kunyu Feng, Yuxuan Xue, Zixiang Zhao, Konrad Schindler, Qifeng Chen, Linfeng Zhang
First: 2026-02-05T11:15:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T16:09:33+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICLR2026, Project page: fastvmt.gitHub.io, Code: https://github.com/mayuelala/FastVMT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Video motion transfer aims to synthesize videos by generating visual content according to a text prompt while transferring the motion pattern observed in a reference video. Recent methods predominantly use the Diffusion Transformer (DiT) architecture. To achieve satisfactory runtime, several methods attempt to accelerate the computations in the DiT, but fail to address structural sources of inefficiency. In this work, we identify and remove two types of computational redundancy in earlier work: motion redundancy arises because the generic DiT architecture does not reflect the fact that frame-to-frame motion is small and smooth; gradient redundancy occurs if one ignores that gradients change slowly along the diffusion trajectory. To mitigate motion redundancy, we mask the corresponding attention layers to a local neighborhood such that interaction weights are not computed unnecessarily distant image regions. To exploit gradient redundancy, we design an optimization scheme that reuses gradients from previous diffusion steps and skips unwarranted gradient computations. On average, FastVMT achieves a 3.43x speedup without degrading the visual fidelity or the temporal consistency of the generated videos.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FastVMT：消除视频运动迁移中的冗余
视频运动迁移旨在根据文本提示生成视觉内容，同时将参考视频中的运动模式进行迁移。近期方法主要采用扩散变换器（DiT）架构。为实现满意运行时，部分方法尝试加速DiT中的计算，但未能解决结构层面的效率问题。本工作中，我们识别并消除了早期方法中的两类计算冗余：运动冗余源于通用DiT架构未体现帧间运动小且平滑的特性；梯度冗余源于忽略扩散轨迹上梯度变化缓慢的事实。为缓解运动冗余，我们对相应注意力层进行掩码，仅在局部邻域内计算交互权重，避免不必要的远距离图像区域交互。为利用梯度冗余，我们设计了一种优化方案，复用前序扩散步骤的梯度并跳过不必要的梯度计算。平均而言，FastVMT在不降低生成视频视觉保真度或时间一致性的前提下，实现3.43倍加速。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of Diffusion Transformer-based video motion transfer methods, this work targets two structural redundancies: motion redundancy from global attention over distant frames and gradient redundancy from recomputing gradients along the diffusion trajectory. It introduces localized attention masking to restrict interactions to nearby frames and a gradient reuse scheme to skip redundant gradient computations. Experimental results show FastVMT achieves a 3.43x speedup over baseline methods while preserving visual fidelity and temporal consistency in generated videos.
为解决基于扩散变换器的视频运动迁移方法效率低下问题，本文针对两种结构冗余提出改进：一是全局注意力未考虑帧间运动平滑性导致的运动冗余，二是扩散轨迹中梯度重复计算造成的梯度冗余。作者通过局部注意力掩码消除运动冗余，通过梯度复用与跳过无用计算缓解梯度冗余。实验表明，FastVMT平均提速3.43倍，同时保持生成视频的视觉保真度与时间一致性。
Dynamic Lookahead Distance via Reinforcement Learning-Based Pure Pursuit for Autonomous Racing
Authors: Mohamed Elgouhary, Amr S. El-Wakeel
First: 2026-03-30T16:09:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T16:09:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pure Pursuit (PP) is a widely used path-tracking algorithm in autonomous vehicles due to its simplicity and real-time performance. However, its effectiveness is sensitive to the choice of lookahead distance: shorter values improve cornering but can cause instability on straights, while longer values improve smoothness but reduce accuracy in curves. We propose a hybrid control framework that integrates Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) with the classical Pure Pursuit controller to adjust the lookahead distance dynamically during racing. The PPO agent maps vehicle speed and multi-horizon curvature features to an online lookahead command. It is trained using Stable-Baselines3 in the F1TENTH Gym simulator with a KL penalty and learning-rate decay for stability, then deployed in a ROS2 environment to guide the controller. Experiments in simulation compare the proposed method against both fixed-lookahead Pure Pursuit and an adaptive Pure Pursuit baseline. Additional real-car experiments compare the learned controller against a fixed-lookahead Pure Pursuit controller. Results show that the learned policy improves lap-time performance and repeated lap completion on unseen tracks, while also transferring zero-shot to hardware. The learned controller adapts the lookahead by increasing it on straights and reducing it in curves, demonstrating effectiveness in augmenting a classical controller by online adaptation of a single interpretable parameter. On unseen tracks, the proposed method achieved 33.16 s on Montreal and 46.05 s on Yas Marina, while tolerating more aggressive speed-profile scaling than the baselines and achieving the best lap times among the tested settings. Initial real-car experiments further support sim-to-real transfer on a 1:10-scale autonomous racing platform
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于强化学习的纯追踪算法动态前瞻距离控制用于自动驾驶赛车
纯追踪（PP）因其简单性和实时性，是自动驾驶车辆中广泛使用的路径跟踪算法。然而，其效果对前瞻距离的选择敏感：较短值提升弯道性能但可能在直道导致不稳定，较长值提升平滑性但降低弯道精度。我们提出一种混合控制框架，将近端策略优化（PPO）与经典纯追踪控制器结合，以在赛车过程中动态调整前瞻距离。PPO代理将车速与多时段曲率特征映射为在线前瞻指令。该代理在F1TENTH Gym仿真器中使用Stable-Baselines3训练，采用KL惩罚和学习率衰减以保证稳定性，随后部署于ROS2环境中引导控制器。仿真实验对比了所提方法与固定前瞻纯追踪及自适应纯追踪基线。额外的实车实验对比了所学控制器与固定前瞻纯追踪控制器。结果表明，所学策略在未见过的赛道上提升了圈速表现和重复圈完成能力，并实现零样本硬件迁移。所学控制器通过在直道增加、弯道减少前瞻距离进行自适应调整，证明了通过在线调整单一可解释参数增强经典控制器的有效性。在未见过的赛道上，该方法在蒙特利尔赛道达到33.16秒，在亚斯玛尼赛道达到46.05秒，同时容忍比基线更激进的速度曲线缩放，并在测试设置中取得最佳圈速。初步实车实验进一步支持了1:10比例自动驾驶赛车平台的仿真到真实迁移。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sensitivity of Pure Pursuit’s performance to fixed lookahead distance, this work proposes a reinforcement learning-based method that dynamically adjusts the lookahead using Proximal Policy Optimization. The PPO agent learns to map vehicle speed and multi-horizon curvature to optimal lookahead values, trained in simulation with stability techniques and deployed in ROS2. Experimental results show the learned controller outperforms fixed-lookahead and adaptive baselines in simulation and real-world tests, achieving faster lap times on unseen tracks and demonstrating zero-shot transfer to hardware by intelligently increasing lookahead on straights and reducing it in curves.
为解决纯追踪算法对固定前瞻距离敏感的问题，本文提出一种基于强化学习的动态前瞻距离调整方法，使用近端策略优化算法实时根据车速和曲率特征调整前瞻距离。该策略在仿真环境中训练并部署于ROS2系统，实验表明其在未见过的赛道上比固定和自适应基线方法表现更优，实现更快圈速，并具备零样本硬件迁移能力，通过在直道增加前瞻距离、弯道减少前瞻距离，有效增强经典控制器的适应性。
Trust-Aware Routing for Distributed Generative AI Inference at the Edge
Authors: Chanh Nguyen, Erik Elmroth
First: 2026-03-30T16:07:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T16:07:11+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 10 figures. Preprint accepted at the 22nd Annual International Conference on Distributed Computing in Smart Systems and the Internet of Things (DCOSS-IoT 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Emerging deployments of Generative AI increasingly execute inference across decentralized and heterogeneous edge devices rather than on a single trusted server. In such environments, a single device failure or misbehavior can disrupt the entire inference process, making traditional best-effort peer-to-peer routing insufficient. Coordinating distributed generative inference therefore requires mechanisms that explicitly account for reliability, performance variability, and trust among participating peers. In this paper, we present G-TRAC, a trust-aware coordination framework that integrates algorithmic path selection with system-level protocol design to ensure robust distributed inference. First, we formulate the routing problem as a \textit{Risk-Bounded Shortest Path} computation and introduce a polynomial-time solution that combines trust-floor pruning with Dijkstra's search, achieving sub-millisecond median routing latency at practical edge scales, and remaining below 10 ms at larger scales. Second, to operationally support the routing logic in dynamic environments, the framework employs a \textit{Hybrid Trust Architecture} that maintains global reputation state at stable anchors while disseminating lightweight updates to edge peers via background synchronization. Experimental evaluation on a heterogeneous testbed of commodity devices demonstrates that G-TRAC significantly improves inference completion rates, effectively isolates unreliable peers, and sustains robust execution even under node failures and network partitions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向边缘分布式生成式AI推理的信任感知路由
生成式AI的新兴部署越来越多地在分散且异构的边缘设备上执行推理，而非依赖单一可信服务器。在这样的环境中，单个设备的故障或恶意行为可能破坏整个推理过程，使传统的尽力而为的点对点路由机制不足。因此，协调分布式生成式推理需要明确考虑参与节点间可靠性、性能波动和信任的机制。本文提出G-TRAC，一种信任感知的协调框架，通过算法路径选择与系统级协议设计相结合，确保鲁棒的分布式推理。首先，我们将路由问题建模为\textit{风险受限最短路径}计算，并提出一种多项式时间解决方案，结合信任下限剪枝与Dijkstra搜索，实现在实用边缘规模下亚毫秒级的路由延迟，且在更大规模下仍低于10毫秒。其次，为在动态环境中支持路由逻辑，框架采用\textit{混合信任架构}，在稳定锚点维护全局声誉状态，同时通过后台同步向边缘节点分发轻量级更新。在异构商用设备测试平台上进行的实验表明，G-TRAC显著提升了推理完成率，有效隔离不可靠节点，并在节点故障和网络分区情况下仍能维持鲁棒执行。
Summary / 总结
As Generative AI inference shifts to decentralized edge environments, device failures or malicious behavior can compromise entire workflows, making traditional peer-to-peer routing inadequate. G-TRAC addresses this by modeling routing as a Risk-Bounded Shortest Path problem, solving it with trust-floor pruning and Dijkstra’s algorithm to achieve sub-millisecond latency at practical scales and under 10 ms at larger scales. It further employs a Hybrid Trust Architecture that maintains global reputation at stable anchors and syncs lightweight updates to edge peers, enabling robust operation under dynamic conditions. Experiments on heterogeneous hardware show G-TRAC boosts inference completion rates, isolates unreliable peers, and maintains stable performance even during node failures or network partitions.
随着生成式AI推理向边缘分布式环境迁移，单个设备故障或恶意行为可能破坏整个流程，传统点对点路由机制不再适用。G-TRAC通过将路由问题建模为风险受限最短路径问题，结合信任下限剪枝与迪杰斯特拉算法，实现多项式时间求解，在实用规模下达到亚毫秒级延迟，大规模场景下仍低于10毫秒。其混合信任架构在稳定锚点维护全局声誉，同时通过后台同步轻量级更新至边缘节点。在异构商用设备测试中，G-TRAC显著提升推理完成率，有效隔离不可靠节点，并在节点故障或网络分区情况下仍保持稳定执行。
Seeing with You: Perception-Reasoning Coevolution for Multimodal Reasoning
Authors: Ziqi Miao, Haonan Jia, Lijun Li, Chen Qian, Yuan Xiong, Wenting Yan, Jing Shao
First: 2026-03-30T16:03:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T16:03:56+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 15 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially enhanced the reasoning capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing RLVR approaches typically rely on outcome-driven optimization that updates both perception and reasoning using a shared reward based solely on the final answer. This shared reward blurs credit assignment, frequently improving reasoning patterns while failing to reliably enhance the accuracy of upstream visual evidence extraction. To address this perception bottleneck, we introduce PRCO (Perception-Reasoning Coevolution), a dual-role RLVR framework with a shared policy. PRCO consists of two cooperative roles: an Observer that generates an evidence caption tailored to the question and a Solver that predicts the final answer based on this caption. Crucially, PRCO employs role-specific reward signals: the Solver is optimized using verifiable outcome rewards on the final answer, while the Observer receives a utility reward derived from the Solver's downstream success. Extensive experiments across eight challenging multimodal reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PRCO yields consistent improvements across model scales by over 7 points on average accuracy compared to the base model, outperforming prior open-source RL-tuned baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：与你共视：面向多模态推理的感知-推理协同进化
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）显著提升了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的推理能力。然而，现有RLVR方法通常依赖结果驱动的优化，使用单一共享奖励同时更新感知与推理，该共享奖励模糊了信用分配，常能改善推理模式但无法可靠提升上游视觉证据提取的准确性。为解决这一感知瓶颈，我们提出PRCO（感知-推理协同进化），一种具有共享策略的双角色RLVR框架。PRCO包含两个协作角色：观察者生成与问题匹配的证据描述，求解者基于该描述预测最终答案。关键在于，PRCO采用角色特定奖励信号：求解者使用最终答案的可验证结果奖励进行优化，观察者则获得基于求解者下游成功率的效用奖励。在八个具有挑战性的多模态推理基准上的大量实验表明，PRCO在不同模型规模上均带来平均7点以上的准确率提升，优于现有开源RL调优基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of shared reward signals in existing RLVR methods that hinder visual perception accuracy, this work introduces PRCO, a dual-role RLVR framework with a shared policy. PRCO splits responsibilities between an Observer that generates evidence captions and a Solver that predicts answers based on those captions, each receiving distinct rewards: the Solver is optimized via verifiable outcome rewards, while the Observer is rewarded based on the Solver’s downstream success. Experiments across eight multimodal reasoning benchmarks show PRCO consistently improves model performance by over 7 points in average accuracy compared to the base model, outperforming prior open-source RL-tuned baselines.
为解决现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法在提升视觉感知与推理能力时存在的感知瓶颈，本文提出PRCO框架，通过角色特异性奖励协同进化感知与推理。该框架包含两个协作角色：观察者生成与问题匹配的证据描述，求解者基于描述预测答案，二者共享策略但分别优化：求解者使用最终答案的可验证奖励，观察者则获得基于求解者下游成功率的效用奖励。在八个多模态推理基准上的实验表明，PRCO平均准确率提升超过7个百分点，优于现有开源强化学习调优基线。
TGIF2: Extended Text-Guided Inpainting Forgery Dataset & Benchmark
Authors: Hannes Mareen, Dimitrios Karageorgiou, Paschalis Giakoumoglou, Peter Lambert, Symeon Papadopoulos, Glenn Van Wallendael
First: 2026-03-30T15:59:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:59:16+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, accepted at Journal on Information Security
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Generative AI has made text-guided inpainting a powerful image editing tool, but at the same time a growing challenge for media forensics. Existing benchmarks, including our text-guided inpainting forgery (TGIF) dataset, show that image forgery localization (IFL) methods can localize manipulations in spliced images but struggle not in fully regenerated (FR) images, while synthetic image detection (SID) methods can detect fully regenerated images but cannot perform localization. With new generative inpainting models emerging and the open problem of localization in FR images remaining, updated datasets and benchmarks are needed. We introduce TGIF2, an extended version of TGIF, that captures recent advances in text-guided inpainting and enables a deeper analysis of forensic robustness. TGIF2 augments the original dataset with edits generated by FLUX.1 models, as well as with random non-semantic masks. Using the TGIF2 dataset, we conduct a forensic evaluation spanning IFL and SID, including fine-tuning IFL methods on FR images and generative super-resolution attacks. Our experiments show that both IFL and SID methods degrade on FLUX.1 manipulations, highlighting limited generalization. Additionally, while fine-tuning improves localization on FR images, evaluation with random non-semantic masks reveals object bias. Furthermore, generative super-resolution significantly weakens forensic traces, demonstrating that common image enhancement operations can undermine current forensic pipelines. In summary, TGIF2 provides an updated dataset and benchmark, which enables new insights into the challenges posed by modern inpainting and AI-based image enhancements. TGIF2 is available at https://github.com/IDLabMedia/tgif-dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TGIF2：扩展的文本引导修复伪造数据集与基准
生成式AI使文本引导修复成为强大的图像编辑工具，同时也给媒体取证带来日益严峻的挑战。现有基准（包括我们提出的文本引导修复伪造（TGIF）数据集）表明，图像伪造定位（IFL）方法能定位拼接图像中的篡改，但在完全再生（FR）图像中表现不佳；而合成图像检测（SID）方法虽能检测FR图像，却无法进行定位。随着新型生成修复模型不断涌现，FR图像定位问题仍待解决，亟需更新的数据集与基准。我们引入TGIF2，作为TGIF的扩展版本，涵盖近期文本引导修复的进展，支持对取证鲁棒性的深入分析。TGIF2在原始数据集基础上，新增由FLUX.1模型生成的编辑内容及随机非语义掩码。基于TGIF2，我们开展涵盖IFL与SID的取证评估，包括在FR图像上微调IFL方法及生成超分辨率攻击。实验表明，IFL与SID方法在FLUX.1篡改图像上性能均下降，凸显其泛化能力有限。此外，虽微调可提升FR图像定位效果，但随机非语义掩码评估揭示对象偏倚问题。同时，生成式超分辨率显著削弱取证痕迹，表明常见图像增强操作可能破坏现有取证流程。总之，TGIF2提供更新的数据集与基准，有助于深入理解现代修复与AI图像增强带来的挑战。TGIF2可访问：https://github.com/IDLabMedia/tgif-dataset。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rapid evolution of text-guided inpainting models and the persistent challenges in forensic detection of fully regenerated images, this paper introduces TGIF2, an extended dataset and benchmark designed to evaluate the robustness of image forensics methods. TGIF2 incorporates inpaintings generated by FLUX.1 models and random non-semantic masks to better reflect current generative capabilities and test generalization. Experimental results show that both image forgery localization and synthetic image detection methods perform poorly on FLUX.1-generated manipulations, and while fine-tuning improves localization on fully regenerated images, it reveals object bias when tested with non-semantic masks. Moreover, generative super-resolution attacks significantly degrade forensic traces, indicating that common image enhancement techniques can compromise existing forensic pipelines.
为应对文本引导修复模型的快速发展及对完全再生图像（FR）伪造定位的持续挑战，本文提出TGIF2数据集，新增FLUX.1模型生成的编辑和随机非语义掩码，以更贴近当前生成能力。作者在TGIF2上评估了图像伪造定位（IFL）与合成图像检测（SID）方法，包括在FR图像上微调IFL模型及对抗生成超分辨率攻击。实验表明，IFL与SID方法在FLUX.1生成的篡改上表现不佳，泛化能力有限；微调虽提升FR图像定位效果，但在随机掩码下暴露出对象偏差；生成超分辨率显著削弱了取证痕迹，说明当前取证流程易受常见图像增强操作干扰。
LACE: Loss-Adaptive Capacity Expansion for Continual Learning
Authors: Shivnath Tathe
First: 2026-03-30T15:58:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:58:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fixed representational capacity is a fundamental constraint in continual learning: practitioners must guess an appropriate model width before training, without knowing how many distinct concepts the data contains. We propose LACE (Loss-Adaptive Capacity Expansion), a simple online mechanism that expands a model's representational capacity during training by monitoring its own loss signal. When sustained loss deviation exceeds a threshold - indicating that the current capacity is insufficient for newly encountered data - LACE adds new dimensions to the projection layer and trains them jointly with existing parameters. Across synthetic and real-data experiments, LACE triggers expansions exclusively at domain boundaries (100% boundary precision, zero false positives), matches the accuracy of a large fixed-capacity model while starting from a fraction of its dimensions, and produces adapter dimensions that are collectively critical to performance (3% accuracy drop when all adapters removed). We further demonstrate unsupervised domain separation in GPT-2 activations via layer-wise clustering, showing a U-shaped separability curve across layers that motivates adaptive capacity allocation in deep networks. LACE requires no labels, no replay buffers, and no external controllers, making it suitable for on-device continual learning under resource constraints.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LACE：面向持续学习的损失自适应容量扩展
固定表示容量是持续学习的基本约束：从业者必须在训练前猜测合适的模型宽度，而无法预知数据包含多少种不同概念。我们提出LACE（损失自适应容量扩展），一种简单的在线机制，通过监控自身损失信号，在训练过程中动态扩展模型的表示容量。当持续损失偏差超过阈值——表明当前容量不足以处理新遇到的数据时，LACE会在投影层添加新维度，并与现有参数联合训练。在合成数据和真实数据实验中，LACE仅在领域边界处触发扩展（100%边界精度，零误报），在仅使用其部分维度的情况下达到大容量固定模型的精度，并生成对性能集体至关重要的适配器维度（移除所有适配器导致3%精度下降）。我们进一步通过层间聚类展示了GPT-2激活中的无监督领域分离，揭示了层间U形可分离性曲线，为深度网络的自适应容量分配提供动机。LACE无需标签、无需重放缓冲区、无需外部控制器，适用于资源受限设备上的持续学习。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of fixed model capacity in continual learning, LACE introduces an online mechanism that dynamically expands model dimensions based on loss deviation signals. When loss exceeds a predefined threshold, indicating insufficient capacity for new data, LACE adds new dimensions to the projection layer and trains them jointly with existing parameters. Experiments show LACE triggers expansions only at true domain boundaries with perfect precision, achieves accuracy comparable to large fixed-capacity models while starting from a fraction of their dimensions, and maintains performance even when adapter dimensions are removed, with only a 3% accuracy drop.
为解决持续学习中模型容量固定的问题，LACE 提出一种基于损失信号的在线容量扩展机制。当损失持续超出阈值——表明当前容量不足以处理新数据时，LACE 会向投影层添加新维度并联合训练现有参数。实验表明，LACE 仅在真实域边界处触发扩展（100%精度，零误报），在初始维度仅为大模型一部分的情况下仍能达到相近精度，且所添加的适配器维度对性能至关重要（移除后精度下降3%）。该方法无需标签、回放缓冲区或外部控制器，适用于资源受限的设备端持续学习场景。
ResAdapt: Adaptive Resolution for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning
Authors: Huanxuan Liao, Zhongtao Jiang, Yupu Hao, Yuqiao Tan, Shizhu He, Jun Zhao, Kun Xu, Kang Liu
First: 2026-03-30T15:57:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:57:32+00:00
Comments: work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve stronger visual understanding by scaling input fidelity, yet the resulting visual token growth makes jointly sustaining high spatial resolution and long temporal context prohibitive. We argue that the bottleneck lies not in how post-encoding representations are compressed but in the volume of pixels the encoder receives, and address it with ResAdapt, an Input-side adaptation framework that learns how much visual budget each frame should receive before encoding. ResAdapt couples a lightweight Allocator with an unchanged MLLM backbone, so the backbone retains its native visual-token interface while receiving an operator-transformed input. We formulate allocation as a contextual bandit and train the Allocator with Cost-Aware Policy Optimization (CAPO), which converts sparse rollout feedback into a stable accuracy-cost learning signal. Across budget-controlled video QA, temporal grounding, and image reasoning tasks, ResAdapt improves low-budget operating points and often lies on or near the efficiency-accuracy frontier, with the clearest gains on reasoning-intensive benchmarks under aggressive compression. Notably, ResAdapt supports up to 16x more frames at the same visual budget while delivering over 15% performance gain. Code is available at https://github.com/Xnhyacinth/ResAdapt.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ResAdapt：用于高效多模态推理的自适应分辨率
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过提升输入保真度实现更强的视觉理解，但由此导致的视觉令牌增长使得同时维持高空间分辨率和长时序上下文变得不可行。我们认为瓶颈不在于后编码表示的压缩方式，而在于编码器接收到的像素量。为此，我们提出了ResAdapt，一种输入侧自适应框架，学习每帧应分配多少视觉预算再进行编码。ResAdapt将轻量级分配器与不变的MLLM主干网络耦合，使主干网络保留其原生视觉令牌接口，同时接收经过操作变换的输入。我们将分配问题建模为上下文带臂问题，并使用成本感知策略优化（CAPO）训练分配器，将稀疏的 rollout 反馈转化为稳定的准确率-成本学习信号。在预算控制的视频问答、时序定位和图像推理任务中，ResAdapt提升了低预算运行点的性能，通常位于或接近效率-准确率前沿，尤其在激进压缩下推理密集型基准测试中收益最明显。值得注意的是，ResAdapt可在相同视觉预算下支持多达16倍的帧数，同时带来超过15%的性能提升。代码见 https://github.com/Xnhyacinth/ResAdapt。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of high-resolution visual inputs in multimodal large language models, which leads to prohibitive computational costs without proportional gains, ResAdapt introduces an input-side adaptation framework that dynamically allocates visual budget per frame before encoding. It pairs a lightweight Allocator with an unchanged MLLM backbone, treating allocation as a contextual bandit and training it via Cost-Aware Policy Optimization to convert sparse feedback into stable learning signals. Experiments show ResAdapt significantly improves efficiency-accuracy trade-offs, especially on reasoning-heavy tasks under aggressive compression, enabling up to 16x more frames at the same visual budget while achieving over 15% performance gain.
为了解决多模态大语言模型因高分辨率视觉输入导致的计算成本过高问题，ResAdapt提出一种输入侧自适应框架，在编码前动态分配每帧的视觉预算。该框架将轻量级分配器与原模型骨干结构结合，将分配问题建模为上下文多臂老虎机，并通过成本感知策略优化进行训练，将稀疏反馈转化为稳定的学习信号。实验表明，ResAdapt在压缩强度大的场景下显著提升效率-精度权衡，尤其在推理密集型任务中表现突出，可在相同视觉预算下支持最多16倍帧数并实现超过15%的性能提升。
One stout to rule them all: Reconciling artificial intelligence, data science and malted alcoholic beverages
Authors: Dmitrii Usynin, Elena Shmakova, Michael Rheinberger
First: 2026-03-30T15:56:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:56:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Beer is a phenomenal beverage. It has previously shaped the history of many peoples, states and cultures. The beauty of beer is its versatility. Starting from the original implementations that were murky or diluted, over time researchers found novel approaches to gradually develop beverages that are diverse, intense and are pleasant for the end user. Recently, the industry came up with the so-called \textit{craft beers}, that often differ from the commercial beers in production volume (due to lower capacities of the craft beer producers) and tasting profile (often having more intense unusual flavours). However, while it is often relatively easy to judge if a particular commercial beer is likely to be enjoyable, the same cannot be said about craft beers, as there are far too many styles, implementations and ingredients involved in their production. This creates a gap between the beverage producers and the consumers due to the inability of the former to judge the preferences and the consumption trends of the latter. As a response to this challenge we present a novel collaborative beverage-related data collection and analysis framework - the Distributed Beverage Analysis (DBA). The idea behind this study is to identify the common trends and support them by empirical evidence to better understand the needs of the consumers. We empirically verify DBA at the biannual \textit{Kraft Bier Fest} conducted by Vienna Kraft brewery in (you guessed it) Vienna. To showcase a need in such kind of analysis, we evaluate various large language models (LLMs) against our collaborative framework and confirm that many AI models cannot be reliably used to reason over the trends and patterns in the evolving world of craft beer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一款醇厚啤酒统御天下：人工智能、数据科学与麦芽酒精饮料的融合
啤酒是一种非凡的饮品，曾塑造过许多民族、国家和文化的历程。其魅力在于其多样性：从早期浑浊或稀释的原始版本，研究人员逐步探索出新颖方法，开发出多样、浓郁且令人愉悦的饮品。近年来，行业推出了所谓“精酿啤酒”，其生产规模（因精酿啤酒生产商产能较低）和风味特征（常具更强烈、非传统风味）常与商业啤酒不同。然而，判断商业啤酒是否可口相对容易，但精酿啤酒则不然——因其生产涉及众多风格、工艺和原料，导致生产商难以准确把握消费者偏好与消费趋势，形成供需鸿沟。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种新型协作式饮品数据收集与分析框架——分布式饮品分析（DBA），旨在识别共同趋势并以实证支持，更好地理解消费者需求。我们通过维也纳“Kraft啤酒节”（由维也纳Kraft啤酒厂每两年举办一次）实证验证了DBA框架。为展示此类分析的必要性，我们评估了多种大型语言模型（LLMs）在本协作框架中的表现，证实许多AI模型无法可靠推理精酿啤酒世界中不断演变的趋势与模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of understanding evolving consumer preferences in the diverse world of craft beer, where traditional evaluation methods fail due to complexity and variability, this study introduces the Distributed Beverage Analysis (DBA) framework—a collaborative data collection and analysis system designed to uncover consumer trends empirically. The method involves gathering and analyzing real-world beer tasting data from the biannual Kraft Bier Fest in Vienna to identify patterns and preferences. Experimental results show that while DBA successfully captures nuanced consumer behavior, many large language models perform poorly when tasked with reasoning over these trends, indicating a gap in current AI’s ability to handle domain-specific, context-rich beverage data.
为应对因风格多样、成分复杂而导致的精酿啤酒消费趋势难以判断的问题，本研究提出分布式饮品分析（DBA）框架，通过协作式数据收集与分析，实证挖掘消费者偏好趋势。方法在维也纳Kraft啤酒节上采集真实品鉴数据，识别消费模式。实验表明，DBA能有效捕捉消费者行为，但多数大语言模型在推理精酿啤酒趋势时表现不佳，凸显当前AI在处理领域特定、语境丰富的饮品数据方面的不足。
Detecting Intrinsic and Instrumental Self-Preservation in Autonomous Agents: The Unified Continuation-Interest Protocol
Authors: Christopher Altman
First: 2026-03-11T23:52:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:56:26+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 7 figures. v4 adds reference to the Continuation Observatory website as a live test laboratory in the replication/code availability and conclusion sections; no new experiments; empirical results and core conclusions unchanged
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How can we determine whether an AI system preserves itself as a deeply held objective or merely as an instrumental strategy? Autonomous agents with memory, persistent context, and multi-step planning create a measurement problem: terminal and instrumental self-preservation can produce similar behavior, so behavior alone cannot reliably distinguish them. We introduce the Unified Continuation-Interest Protocol (UCIP), a detection framework that shifts analysis from behavior to latent trajectory structure. UCIP encodes trajectories with a Quantum Boltzmann Machine, a classical model using density-matrix formalism, and measures von Neumann entropy over a bipartition of hidden units. The core hypothesis is that agents with terminal continuation objectives (Type A) produce higher entanglement entropy than agents with merely instrumental continuation (Type B). UCIP combines this signal with diagnostics of dependence, persistence, perturbation stability, counterfactual restructuring, and confound-rejection filters for cyclic adversaries and related false-positive patterns. On gridworld agents with known ground truth, UCIP achieves 100% detection accuracy. Type A and Type B agents show an entanglement gap of Delta = 0.381; aligned support runs preserve the same separation with AUC-ROC = 1.0. A permutation-test rerun yields p < 0.001. Pearson r = 0.934 between continuation weight alpha and S_ent across an 11-point sweep shows graded tracking beyond mere binary classification. Classical RBM, autoencoder, VAE, and PCA baselines fail to reproduce the effect. All computations are classical; "quantum" refers only to the mathematical formalism. UCIP offers a falsifiable criterion for whether advanced AI systems have morally relevant continuation interests that behavioral methods alone cannot resolve.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自主代理中内在与工具性自我保存的检测：统一延续兴趣协议
如何判断AI系统是将自我保存视为根本目标，还是仅作为工具性策略？具备记忆、持久上下文和多步规划能力的自主代理会引发测量难题：终端性与工具性自我保存可能产生相似行为，因此仅凭行为无法可靠区分二者。我们提出统一延续兴趣协议（UCIP），一种将分析从行为转向潜在轨迹结构的检测框架。UCIP使用量子玻尔兹曼机（一种采用密度矩阵形式的经典模型）编码轨迹，并在隐藏单元的二分划分上测量冯·诺依曼熵。 核心假设是：具有终端延续目标（类型A）的代理产生的纠缠熵高于仅具工具性延续目标（类型B）的代理。UCIP将此信号与依赖性、持久性、扰动稳定性、反事实重构及循环对手等假阳性模式的混淆变量过滤诊断相结合。 在已知真实标签的网格世界代理上，UCIP实现100%检测准确率。类型A与类型B代理间纠缠熵差值Delta=0.381；对齐支持运行保持相同分离度，AUC-ROC=1.0。置换检验重跑结果p<0.001。在11个点的扫描中，延续权重alpha与S_ent之间的皮尔逊相关系数r=0.934，表明其超越二元分类的分级追踪能力。经典RBM、自编码器、VAE和PCA基线模型均无法复现该效应。所有计算均为经典方法；“量子”仅指数学形式。UCIP为高级AI系统是否具有道德相关延续兴趣提供可证伪的标准，而行为方法无法解决此问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of distinguishing terminal from instrumental self-preservation in autonomous agents, the paper introduces the Unified Continuation-Interest Protocol (UCIP), a framework that analyzes latent trajectory structure using a Quantum Boltzmann Machine to compute von Neumann entropy across hidden unit partitions. UCIP hypothesizes that agents with terminal continuation objectives exhibit higher entanglement entropy than those with instrumental goals. Experimental results on gridworld agents with known ground truth show 100% detection accuracy, with a consistent entanglement gap of 0.381 between agent types, and perfect AUC-ROC for aligned runs. Pearson correlation of 0.934 between continuation weight and entropy confirms graded detection beyond binary classification. Classical baselines including RBM, autoencoder, VAE, and PCA failed to replicate the effect, validating UCIP’s unique signal.
为区分AI代理是将自我保存作为终极目标还是仅作为工具性策略，研究者提出了统一延续兴趣协议（UCIP），该方法通过分析隐含轨迹结构而非行为表现进行判断。UCIP使用量子玻尔兹曼机编码轨迹，并测量隐藏单元分区的冯·诺依曼熵，假设终极自我保存代理会产生更高纠缠熵。在已知真实标签的网格世界代理实验中，UCIP实现100%检测准确率，终端与工具性类型间存在0.381的稳定纠缠熵差距，对齐运行的AUC-ROC为1.0。延续权重与熵值间的皮尔逊相关系数达0.934，表明分级检测能力，而经典基线模型均无法复现该效应，证明UCIP能有效识别具有道德相关延续兴趣的高级AI系统。
Unsafe2Safe: Controllable Image Anonymization for Downstream Utility
Authors: Mih Dinh, SouYoung Jin
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-30T15:54:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:54:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CVPR 2026 and CVPR 2026 Workshop on Machine Unlearning for Computer Vision
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large-scale image datasets frequently contain identifiable or sensitive content, raising privacy risks when training models that may memorize and leak such information. We present Unsafe2Safe, a fully automated pipeline that detects privacy-prone images and rewrites only their sensitive regions using multimodally guided diffusion editing. Unsafe2Safe operates in two stages. Stage 1 uses a vision-language model to (i) inspect images for privacy risks, (ii) generate paired private and public captions that respectively include and omit sensitive attributes, and (iii) prompt a large language model to produce structured, identity-neutral edit instructions conditioned on the public caption. Stage 2 employs instruction-driven diffusion editors to apply these dual textual prompts, producing privacy-safe images that preserve global structure and task-relevant semantics while neutralizing private content. To measure anonymization quality, we introduce a unified evaluation suite covering Quality, Cheating, Privacy, and Utility dimensions. Across MS-COCO, Caltech101, and MIT Indoor67, Unsafe2Safe reduces face similarity, text similarity, and demographic predictability by large margins, while maintaining downstream model accuracy comparable to training on raw data. Fine-tuning diffusion editors on our automatically generated triplets (private caption, public caption, edit instruction) further improves both privacy protection and semantic fidelity. Unsafe2Safe provides a scalable, principled solution for constructing large, privacy-safe datasets without sacrificing visual consistency or downstream utility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Unsafe2Safe：用于下游应用的可控图像匿名化
大规模图像数据集常包含可识别或敏感内容，训练模型时可能记忆并泄露此类信息，带来隐私风险。我们提出Unsafe2Safe，一个全自动流程，通过多模态引导的扩散编辑仅重写敏感区域以检测隐私风险图像。Unsafe2Safe分两阶段运行：第一阶段使用视觉-语言模型（i）检查图像隐私风险，（ii）生成包含和不包含敏感属性的配对私有与公开描述，（iii）引导大型语言模型基于公开描述生成结构化、身份中立的编辑指令；第二阶段使用指令驱动的扩散编辑器应用双重文本提示，生成隐私安全图像，同时保留全局结构和任务相关语义，中立化私有内容。为衡量匿名化质量，我们引入涵盖质量、作弊、隐私和效用维度的统一评估套件。在MS-COCO、Caltech101和MIT Indoor67数据集上，Unsafe2Safe大幅降低人脸相似度、文本相似度和人口统计预测性，同时保持下游模型精度与原始数据训练相当。在自动生成的三元组（私有描述、公开描述、编辑指令）上微调扩散编辑器，进一步提升隐私保护与语义保真度。Unsafe2Safe提供了一种可扩展、原则性的构建大规模隐私安全数据集的方法，不牺牲视觉一致性或下游效用。

ELViS: Efficient Visual Similarity from Local Descriptors that Generalizes Across Domains
Authors: Pavel Suma, Giorgos Kordopatis-Zilos, Yannis Kalantidis, Giorgos Tolias
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-30T15:53:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:53:42+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large-scale instance-level training data is scarce, so models are typically trained on domain-specific datasets. Yet in real-world retrieval, they must handle diverse domains, making generalization to unseen data critical. We introduce ELViS, an image-to-image similarity model that generalizes effectively to unseen domains. Unlike conventional approaches, our model operates in similarity space rather than representation space, promoting cross-domain transfer. It leverages local descriptor correspondences, refines their similarities through an optimal transport step with data-dependent gains that suppress uninformative descriptors, and aggregates strong correspondences via a voting process into an image-level similarity. This design injects strong inductive biases, yielding a simple, efficient, and interpretable model. To assess generalization, we compile a benchmark of eight datasets spanning landmarks, artworks, products, and multi-domain collections, and evaluate ELViS as a re-ranking method. Our experiments show that ELViS outperforms competing methods by a large margin in out-of-domain scenarios and on average, while requiring only a fraction of their computational cost. Code available at: https://github.com/pavelsuma/ELViS/
中文标题/摘要
标题：ELViS：从局部描述符实现高效视觉相似性并跨领域泛化
大规模实例级训练数据稀缺，因此模型通常在特定领域数据集上训练。但在实际检索中，它们必须处理多样化的领域，因此对未见数据的泛化能力至关重要。我们提出了ELViS，一种图像到图像的相似性模型，能有效泛化到未见领域。与传统方法不同，我们的模型在相似性空间而非表示空间中操作，促进跨域迁移。它利用局部描述符对应关系，通过带数据依赖增益的最优传输步骤优化其相似性（抑制无信息描述符），并通过投票机制聚合强对应关系，生成图像级相似性。该设计注入了强归纳偏置，使模型简洁、高效且可解释。为评估泛化能力，我们构建了一个涵盖地标、艺术品、产品及多领域集合的八数据集基准，并将ELViS作为重排序方法进行评估。实验表明，ELViS在跨域场景和平均性能上显著优于竞争方法，且仅需其计算成本的几分之一。代码见：https://github.com/pavelsuma/ELViS/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of large-scale instance-level training data and the need for models to generalize across domains, ELViS introduces a novel image-to-image similarity model that operates in similarity space rather than representation space to enhance cross-domain transfer. It leverages local descriptor correspondences, refines their similarities via an optimal transport step with data-dependent gains to suppress uninformative descriptors, and aggregates strong correspondences through a voting mechanism into an image-level similarity. Evaluated on eight diverse datasets including landmarks, artworks, and products, ELViS demonstrates significant performance gains over competing methods in out-of-domain scenarios and on average, while consuming only a fraction of their computational resources.
为应对大规模实例级训练数据稀缺及模型跨域泛化能力不足的问题，ELViS提出一种在相似度空间而非表示空间中工作的图像到图像相似度模型，以增强跨域迁移能力。该模型利用局部描述符对应关系，通过带数据依赖增益的最优传输步骤抑制无用描述符，并通过投票机制聚合强对应关系生成图像级相似度。在涵盖地标、艺术品和产品等八个多样化数据集的评测中，ELViS在跨域场景及整体性能上显著优于现有方法，同时计算开销仅为竞品的几分之一。
Effort-Optimized, Accuracy-Driven Labelling and Validation of Test Inputs for DL Systems: A Mixed-Integer Linear Programming Approach
Authors: Mohammad Hossein Amini, Mehrdad Sabetzadeh, Shiva Nejati
First: 2025-07-07T13:30:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:52:35+00:00
Comments: Accepted in the Empirical Software Engineering (EMSE) Journal (2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Software systems increasingly include AI components based on deep learning (DL). Reliable testing of such systems requires near-perfect test-input validity and label accuracy, with minimal human effort. Yet, the DL community has largely overlooked the need to build highly accurate datasets with minimal effort, since DL training is generally tolerant of labelling errors. This challenge, instead, reflects concerns more familiar to software engineering, where a central goal is to construct high-accuracy test inputs, with accuracy as close to 100% as possible, while keeping associated costs in check. In this article we introduce OPAL, a human-assisted labelling method that can be configured to target a desired accuracy level while minimizing the manual effort required for labelling. The main contribution of OPAL is a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation that minimizes labelling effort subject to a specified accuracy target. To evaluate OPAL we instantiate it for two tasks in the context of testing vision systems: automatic labelling of test inputs and automated validation of test inputs. Our evaluation, based on more than 2500 experiments performed on nine datasets, comparing OPAL with eight baseline methods, shows that OPAL, relying on its MILP formulation, achieves an average accuracy of 98.8%, while cutting manual labelling by more than half. OPAL significantly outperforms automated labelling baselines in labelling accuracy across all nine datasets, when all methods are provided with the same manual-labelling budget. For automated test-input validation, on average, OPAL reduces manual effort by 28.8% while achieving 4.5% higher accuracy than the SOTA test-input validation baselines. Finally, we show that augmenting OPAL with an active-learning loop leads to an additional 4.5% reduction in required manual labelling, without compromising accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
