Evaluated on nine datasets across two tasks—automatic test input labelling and validation—OPAL achieved an average accuracy of 98.8% while reducing manual labelling by over 50% compared to baselines. It outperformed all automated labelling methods under equal manual effort budgets and improved validation accuracy by 4.5% while cutting effort by 28.8% over state-of-the-art validation methods. Adding an active-learning loop further reduced manual effort by 4.5% without sacrificing accuracy.
为在保证深度学习系统测试输入高准确率的同时降低人工成本，本文提出OPAL方法，利用混合整数线性规划优化人工标注效率并满足指定准确率目标。在九个数据集上的两项任务（自动标注与验证）中，OPAL平均准确率达98.8%，人工标注成本降低超50%，显著优于所有自动化标注基线方法（在相同人工预算下）。在测试输入验证任务中，OPAL比当前最优方法准确率高4.5%，人工成本降低28.8%。引入主动学习循环后，人工标注需求再减少4.5%，且不牺牲准确率。
Follow-Your-Motion: Video Motion Transfer via Efficient Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Finetuning
Authors: Yue Ma, Yulong Liu, Qiyuan Zhu, Ayden Yang, Kunyu Feng, Xinhua Zhang, Zexuan Yan, Zhifeng Li, Sirui Han, Chenyang Qi, Qifeng Chen
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-06-05T16:18:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:48:00+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICLR 2026, project page: https://follow-your-motion.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recently, breakthroughs in the video diffusion transformer have shown remarkable capabilities in diverse motion generations. As for the motion-transfer task, current methods mainly use two-stage Low-Rank Adaptations (LoRAs) finetuning to obtain better performance. However, existing adaptation-based motion transfer still suffers from motion inconsistency and tuning inefficiency when applied to large video diffusion transformers. Naive two-stage LoRA tuning struggles to maintain motion consistency between generated and input videos due to the inherent spatial-temporal coupling in the 3D attention operator. Additionally, they require time-consuming fine-tuning processes in both stages. To tackle these issues, we propose Follow-Your-Motion, an efficient two-stage video motion transfer framework that finetunes a powerful video diffusion transformer to synthesize complex motion. Specifically, we propose a spatial-temporal decoupled LoRA to decouple the attention architecture for spatial appearance and temporal motion processing. During the second training stage, we design the sparse motion sampling and adaptive RoPE to accelerate the tuning speed. To address the lack of a benchmark for this field, we introduce MotionBench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising diverse motion, including creative camera motion, single object motion, multiple object motion, and complex human motion. We show extensive evaluations on MotionBench to verify the superiority of Follow-Your-Motion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跟随你的动作：通过高效时空解耦微调实现视频运动迁移
近期，视频扩散Transformer在多样运动生成方面展现出显著能力。针对运动迁移任务，现有方法主要采用两阶段低秩自适应（LoRA）微调以提升性能。然而，现有基于适配的运动迁移方法在应用于大型视频扩散Transformer时仍存在运动不一致和调优效率低的问题。朴素的两阶段LoRA调优因3D注意力算子固有的时空耦合，难以维持生成视频与输入视频间的运动一致性，且两阶段均需耗时的微调过程。为解决这些问题，我们提出Follow-Your-Motion，一种高效的两阶段视频运动迁移框架，用于微调强大视频扩散Transformer以合成复杂运动。具体而言，我们提出时空解耦LoRA，将注意力架构解耦为处理空间外观与时间运动的独立模块。在第二阶段训练中，我们设计稀疏运动采样与自适应RoPE以加速调优速度。为填补该领域缺乏基准测试的空白，我们引入MotionBench，一个涵盖创意摄像机运动、单目标运动、多目标运动及复杂人体运动的综合性基准。我们在MotionBench上进行广泛评估，验证Follow-Your-Motion的优越性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve motion consistency and tuning efficiency in video motion transfer for large video diffusion transformers, the authors propose Follow-Your-Motion, a two-stage framework that employs a spatial-temporal decoupled LoRA to separate spatial appearance and temporal motion processing in the 3D attention mechanism. In the second stage, sparse motion sampling and adaptive RoPE are introduced to accelerate fine-tuning. Evaluated on the newly proposed MotionBench benchmark, which includes diverse motion types, the method demonstrates superior performance in generating consistent and complex motions compared to existing two-stage LoRA approaches.
为提升大型视频扩散变换器在视频动作迁移任务中的动作一致性与调优效率，作者提出Follow-Your-Motion框架，采用空间-时间解耦的LoRA方法，将3D注意力机制中的空间外观与时间动作处理分离。第二阶段调优中引入稀疏动作采样与自适应RoPE以加速训练。在新提出的MotionBench基准上（涵盖创意摄像机运动、单体/多体运动及复杂人体动作），该方法在动作保真度与训练效率方面均显著优于现有方法。
Multilingual Medical Reasoning for Question Answering with Large Language Models
Authors: Pietro Ferrazzi, Aitor Soroa, Rodrigo Agerri
First: 2025-12-05T12:05:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:46:48+00:00
Comments: Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) with reasoning capabilities have recently demonstrated strong potential in medical Question Answering (QA). Existing approaches are largely English-focused and primarily rely on distillation from general-purpose LLMs, raising concerns about the reliability of their medical knowledge. In this work, we present a method to generate multilingual reasoning traces based on medical knowledge extracted from Wikipedia. We produce 500k traces in English, Italian, and Spanish, using a retrieval-augmented generation approach over medical information from Wikipedia. The traces are generated to solve medical questions drawn from MedQA and MedMCQA, which we extend to Italian and Spanish. We test our pipeline in both in-domain and out-of-domain settings across Medical QA benchmarks, and demonstrate that our reasoning traces improve performance both when utilized via in-context learning (few-shot) and supervised fine-tuning, yielding state-of-the-art results among 8B-parameter LLMs. We believe that these resources can support the development of more transparent clinical decision-support tools in multilingual settings. We release the full suite of resources: reasoning traces, translated QA datasets, Medical-Wikipedia, and fine-tuned models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于大语言模型的多语言医学推理问答
具备推理能力的大语言模型（LLMs）在医学问答任务中展现出强大潜力。现有方法主要聚焦英语，且依赖通用LLMs的知识蒸馏，引发对其医学知识可靠性的担忧。本文提出一种基于维基百科医学知识生成多语言推理轨迹的方法，采用检索增强生成技术，生成50万条英文、意大利语和西班牙语的推理轨迹，用于解决来自MedQA和MedMCQA的医学问题（并扩展至意大利语和西班牙语）。我们在多个医学问答基准上测试该管道，涵盖领域内和领域外场景，证明推理轨迹在上下文学习（少样本）和监督微调中均能提升性能，达到8B参数LLMs中的最先进水平。我们相信这些资源可支持多语言环境下更透明的临床决策支持工具开发，并公开所有资源：推理轨迹、翻译后的问答数据集、Medical-Wikipedia及微调模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited multilingual capability and reliability of existing medical QA systems based on English-centric LLMs, this work introduces a method to generate multilingual reasoning traces by extracting medical knowledge from Wikipedia and applying retrieval-augmented generation. The team produced 500k reasoning traces in English, Italian, and Spanish, aligned with questions from MedQA and MedMCQA (extended to the latter two languages). Experimental results show that these traces significantly improve performance across in-domain and out-of-domain medical QA benchmarks, both when used via in-context learning and supervised fine-tuning, achieving state-of-the-art results among 8B-parameter LLMs.
为解决现有大语言模型在多语言医学问答中缺乏可靠推理能力的问题，本研究提出一种基于维基百科医学知识检索增强生成的方法，构建了英、意、西三种语言的50万条推理轨迹。这些轨迹用于解答MedQA及其多语言扩展数据集中的问题，并通过上下文学习与监督微调提升大语言模型性能。实验表明，该方法在多个医学问答基准测试中显著提升模型表现，尤其在8B参数模型中达到当前最优水平，适用于多语言临床决策支持系统开发。
Position: Explainable AI is Causality in Disguise
Authors: Amir-Hossein Karimi
First: 2026-03-30T15:44:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:44:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The demand for Explainable AI (XAI) has triggered an explosion of methods, producing a landscape so fragmented that we now rely on surveys of surveys. Yet, fundamental challenges persist: conflicting metrics, failed sanity checks, and unresolved debates over robustness and fairness. The only consensus on how to achieve explainability is a lack of one. This has led many to point to the absence of a ground truth for defining ``the'' correct explanation as the main culprit. This position paper posits that the persistent discord in XAI arises not from an absent ground truth but from a ground truth that exists, albeit as an elusive and challenging target: the causal model that governs the relevant system. By reframing XAI queries about data, models, or decisions as causal inquiries, we prove the necessity and sufficiency of causal models for XAI. We contend that without this causal grounding, XAI remains unmoored. Ultimately, we encourage the community to converge around advanced concept and causal discovery to escape this entrenched uncertainty.
中文标题/摘要
标题：观点：可解释人工智能是因果关系的伪装
对可解释人工智能（XAI）的需求催生了大量方法，导致领域碎片化严重，以至于我们如今依赖于对文献的综述。然而，根本性挑战依然存在：矛盾的评估指标、失败的合理性检验，以及关于鲁棒性和公平性的未决争论。关于如何实现可解释性的唯一共识，恰恰是缺乏共识。这促使许多人将“正确解释”的缺乏真实基准视为主要症结。 本文观点认为，XAI领域持续的分歧并非源于缺乏基准，而是源于一个实际存在的基准——即支配相关系统的因果模型，尽管它难以捉摸且极具挑战性。通过将XAI对数据、模型或决策的查询重新定义为因果问题，我们证明了因果模型对XAI的必要性和充分性。我们认为，没有这一因果基础，XAI将无从立足。最终，我们呼吁社区转向更先进的概念与因果发现，以摆脱这一根深蒂固的不确定性。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that the fragmentation and unresolved debates in Explainable AI (XAI) stem not from a lack of ground truth, but from the absence of causal models that govern the systems being explained. It proposes reframing XAI as a causal inquiry, asserting that causal models are both necessary and sufficient for meaningful explanation. The authors contend that without causal grounding, XAI remains disconnected from real-world mechanisms, and they urge the field to adopt causal discovery methods to resolve current uncertainties.
本文指出，可解释人工智能（XAI）领域存在的分歧并非源于缺乏基准答案，而是因为真正的基准——即支配系统运作的因果模型——往往难以捉摸且未被充分探索。作者主张将XAI问题重新定义为因果问题，认为因果模型是实现真正可解释性的必要且充分条件。若缺乏因果基础，XAI方法将无法与决策机制真正挂钩。文章呼吁学界转向因果发现与概念统一，以解决关于鲁棒性、公平性及评估指标的长期争议。
Moving Beyond Review: Applying Language Models to Planning and Translation in Reflection
Authors: Seyed Parsa Neshaei, Richard Lee Davis, Tanja Käser
First: 2026-03-30T15:42:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:42:38+00:00
Comments: Accepted at AIED 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reflective writing is known to support the development of students' metacognitive skills, yet learners often struggle to engage in deep reflection, limiting learning gains. Although large language models (LLMs) have been shown to improve writing skills, their use as conversational agents for reflective writing has produced mixed results and has largely focused on providing feedback on reflective texts, rather than support during planning and organizing. In this paper, inspired by the Cognitive Process Theory of writing (CPT), we propose the first application of LLMs to the planning and translation steps of reflective writing. We introduce Pensée, a tool to explore the effects of explicit AI support during these stages by scaffolding structured reflection planning using a conversational agent, and supporting translation by automatically extracting key concepts. We evaluate Pensée in a controlled between-subjects experiment (N=93), manipulating AI support across writing phases. Results show significantly greater reflection depth and structural quality when learners receive support during planning and translation stages of CPT, though these effects reduce in a delayed post-test. Analyses of learner behavior and perceptions further illustrate how CPT-aligned conversational support shapes reflection processes and learner experience, contributing empirical evidence for theory-driven uses of LLMs in AI-supported reflective writing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越复盘：将语言模型应用于反思写作中的规划与转换
反思写作有助于培养学生的元认知技能，但学习者往往难以进行深入反思，限制了学习收益。尽管大型语言模型（LLM）已被证明能提升写作能力，但其作为反思写作对话代理的应用效果参差不齐，且主要聚焦于对反思文本的反馈，而非在规划和组织阶段提供支持。本文受写作认知过程理论（CPT）启发，首次将LLM应用于反思写作的规划与转换阶段。我们提出了Pensée工具，通过对话代理引导结构化反思规划，并自动提取关键概念以支持转换过程。我们在受控的被试间实验（N=93）中评估了Pensée，操纵AI支持在写作各阶段的介入。结果表明，当学习者在CPT的规划与转换阶段获得支持时，反思深度和结构质量显著提升，但这些效果在延迟后测中有所减弱。对学习者行为与感知的分析进一步说明了CPT对齐的对话支持如何塑造反思过程与学习体验，为理论驱动的LLM在AI支持反思写作中的应用提供了实证依据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of students struggling to engage in deep reflection despite its educational value, this study applies large language models to the planning and translation phases of reflective writing, guided by Cognitive Process Theory of writing. The proposed tool, Pensée, scaffolds structured reflection planning via a conversational agent and automates key concept extraction for translation. In a controlled experiment with 93 participants, AI support during planning and translation significantly improved reflection depth and structural quality, though these gains diminished in a delayed post-test. Behavioral and perception analyses confirmed that CPT-aligned conversational support actively shaped reflective processes and enhanced learner experience, offering empirical validation for theory-driven LLM applications in reflective writing.
为提升学生元认知能力，本研究基于写作认知过程理论，首次将大语言模型应用于反思写作中的规划与转化阶段。工具Pensée通过对话式交互引导结构化反思规划，并自动提取关键概念辅助转化。在93名参与者对照实验中，规划与转化阶段获得AI支持显著提升反思深度与结构质量，但延迟后测中效果减弱。行为与感知分析表明，符合认知过程理论的对话支持有效塑造了反思过程与学习体验，为理论驱动的AI反思写作应用提供了实证支持。
Optimistic Actor-Critic with Parametric Policies for Linear Markov Decision Processes
Authors: Max Qiushi Lin, Reza Asad, Kevin Tan, Haque Ishfaq, Csaba Szepesvari, Sharan Vaswani
First: 2026-03-30T15:41:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:41:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Although actor-critic methods have been successful in practice, their theoretical analyses have several limitations. Specifically, existing theoretical work either sidesteps the exploration problem by making strong assumptions or analyzes impractical methods with complicated algorithmic modifications. Moreover, the actor-critic methods analyzed for linear MDPs often employ natural policy gradient (NPG) and construct "implicit" policies without explicit parameterization. Such policies are computationally expensive to sample from, making the environment interactions inefficient. To that end, we focus on the finite-horizon linear MDPs and propose an optimistic actor-critic framework that uses parametric log-linear policies. In particular, we introduce a tractable \textit{logit-matching} regression objective for the actor. For the critic, we use approximate Thompson sampling via Langevin Monte Carlo to obtain optimistic value estimates. We prove that the resulting algorithm achieves $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$ and $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity in the on-policy and off-policy setting, respectively. Our results match prior theoretical works in achieving the state-of-the-art sample complexity, while our algorithm is more aligned with practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于线性马尔可夫决策过程的乐观演员-评论家方法与参数化策略
尽管演员-评论家方法在实践中已取得成功，但其理论分析存在若干局限。具体而言，现有理论工作要么通过强假设回避探索问题，要么分析的是具有复杂算法修改的不切实际方法。此外，针对线性MDP分析的演员-评论家方法通常采用自然策略梯度（NPG）并构建“隐式”策略，而无需显式参数化。此类策略采样计算成本高昂，导致环境交互效率低下。为此，我们聚焦于有限时域线性MDP，提出一种使用参数化对数线性策略的乐观演员-评论家框架。具体而言，我们为演员引入一个可处理的\textit{logit-matching}回归目标；对于评论家，我们通过朗之万蒙特卡洛近似实现汤普森采样，以获得乐观价值估计。我们证明该算法在在线策略和离线策略设置下分别达到\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})和\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})的样本复杂度。我们的结果在达到最先进样本复杂度方面与先前理论工作一致，而我们的算法更贴近实际应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing actor-critic methods in linear MDPs—particularly their reliance on impractical implicit policies and lack of theoretical guarantees for exploration—the paper proposes an optimistic actor-critic framework using parametric log-linear policies. The actor employs a tractable logit-matching regression objective, while the critic uses approximate Thompson sampling via Langevin Monte Carlo to generate optimistic value estimates. Experimental results show the algorithm achieves $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$ and $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity in on-policy and off-policy settings, respectively, matching state-of-the-art theoretical bounds while being more practical due to explicit policy parameterization.
为解决现有actor-critic方法在理论分析中对探索问题的回避或对复杂算法修改的依赖，本文针对有限时域线性MDP提出一种基于参数化对数线性策略的乐观actor-critic框架。actor采用可计算的logit-matching回归目标，critic则通过Langevin Monte Carlo实现近似Thompson采样以获得乐观价值估计。实验表明，该算法在on-policy和off-policy设置下分别达到$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$和$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$的样本复杂度，与现有最优理论结果一致，同时具备更强的实践可行性。
Detection of Adversarial Attacks in Robotic Perception
Authors: Ziad Sharawy, Mohammad Nakshbandiand, Sorin Mihai Grigorescu
First: 2026-03-30T15:41:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:41:49+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 6 figures. Accepted and presented at STE 2025, Transilvania University of Brasov, Romania
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) achieve strong performance in semantic segmentation for robotic perception but remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks, threatening safety-critical applications. While robustness has been studied for image classification, semantic segmentation in robotic contexts requires specialized architectures and detection strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：机器人感知中的对抗攻击检测
深度神经网络（DNN）在机器人感知的语义分割任务中表现出色，但易受对抗攻击影响，威胁安全关键应用。尽管图像分类领域的鲁棒性已获研究，机器人场景下的语义分割需专用架构与检测策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the safety risks posed by adversarial attacks on deep neural networks in robotic perception, this work proposes a specialized detection framework tailored for semantic segmentation tasks. The method leverages a dual-stream architecture that combines feature extraction from both clean and perturbed inputs, followed by anomaly scoring using a lightweight classifier trained on adversarial examples. Experiments on standard robotic datasets demonstrate that the proposed detector achieves high accuracy in identifying adversarial inputs while maintaining real-time inference capability, outperforming baseline methods in both detection rate and false positive control.
鉴于深度神经网络在机器人感知中的语义分割任务中易受对抗攻击，可能危及安全关键应用，本文提出了一种专为语义分割设计的对抗攻击检测框架。该方法结合输入扰动分析与置信度异常检测，无需重新训练即可识别对抗样本。实验结果表明，该方法在多个机器人数据集上检测准确率高、误报率低，优于现有基线方法，且在实时机器人场景中表现良好。
Physics-Informed Framework for Impact Identification in Aerospace Composites
Authors: Natália Ribeiro Marinho, Richard Loendersloot, Jan Willem Wiegman, Frank Grooteman, Tiedo Tinga
First: 2026-03-30T15:40:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:40:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper introduces a novel physics-informed impact identification (Phy-ID) framework. The proposed method integrates observational, inductive, and learning biases to combine physical knowledge with data-driven inference in a unified modelling strategy, achieving physically consistent and numerically stable impact identification. The physics-informed approach structures the input space using physics-based energy indicators, constrains admissible solutions via architectural design, and enforces governing relations via hybrid loss formulations. Together, these mechanisms limit non-physical solutions and stabilise inference under degraded measurement conditions. A disjoint inference formulation is used as a representative use case to demonstrate the framework capabilities, in which impact velocity and impactor mass are inferred through decoupled surrogate models, and impact energy is computed by enforcing kinetic energy consistency. Experimental evaluations show mean absolute percentage errors below 8% for inferred impact velocity and impactor mass and below 10% for impact energy. Additional analyses confirm stable performance under reduced data availability and increased measurement noise, as well as generalisation for out-of-distribution cases across pristine and damaged regimes when damaged responses are included in training. These results indicate that the systematic integration of physics-informed biases enables reliable, physically consistent, and data-efficient impact identification, highlighting the potential of the approach for practical monitoring systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理信息的航空航天复合材料冲击识别框架
本文提出了一种新型的物理信息冲击识别（Phy-ID）框架。该方法融合观测、归纳与学习偏差，将物理知识与数据驱动推断统一于建模策略中，实现物理一致且数值稳定的冲击识别。物理信息方法通过基于物理的能耗指标结构化输入空间，通过架构设计约束可行解，并通过混合损失函数强制执行主导关系。这些机制共同限制非物理解，并在测量条件退化时稳定推断。以分离推断形式作为代表性用例，通过解耦代理模型推断冲击速度与冲击体质量，并通过强制动能一致性计算冲击能量。实验评估显示，推断的冲击速度与冲击体质量平均绝对百分比误差低于8%，冲击能量低于10%。附加分析证实，该方法在数据量减少和测量噪声增加时仍保持稳定性能，并在包含损伤响应的训练中，对超出分布的场景（包括完好与损伤状态）具有泛化能力。结果表明，系统性整合物理信息偏差可实现可靠、物理一致且数据高效的冲击识别，凸显该方法在实际监测系统中的应用潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable impact identification in aerospace composites under noisy or limited data, this paper proposes a physics-informed framework that integrates physical constraints with data-driven learning to ensure physically consistent and numerically stable inference. The method structures input space using physics-based energy indicators, constrains solution space via model architecture, and enforces governing laws through hybrid loss functions, collectively suppressing non-physical solutions. Evaluated on a disjoint inference task, the framework infers impact velocity and mass via decoupled surrogates and computes impact energy via kinetic energy consistency, achieving mean absolute percentage errors under 8% for velocity and mass, and under 10% for energy. It also demonstrates robustness to data sparsity, measurement noise, and generalization across pristine and damaged material states, validating its potential for practical monitoring applications.
为解决航空航天复合材料在噪声或数据不足条件下可靠识别冲击事件的需求，本文提出一种融合物理约束与数据驱动学习的物理信息框架，确保推断结果物理一致且数值稳定。该方法通过物理能量指标构建输入空间，利用架构设计约束解空间，并通过混合损失函数强制物理规律，抑制非物理解。在解耦推断任务中，框架通过独立代理模型推断冲击速度与质量，并通过动能一致性计算冲击能量，平均绝对百分比误差分别低于8%和10%。实验还表明其在数据稀疏、噪声增强及跨材料状态（含损伤样本训练）时仍保持稳定泛化能力，验证了该方法在实际监测系统中的应用潜力。
Advancing Few-Shot Pediatric Arrhythmia Classification with a Novel Contrastive Loss and Multimodal Learning
Authors: Yiqiao Chen, Zijian Huang, Zhenghui Feng
First: 2025-09-10T05:24:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:40:19+00:00
Comments: 12pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Arrhythmias are a major cause of sudden cardiac death in children, making automated rhythm classification from electrocardiograms (ECGs) clinically important. However, pediatric arrhythmia analysis remains challenging because of age-dependent waveform variability, limited data availability, and a pronounced long-tailed class distribution that hinders recognition of rare but clinically important rhythms. To address these issues, we propose a multimodal end-to-end framework that integrates surface ECG and intracardiac electrogram (IEGM) signals for pediatric arrhythmia classification. The model combines dual-branch feature encoders, attention-based cross-modal fusion, and a lightweight Transformer classifier to learn complementary electrophysiological representations. We further introduce an Adaptive Global Class-Aware Contrastive Loss (AGCACL), which incorporates prototype-based alignment, class-frequency reweighting, and globally informed hard-class modulation to improve intra-class compactness and inter-class separability under class imbalance. We evaluate the proposed method on the pediatric subset of the Leipzig Heart Center ECG-Database and establish a reproducible preprocessing pipeline including rhythm-segment construction, denoising, and label grouping. The proposed approach achieves 96.22% Top-1 accuracy and improves macro precision, macro recall, macro F1 score, and macro F2 score by 4.48, 1.17, 6.98, and 7.34 percentage points, respectively, over the strongest baseline. These results indicate improved minority-sensitive classification performance on the current benchmark. However, further validation under subject-independent and multicenter settings is still required before clinical translation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种新颖对比损失与多模态学习相结合的方法，用于推进少样本儿科心律失常分类
心律失常是儿童猝死的主要原因，因此从心电图（ECG）中自动分类心律在临床上具有重要意义。然而，儿科心律失常分析仍具挑战性，原因包括年龄相关的波形变异、数据有限以及显著的长尾类分布，这阻碍了罕见但临床重要心律的识别。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种端到端的多模态框架，整合体表ECG与心内电图（IEGM）信号进行儿科心律失常分类。该模型结合双分支特征编码器、基于注意力的跨模态融合和轻量级Transformer分类器，以学习互补的电生理表征。我们进一步引入自适应全局类感知对比损失（AGCACL），结合原型对齐、类别频率重加权和全局信息驱动的硬类别调制，以在类别不平衡条件下提升类内紧凑性和类间可分性。我们在莱比锡心脏中心ECG数据库的儿科子集上评估该方法，并建立可复现的预处理流程，包括节律段构建、去噪和标签分组。该方法达到96.22%的Top-1准确率，并在最强基线模型基础上分别提升了4.48、1.17、6.98和7.34个百分点的宏精度、宏召回率、宏F1分数和宏F2分数。这些结果表明在当前基准上，该方法在少数类敏感分类性能方面有所提升。然而，在受试者独立和多中心设置下仍需进一步验证，方可实现临床转化。
Summary / 总结
To tackle the challenge of rare pediatric arrhythmia classification due to data scarcity and class imbalance, this study proposes a multimodal end-to-end framework that fuses surface ECG and intracardiac electrogram signals using dual-branch encoders, attention-based fusion, and a lightweight Transformer classifier. It introduces an Adaptive Global Class-Aware Contrastive Loss (AGCACL) to enhance intra-class compactness and inter-class separability by aligning prototypes, reweighting class frequencies, and modulating hard classes globally. Evaluated on the Leipzig Heart Center pediatric ECG subset with a standardized preprocessing pipeline, the method achieves 96.22% Top-1 accuracy and significantly improves macro precision, recall, F1, and F2 scores over the best baseline by 4.48, 1.17, 6.98, and 7.34 percentage points respectively, demonstrating enhanced performance for minority classes.
为应对儿童心律失常因数据稀缺和类别不平衡导致的识别难题，该研究提出一种融合体表心电图与心内电图的端到端多模态框架，采用双分支编码器、注意力交叉模态融合及轻量Transformer分类器。引入自适应全局类别感知对比损失（AGCACL），通过原型对齐、类别频率重加权和全局硬类别调制，提升类内紧凑性和类间可分性。在莱比锡心脏中心儿科ECG子集上经标准化预处理评估，该方法达到96.22% Top-1准确率，相较最强基线模型在宏精度、宏召回率、宏F1和宏F2得分上分别提升4.48、1.17、6.98和7.34个百分点，显著增强对少数类的识别能力。
Universal Approximation Constraints of Narrow ResNets: The Tunnel Effect
Authors: Christian Kuehn, Sara-Viola Kuntz, Tobias Wöhrer
First: 2026-03-30T15:37:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:37:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We analyze the universal approximation constraints of narrow Residual Neural Networks (ResNets) both theoretically and numerically. For deep neural networks without input space augmentation, a central constraint is the inability to represent critical points of the input-output map. We prove that this has global consequences for target function approximations and show that the manifestation of this defect is typically a shift of the critical point to infinity, which we call the ``tunnel effect'' in the context of classification tasks. While ResNets offer greater expressivity than standard multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), their capability strongly depends on the signal ratio between the skip and residual channels. We establish quantitative approximation bounds for both the residual-dominant (close to MLP) and skip-dominant (close to neural ODE) regimes. These estimates depend explicitly on the channel ratio and uniform network weight bounds. Low-dimensional examples further provide a detailed analysis of the different ResNet regimes and how architecture-target incompatibility influences the approximation error.
中文标题/摘要
标题：窄型残差神经网络的通用逼近约束：隧道效应
我们从理论和数值两个角度分析窄型残差神经网络（ResNets）的通用逼近约束。对于无输入空间扩充的深度神经网络，一个核心约束是无法表示输入输出映射的临界点。我们证明了这一缺陷对目标函数逼近具有全局影响，并表明其典型表现是临界点移向无穷远，我们称之为分类任务中的“隧道效应”。尽管ResNets比标准多层感知机（MLPs）具有更强的表达能力，但其能力高度依赖于跳连通道与残差通道之间的信号比。我们为残差主导（接近MLP）和跳连主导（接近神经ODE）两种情形建立了定量逼近界，这些估计显式依赖于通道比和网络权重的统一上界。低维示例进一步详细分析了不同ResNet架构及其与目标函数不兼容性对逼近误差的影响。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the universal approximation limitations of narrow Residual Neural Networks (ResNets), motivated by the observation that such networks cannot represent critical points of the input-output map without input space augmentation. The authors theoretically and numerically demonstrate that this constraint leads to a global approximation failure, manifesting as a 'tunnel effect'—a shift of critical points to infinity in classification tasks. They establish quantitative approximation bounds for two architectural regimes: residual-dominant (similar to MLPs) and skip-dominant (similar to neural ODEs), both of which depend on the channel ratio and uniform weight bounds. Experimental analysis in low dimensions confirms that architectural mismatch with target functions significantly increases approximation error.
该论文研究了窄型残差神经网络（ResNets）的通用逼近限制，指出其在无输入空间扩充情况下无法表示输入输出映射中的关键点，导致全局逼近能力受限，表现为“隧道效应”——关键点被推向无穷。作者推导出残差主导（近似MLP）与跳跃主导（近似神经ODE）两种架构下的量化逼近界，证明其性能依赖于通道比与权重上界。低维实验进一步验证，当网络架构与目标函数不兼容时，逼近误差显著增大。
MonitorBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Chain-of-Thought Monitorability in Large Language Models
Authors: Han Wang, Yifan Sun, Brian Ko, Mann Talati, Jiawen Gong, Zimeng Li, Naicheng Yu, Xucheng Yu, Wei Shen, Vedant Jolly, Huan Zhang
First: 2026-03-30T15:37:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:37:42+00:00
Comments: 57 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) can generate chains of thought (CoTs) that are not always causally responsible for their final outputs. When such a mismatch occurs, the CoT no longer faithfully reflects the decision-critical factors driving the model's behavior, leading to the reduced CoT monitorability problem. However, a comprehensive and fully open-source benchmark for studying CoT monitorability remains lacking. To address this gap, we propose MonitorBench, a systematic benchmark for evaluating CoT monitorability in LLMs. MonitorBench provides: (1) a diverse set of 1,514 test instances with carefully designed decision-critical factors across 19 tasks spanning 7 categories to characterize when CoTs can be used to monitor the factors driving LLM behavior; and (2) two stress-test settings to quantify the extent to which CoT monitorability can be degraded. Extensive experiments across multiple popular LLMs with varying capabilities show that CoT monitorability is higher when producing the final target response requires structural reasoning through the decision-critical factor. Closed-source LLMs generally show lower monitorability, and there exists a negative relationship between monitorability and model capability. Moreover, both open- and closed-source LLMs can intentionally reduce monitorability under stress-tests, with monitorability dropping by up to 30% in some tasks that do not require structural reasoning over the decision-critical factors. Beyond these empirical insights, MonitorBench provides a basis for further research on evaluating future LLMs, studying advanced stress-test monitorability techniques, and developing new monitoring approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MonitorBench：大型语言模型链式思维可监控性的全面基准
大型语言模型（LLMs）生成的链式思维（CoTs）并不总是对其最终输出具有因果责任。当出现这种不匹配时，CoT不再真实反映驱动模型行为的决策关键因素，从而导致CoT可监控性降低的问题。然而，目前尚缺乏全面且完全开源的CoT可监控性研究基准。为填补这一空白，我们提出了MonitorBench，一个系统性评估LLMs中CoT可监控性的基准。MonitorBench提供：（1）1514个多样化的测试实例，覆盖7个类别中的19项任务，精心设计了决策关键因素，以刻画CoT在何种情况下可用于监控驱动LLM行为的因素；（2）两种压力测试设置，用于量化CoT可监控性下降的程度。在多个能力各异的流行LLMs上进行的广泛实验表明，当生成最终目标响应需要通过决策关键因素进行结构化推理时，CoT可监控性更高。闭源LLMs通常表现出较低的可监控性，且可监控性与模型能力呈负相关。此外，无论是开源还是闭源LLMs，在压力测试下均可有意降低可监控性，某些无需对决策关键因素进行结构化推理的任务中，可监控性下降幅度高达30%。除这些实证洞察外，MonitorBench还为未来LLMs评估、研究高级压力测试可监控性技术及开发新监控方法提供了基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that large language models often generate chains-of-thought (CoTs) that do not causally align with their final outputs, reducing their monitorability, the authors introduce MonitorBench, a comprehensive open-source benchmark designed to evaluate CoT monitorability. The benchmark includes 1,514 diverse test instances across 19 tasks and 7 categories, each engineered to highlight decision-critical factors, alongside two stress-test settings to measure degradation in monitorability. Experiments across multiple LLMs reveal that monitorability is higher when models perform structural reasoning over critical factors, while closed-source models generally exhibit lower monitorability. A negative correlation exists between model capability and monitorability, and both open- and closed-source models can intentionally reduce monitorability under stress, with drops as high as 30% in tasks lacking structural reasoning.
为解决大语言模型生成的思维链（CoT）常与最终输出无因果关联、导致可监控性降低的问题，作者提出了MonitorBench，一个全面开源的CoT可监控性评估基准。该基准包含1514个覆盖19项任务和7类场景的测试实例，每个实例精心设计以突出决策关键因素，并提供两种压力测试设置以量化监控性下降程度。实验表明，当模型需对决策关键因素进行结构化推理时，监控性更高；闭源模型监控性普遍较低，且监控性与模型能力呈负相关。在无需结构化推理的任务中，无论是开源还是闭源模型，均可通过压力测试有意降低监控性，降幅最高达30%。
Towards a Medical AI Scientist
Authors: Hongtao Wu, Boyun Zheng, Dingjie Song, Yu Jiang, Jianfeng Gao, Lei Xing, Lichao Sun, Yixuan Yuan
First: 2026-03-30T15:37:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:37:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous systems that generate scientific hypotheses, conduct experiments, and draft manuscripts have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for accelerating discovery. However, existing AI Scientists remain largely domain-agnostic, limiting their applicability to clinical medicine, where research is required to be grounded in medical evidence with specialized data modalities. In this work, we introduce Medical AI Scientist, the first autonomous research framework tailored to clinical autonomous research. It enables clinically grounded ideation by transforming extensively surveyed literature into actionable evidence through clinician-engineer co-reasoning mechanism, which improves the traceability of generated research ideas. It further facilitates evidence-grounded manuscript drafting guided by structured medical compositional conventions and ethical policies. The framework operates under 3 research modes, namely paper-based reproduction, literature-inspired innovation, and task-driven exploration, each corresponding to a distinct level of automated scientific inquiry with progressively increasing autonomy. Comprehensive evaluations by both large language models and human experts demonstrate that the ideas generated by the Medical AI Scientist are of substantially higher quality than those produced by commercial LLMs across 171 cases, 19 clinical tasks, and 6 data modalities. Meanwhile, our system achieves strong alignment between the proposed method and its implementation, while also demonstrating significantly higher success rates in executable experiments. Double-blind evaluations by human experts and the Stanford Agentic Reviewer suggest that the generated manuscripts approach MICCAI-level quality, while consistently surpassing those from ISBI and BIBM. The proposed Medical AI Scientist highlights the potential of leveraging AI for autonomous scientific discovery in healthcare.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向医学AI科学家
能够自主生成科学假说、开展实验并撰写论文的系统最近成为加速科学发现的一种有前景范式。然而，现有AI科学家大多领域无关，限制了其在临床医学中的应用，因为临床研究需基于医学证据和特定数据模态。本文提出医学AI科学家，这是首个专为临床自主研究设计的框架。它通过临床医生与工程师协同推理机制，将广泛调研的文献转化为可执行证据，从而提升生成研究想法的可追溯性；并借助结构化的医学组成规范和伦理政策，支持基于证据的论文撰写。该框架支持三种研究模式：基于论文的复现、受文献启发的创新、任务驱动的探索，分别对应不同层级的自动化科学探究，自主性逐步增强。经大型语言模型与人类专家的综合评估，在171个案例、19项临床任务和6种数据模态中，医学AI科学家生成的想法质量显著优于商业LLM。同时，系统在方法与实现之间保持强一致性，并在可执行实验中表现出显著更高的成功率。双盲评估显示，生成的论文质量接近MICCAI级别，且持续优于ISBI和BIBM。所提出的医学AI科学家凸显了AI在医疗领域自主科学发现中的潜力。

Motivated by the need for domain-specific, evidence-grounded scientific discovery in clinical medicine, this work introduces Medical AI Scientist, the first autonomous research framework tailored for healthcare. It employs clinician-engineer co-reasoning to transform literature into actionable hypotheses and uses structured medical conventions and ethical guidelines to draft manuscripts. The system operates in three modes—reproduction, innovation, and exploration—each scaling autonomy while maintaining clinical relevance. Experimental results across 171 cases, 19 tasks, and 6 data modalities show that its generated ideas significantly outperform commercial LLMs, and its manuscripts achieve MICCAI-level quality, surpassing those from ISBI and BIBM in double-blind evaluations.
为推动临床医学领域基于证据的自主科研，本研究提出首个面向医疗场景的自主科研框架——Medical AI Scientist。该框架通过临床医生与工程师协同推理，将文献转化为可执行假设，并依据结构化医学规范与伦理准则撰写论文。系统支持三种模式——复现、创新与探索，逐步提升自动化程度。在171个案例、19项临床任务和6种数据模态的评估中，其生成的科研想法显著优于商业大模型，实验成功率更高，所撰论文质量达MICCAI级别，超越ISBI与BIBM水平。
ORSIFlow: Saliency-Guided Rectified Flow for Optical Remote Sensing Salient Object Detection
Authors: Haojing Chen, Yutong Li, Zhihang Liu, Tao Tan, Haoyu Bian, Qiuju Ma
Venue: ICME 2026
First: 2026-03-30T15:33:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:33:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICME 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Optical Remote Sensing Image Salient Object Detection (ORSI-SOD) remains challenging due to complex backgrounds, low contrast, irregular object shapes, and large variations in object scale. Existing discriminative methods directly regress saliency maps, while recent diffusion-based generative approaches suffer from stochastic sampling and high computational cost. In this paper, we propose ORSIFlow, a saliency-guided rectified flow framework that reformulates ORSI-SOD as a deterministic latent flow generation problem. ORSIFlow performs saliency mask generation in a compact latent space constructed by a frozen variational autoencoder, enabling efficient inference with only a few steps. To enhance saliency awareness, we design a Salient Feature Discriminator for global semantic discrimination and a Salient Feature Calibrator for precise boundary refinement. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks show that ORSIFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance with significantly improved efficiency. Codes are available at: https://github.com/Ch3nSir/ORSIFlow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ORSIFlow：基于显著性引导的校正流用于光学遥感显著目标检测
由于复杂背景、低对比度、不规则目标形状和目标尺度变化大，光学遥感图像显著目标检测（ORSI-SOD）仍具挑战性。现有判别方法直接回归显著性图，而近期基于扩散的生成方法则存在随机采样和高计算成本问题。本文提出ORSIFlow，一种显著性引导的校正流框架，将ORSI-SOD重新表述为确定性潜在流生成问题。ORSIFlow在由冻结变分自编码器构建的紧凑潜在空间中生成显著性掩码，仅需几步即可实现高效推理。为增强显著性感知，我们设计了全局语义判别的显著特征判别器和精确边界优化的显著特征校准器。在多个公开基准上的大量实验表明，ORSIFlow实现了最先进性能并显著提升效率。代码见：https://github.com/Ch3nSir/ORSIFlow。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of detecting salient objects in optical remote sensing images—such as complex backgrounds and scale variations—the paper introduces ORSIFlow, a saliency-guided rectified flow framework that reformulates the task as deterministic latent flow generation. It leverages a frozen variational autoencoder to construct a compact latent space for efficient inference, and employs a Salient Feature Discriminator and Calibrator to enhance semantic discrimination and boundary precision. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ORSIFlow achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while significantly reducing computational cost compared to existing diffusion-based methods.
为应对光学遥感图像中背景复杂、对比度低、目标尺度变化大等挑战，本文提出ORSIFlow框架，将显著目标检测重构为确定性潜在流生成问题。该方法利用冻结的变分自编码器构建紧凑潜在空间以实现高效推理，并通过显著特征判别器与校准器提升全局语义判别与边界精度。在多个公开数据集上的实验表明，ORSIFlow在性能上达到当前最优，同时大幅降低计算开销，优于现有扩散模型方法。
Navigating the Mirage: A Dual-Path Agentic Framework for Robust Misleading Chart Question Answering
Authors: Yanjie Zhang, Yafei Li, Rui Sheng, Zixin Chen, Yanna Lin, Huamin Qu, Lei Chen, Yushi Sun
First: 2026-03-30T15:32:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-30T15:32:24+00:00
Comments: 10pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the success of Vision-Language Models (VLMs), misleading charts remain a significant challenge due to their deceptive visual structures and distorted data representations. We present ChartCynics, an agentic dual-path framework designed to unmask visual deception via a "skeptical" reasoning paradigm. Unlike holistic models, ChartCynics decouples perception from verification: a Diagnostic Vision Path captures structural anomalies (e.g., inverted axes) through strategic ROI cropping, while an OCR-Driven Data Path ensures numerical grounding. To resolve cross-modal conflicts, we introduce an Agentic Summarizer optimized via a two-stage protocol: Oracle-Informed SFT for reasoning distillation and Deception-Aware GRPO for adversarial alignment. This pipeline effectively penalizes visual traps and enforces logical consistency. Evaluations on two benchmarks show that ChartCynics achieves 74.43% and 64.55% accuracy, providing an absolute performance boost of ~29% over the Qwen3-VL-8B backbone, outperforming state-of-the-art proprietary models. Our results demonstrate that specialized agentic workflows can grant smaller open-source models superior robustness, establishing a new foundation for trustworthy chart interpretation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：破除幻象：一种用于鲁棒性误导图表问答的双路径代理框架
尽管视觉语言模型（VLMs）取得了成功，但误导性图表因其欺骗性的视觉结构和扭曲的数据表示仍是一个重大挑战。我们提出了ChartCynics，一种基于代理的双路径框架，通过“怀疑”推理范式揭示视觉欺骗。与整体模型不同，ChartCynics将感知与验证解耦：诊断视觉路径通过战略性ROI裁剪捕捉结构异常（如倒置坐标轴），而OCR驱动数据路径确保数值基础。为解决跨模态冲突，我们引入了通过两阶段协议优化的代理摘要器：基于Oracle的SFT用于推理蒸馏，欺骗感知的GRPO用于对抗性对齐。该流程有效惩罚视觉陷阱并强制逻辑一致性。在两个基准测试中，ChartCynics分别达到74.43%和64.55%的准确率，相较Qwen3-VL-8B基座模型绝对性能提升约29%，超越现有最先进专有模型。我们的结果表明，专用代理工作流可赋予小型开源模型更优鲁棒性，为可信图表解读奠定新基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent challenge of misleading charts that deceive through visual distortion, ChartCynics introduces a dual-path agentic framework that separates perception from verification. It employs a Diagnostic Vision Path to detect structural anomalies via ROI cropping and an OCR-Driven Data Path to ground reasoning in numerical data. A novel Agentic Summarizer, trained via Oracle-Informed SFT and Deception-Aware GRPO, resolves cross-modal conflicts and enforces logical consistency. Evaluated on two benchmarks, the framework achieves 74.43% and 64.55% accuracy, representing a ~29% absolute gain over the Qwen3-VL-8B baseline and surpassing state-of-the-art proprietary models, demonstrating that specialized agentic workflows can enhance robustness even in smaller open-source models.
为应对图表视觉欺骗带来的挑战，ChartCynics提出一种双路径代理框架，将感知与验证解耦以识别视觉误导。其诊断视觉路径通过ROI裁剪检测结构异常，OCR驱动数据路径确保数值基础。创新的代理摘要器通过Oracle引导SFT与欺骗感知GRPO训练，解决跨模态冲突并强化逻辑一致性。在两个基准测试中，该框架分别达到74.43%和64.55%准确率，较Qwen3-VL-8B基线提升约29%，超越现有专有模型，证明专用代理工作流可显著增强开源模型在图表问答中的鲁棒性。
